- [ ] **Step 3: Write `evm_call.rs`**

```rust
//! Turning a form a Solidity developer fills in (function, arguments, caller, initial storage)
//! into the guest's input vector, including the storage witnesses the developer never sees.
use crate::views::{DiscoveryStep, EvmCallForm, StorageSeed};
use evm_core::abi::{run_call_with, Workspace};
use evm_core::interp::{Halt, Outcome};
use evm_core::u256::U256;
use evm_core::Host;
use rand_zkvm::evm::{mapping_slot, mapping_slot2, selector, slot_index, EvmCall, HostRef, SparseTree, ALICE, BOB, TOKEN};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const MAX_WITNESSES: usize = 16;
pub const MAX_RUNS: usize = 32;
const CONSTANT_SLOTS: u32 = 16;

pub fn u256_hex(v: &U256) -> String { format!("0x{}", hex::encode(v.to_be_bytes())) }

pub fn parse_u256(s: &str) -> Result<U256, String> {
    let s = s.trim();
    if s.is_empty() { return Err("empty number".into()); }
    if let Some(h) = s.strip_prefix("0x").or_else(|| s.strip_prefix("0X")) {
        let h = h.trim_start_matches('0');
        if h.len() > 64 { return Err(format!("{s} is wider than 256 bits")); }
        let padded = format!("{h:0>64}");
        let bytes: [u8; 32] = hex::decode(&padded).map_err(|e| format!("{s}: {e}"))?.try_into().unwrap();
        return Ok(U256::from_be_bytes(&bytes));
    }
    // Big-endian bytes × 10 + digit, with the carry out of byte 0 meaning overflow.
    let mut be = [0u8; 32];
    for ch in s.chars() {
        let d = ch.to_digit(10).ok_or_else(|| format!("{s} is not a number"))?;
        let mut carry = d;
        for b in be.iter_mut().rev() {
            let x = *b as u32 * 10 + carry;
            *b = x as u8;
            carry = x >> 8;
        }
        if carry != 0 { return Err(format!("{s} does not fit in 256 bits")); }
    }
    Ok(U256::from_be_bytes(&be))
}

fn split_params(sig: &str) -> Result<(&str, Vec<String>), String> {
    let open = sig.find('(').ok_or_else(|| format!("{sig}: not a signature"))?;
    let inner = sig[open + 1..].strip_suffix(')').ok_or_else(|| format!("{sig}: not a signature"))?;
    if inner.contains('(') { return Err(format!("{sig}: tuple parameters are not supported")); }
    let params = if inner.is_empty() { vec![] } else { inner.split(',').map(|p| p.trim().to_string()).collect() };
    Ok((&sig[..open], params))
}

fn encode_arg(ty: &str, arg: &str) -> Result<[u8; 32], String> {
    let arg = arg.trim();
    if ty.ends_with(']') || ty == "string" || ty == "bytes" {
        return Err(format!("{ty}: dynamic and array types are not supported by the terminal's encoder"));
    }
    if ty == "bool" {
        return match arg { "true" | "1" => Ok(U256::ONE.to_be_bytes()), "false" | "0" => Ok([0; 32]), _ => Err(format!("bool: {arg}")) };
    }
    if ty == "address" {
        let h = arg.strip_prefix("0x").ok_or_else(|| format!("address must be 0x-hex: {arg}"))?;
        if h.len() > 40 { return Err(format!("address wider than 20 bytes: {arg}")); }
        return Ok(parse_u256(arg)?.to_be_bytes());
    }
    if let Some(n) = ty.strip_prefix("bytes") {
        let n: usize = n.parse().map_err(|_| format!("{ty}: not supported by the terminal's encoder"))?;
        if !(1..=32).contains(&n) { return Err(format!("{ty}: not supported")); }
        let b = hex::decode(arg.trim_start_matches("0x")).map_err(|e| format!("{ty}: {e}"))?;
        if b.len() > n { return Err(format!("{ty}: {} bytes given", b.len())); }
        let mut out = [0u8; 32];
        out[..b.len()].copy_from_slice(&b);
        return Ok(out);
    }
    let (signed, bits) = if let Some(b) = ty.strip_prefix("uint") { (false, b) } else if let Some(b) = ty.strip_prefix("int") { (true, b) } else {
        return Err(format!("{ty}: not supported by the terminal's encoder"));
    };
    let bits: usize = if bits.is_empty() { 256 } else { bits.parse().map_err(|_| format!("{ty}: not supported"))? };
    if bits == 0 || bits > 256 || bits % 8 != 0 { return Err(format!("{ty}: not supported")); }
    let (neg, mag) = match arg.strip_prefix('-') { Some(m) if signed => (true, m), Some(_) => return Err(format!("{ty} cannot be negative")), None => (false, arg) };
    let be = parse_u256(mag)?.to_be_bytes();
    // |v| < 2^limit, or ≤ 2^limit for a negative signed value. Big-endian byte arrays compare
    // lexicographically in numeric order.
    let limit = if signed { bits - 1 } else { bits };
    if limit < 256 {
        let mut bound = [0u8; 32];
        bound[31 - limit / 8] = 1 << (limit % 8);
        let ok = if neg { be <= bound } else { be < bound };
        if !ok { return Err(format!("{arg} does not fit in {ty}")); }
    }
    Ok(if neg { neg_be(be) } else { be })
}

/// Two's complement of a big-endian 256-bit value.
fn neg_be(mut b: [u8; 32]) -> [u8; 32] {
    for x in b.iter_mut() { *x = !*x; }
    for x in b.iter_mut().rev() { let (s, c) = x.overflowing_add(1); *x = s; if !c { break; } }
    b
}

pub fn encode_calldata(sig: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let (_, params) = split_params(sig)?;
    if params.len() != args.len() { return Err(format!("{sig} expects {} argument(s), got {}", params.len(), args.len())); }
    let canonical = format!("{}({})", &sig[..sig.find('(').unwrap()], params.join(","));
    let mut out = selector(&canonical).to_vec();
    for (ty, a) in params.iter().zip(args) { out.extend_from_slice(&encode_arg(ty, a)?); }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Delegates to `HostRef` and records the 32-byte hash of every 64-byte Keccak message.
struct TraceHost { mapping_hashes: Vec<U256> }

impl Host for TraceHost {
    fn keccak_f(&mut self, state: &mut [u32; 50]) {
        let byte = |s: &[u32; 50], k: usize| s[k / 4].to_le_bytes()[k % 4];
        let is_64 = byte(state, 64) == 0x01 && byte(state, 135) & 0x80 != 0 && (65..135).all(|k| byte(state, k) == 0);
        HostRef.keccak_f(state);
        if is_64 {
            let mut h = [0u8; 32];
            for (k, b) in h.iter_mut().enumerate() { *b = byte(state, k); }
            self.mapping_hashes.push(U256::from_be_bytes(&h));
        }
    }
    fn poseidon2(&mut self, words: &mut [u32], n: usize) { HostRef.poseidon2(words, n) }
}

fn dry_run(call: &EvmCall) -> (Outcome, Vec<U256>) {
    let words = call.input_words();
    let mut ws = Box::new(Workspace::ZERO);
    let mut host = TraceHost { mapping_hashes: Vec::new() };
    let (_, o) = run_call_with(&mut host, &mut ws, |i| words[i as usize], words.len() as u32);
    (o, host.mapping_hashes)
}

fn seed_tree(seeds: &[StorageSeed]) -> Result<(SparseTree, Vec<U256>), String> {
    let mut tree = SparseTree::new();
    let mut slots = Vec::new();
    for s in seeds {
        let (slot, value) = match s {
            StorageSeed::Slot { slot, value } => (parse_u256(slot)?, parse_u256(value)?),
            StorageSeed::Mapping { base, key, value } => (mapping_slot(&parse_u256(key)?, *base), parse_u256(value)?),
            StorageSeed::Mapping2 { base, key1, key2, value } => (mapping_slot2(&parse_u256(key1)?, &parse_u256(key2)?, *base), parse_u256(value)?),
        };
        tree.insert(slot, value);
        if !slots.contains(&slot) { slots.push(slot); }
    }
    Ok((tree, slots))
}

fn collides(touched: &[U256], s: &U256) -> bool { touched.iter().any(|t| slot_index(t) == slot_index(s)) }

pub fn build(code: &[u8], form: &EvmCallForm) -> Result<(EvmCall, Vec<DiscoveryStep>), String> {
    let calldata = encode_calldata(&form.function, &form.args)?;
    let (tree, seeded) = seed_tree(&form.storage)?;
    if seeded.len() > MAX_WITNESSES { return Err(format!("{} seeded slots; the guest takes at most {MAX_WITNESSES}", seeded.len())); }
    let mut call = EvmCall {
        code: code.to_vec(), calldata,
        address: form.address.as_deref().map(parse_u256).transpose()?.unwrap_or(TOKEN),
        caller: parse_u256(&form.caller)?, callvalue: parse_u256(&form.value)?,
        gas_limit: form.gas_limit.min(u32::MAX as u64), tree, touched: seeded.clone(),
    };
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let mut tried_constants: BTreeSet<u32> = BTreeSet::new();
    for run in 0..MAX_RUNS {
        let (o, hashes) = dry_run(&call);
        if o.halt != Halt::NoWitness {
            prune(&mut call, &seeded, &o);
            return Ok((call, steps));
        }
        if call.touched.len() >= MAX_WITNESSES {
            return Err(format!("the call reads more than {MAX_WITNESSES} storage slots, the most one guest input can carry"));
        }
        let next = hashes.iter().rev().find(|h| !call.touched.contains(h) && !collides(&call.touched, h)).copied()
            .or_else(|| (0..CONSTANT_SLOTS).find(|c| !tried_constants.contains(c) && !call.touched.contains(&U256::from_u32(*c))).map(|c| { tried_constants.insert(c); U256::from_u32(c) }));
        let Some(slot) = next else { return Err("could not find the storage slot the call reads (it is not a constant below 16 or a mapping entry)".into()) };
        call.touched.push(slot);
        steps.push(DiscoveryStep { run, slot: u256_hex(&slot), gas_used: o.gas_used });
    }
    Err(format!("witness discovery gave up after {MAX_RUNS} runs"))
}

/// Remove discovered slots the call does not need: same halt and same gas without them.
fn prune(call: &mut EvmCall, seeded: &[U256], reference: &Outcome) {
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < call.touched.len() {
        let s = call.touched[i];
        if seeded.contains(&s) { i += 1; continue; }
        call.touched.remove(i);
        let (o, _) = dry_run(call);
        if o.halt == reference.halt && o.gas_used == reference.gas_used { continue; }
        call.touched.insert(i, s);
        i += 1;
    }
}

pub fn erc20_transfer_form() -> EvmCallForm {
    EvmCallForm {
        function: "transfer(address,uint256)".into(), args: vec![u256_hex(&BOB), "250".into()],
        caller: u256_hex(&ALICE), address: None, value: "0".into(), gas_limit: 1_000_000,
        storage: vec![StorageSeed::Mapping { base: 0, key: u256_hex(&ALICE), value: "1000".into() }],
    }
}
```

Only `U256::{ZERO, ONE, from_u32, from_be_bytes, to_be_bytes}` are used; arithmetic is done on big-endian byte arrays so nothing depends on `evm_core::u256`'s operator names.

Add `pub mod evm_call;` to `lib.rs`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `sbpf_call.rs`**

```rust
//! A Solana instruction as a developer writes it (program id, accounts, data) → the serialized
//! input the program's entrypoint reads, exactly as the runtime lays it out.
use crate::views::{AccountForm, SbpfCallForm};
use rand_zkvm::sbpf::{deserialize_accounts, deserialize_instruction, serialize_aligned, spl_transfer, Account, SbpfCall, MAX_ACCOUNTS};

pub fn parse_key(s: &str) -> Result<[u8; 32], String> {
    let s = s.trim();
    let bytes = if let Some(h) = s.strip_prefix("0x") { hex::decode(h).map_err(|e| format!("{s}: {e}"))? }
        else { bs58::decode(s).into_vec().map_err(|e| format!("{s}: {e}"))? };
    bytes.try_into().map_err(|b: Vec<u8>| format!("{s}: {} bytes, a key is 32", b.len()))
}

fn parse_hex(s: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> { hex::decode(s.trim().trim_start_matches("0x")).map_err(|e| format!("{s}: {e}")) }

pub fn build(elf: &[u8], form: &SbpfCallForm) -> Result<SbpfCall, String> {
    if form.accounts.len() > MAX_ACCOUNTS { return Err(format!("{} accounts; at most {MAX_ACCOUNTS}", form.accounts.len())); }
    let program_id = parse_key(&form.program_id)?;
    let accounts = form.accounts.iter().map(|a| Ok(Account {
        key: parse_key(&a.key)?, owner: parse_key(&a.owner)?, lamports: a.lamports, data: parse_hex(&a.data)?,
        is_signer: a.is_signer, is_writable: a.is_writable, executable: a.executable, rent_epoch: u64::MAX,
    })).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, String>>()?;
    let data = parse_hex(&form.data)?;
    let input = serialize_aligned(&accounts, &data, &program_id);
    if input.len() > sbpf_core::abi::MAX_INPUT_BYTES { return Err(format!("serialized input is {} bytes; the guest takes at most {}", input.len(), sbpf_core::abi::MAX_INPUT_BYTES)); }
    Ok(SbpfCall { elf: elf.to_vec(), input })
}

fn key_text(k: &[u8; 32]) -> String { bs58::encode(k).into_string() }

pub fn form_from_input(input: &[u8]) -> SbpfCallForm {
    let accounts = deserialize_accounts(input).into_iter().map(|a| AccountForm {
        key: key_text(&a.key), owner: key_text(&a.owner), lamports: a.lamports, data: format!("0x{}", hex::encode(&a.data)),
        is_signer: a.is_signer, is_writable: a.is_writable, executable: a.executable,
    }).collect();
    let (data, program_id) = deserialize_instruction(input);
    SbpfCallForm { program_id: key_text(&program_id), accounts, data: format!("0x{}", hex::encode(data)) }
}

pub fn spl_transfer_form(amount: u64) -> SbpfCallForm { form_from_input(&spl_transfer(amount).input) }
```

`form.data` is tag 3 then 250 as a little-endian `u64`.

`rent_epoch` is fixed at `u64::MAX` as `spl_transfer` does; if `deserialize_accounts` returns a different `rent_epoch` for the SPL fixture, add a `rent_epoch` field to `AccountForm` (default `u64::MAX`) rather than hard-coding.

Add `pub mod sbpf_call;` to `lib.rs`.

```rust
//! The file formats at each stage boundary: what goes into solc and comes out, the shim crate the
//! RV32 build compiles, and cargo's JSON diagnostics. Pure functions; the API drives the I/O.
use crate::views::{Diagnostic, StageError};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::io;
use std::path::Path;

pub const SHIM_UP: &str = "../../..";

pub fn solc_input(files: &[(String, String)]) -> Value {
    let sources: serde_json::Map<String, Value> = files.iter().map(|(n, c)| (n.clone(), json!({ "content": c }))).collect();
    json!({
        "language": "Solidity",
        "sources": sources,
        "settings": {
            "optimizer": { "enabled": true, "runs": 200 },
            "evmVersion": "shanghai",
            "outputSelection": { "*": { "*": ["abi", "evm.bytecode.object", "evm.deployedBytecode.object"] } }
        }
    })
}

pub fn line_col(src: &str, byte: usize) -> (u32, u32) {
    let byte = byte.min(src.len());
    let before = &src.as_bytes()[..byte];
    let line = before.iter().filter(|&&b| b == b'\n').count() as u32 + 1;
    let col = (byte - before.iter().rposition(|&b| b == b'\n').map(|p| p + 1).unwrap_or(0)) as u32 + 1;
    (line, col)
}

pub struct SolcResult { pub contract: String, pub contracts: Vec<String>, pub runtime: Vec<u8>, pub creation_hex: String, pub abi: Value }

fn err(kind: &str, message: String, diagnostics: Vec<Diagnostic>) -> StageError { StageError { kind: kind.into(), message, diagnostics } }

pub fn parse_solc_output(out: &Value, files: &[(String, String)], want: Option<&str>) -> Result<SolcResult, StageError> {
    let diagnostics: Vec<Diagnostic> = out["errors"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().map(|e| {
        let file = e["sourceLocation"]["file"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let start = e["sourceLocation"]["start"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize;
        let src = files.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == file).map(|(_, c)| c.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        let (line, col) = if file.is_empty() { (0, 0) } else { line_col(src, start) };
        Diagnostic { file, line, col, severity: e["severity"].as_str().unwrap_or("error").to_string(), message: e["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string() }
    }).collect();
    if diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.severity == "error") {
        let first = diagnostics.iter().find(|d| d.severity == "error").unwrap();
        return Err(err("compile", format!("{}:{}:{}: {}", first.file, first.line, first.col, first.message), diagnostics));
    }
    let mut found: Vec<(String, Value)> = Vec::new();
    for (_, by_name) in out["contracts"].as_object().into_iter().flatten() {
        for (name, c) in by_name.as_object().into_iter().flatten() {
            if !c["evm"]["deployedBytecode"]["object"].as_str().unwrap_or("").is_empty() { found.push((name.clone(), c.clone())); }
        }
    }
    found.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let contracts: Vec<String> = found.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();
    let chosen = match (want, found.len()) {
        (_, 0) => return Err(err("compile", "no deployable contract: every contract is abstract or an interface".into(), diagnostics)),
        (Some(w), _) => found.iter().find(|(n, _)| n == w).ok_or_else(|| err("unsupported", format!("no contract named {w}; the file defines {}", contracts.join(", ")), diagnostics.clone()))?,
        (None, 1) => &found[0],
        (None, _) => return Err(err("unsupported", format!("choose a contract: the file defines {}", contracts.join(", ")), diagnostics)),
    };
    let runtime = hex::decode(chosen.1["evm"]["deployedBytecode"]["object"].as_str().unwrap()).map_err(|e| err("compile", format!("solc printed bad hex: {e}"), vec![]))?;
    Ok(SolcResult {
        contract: chosen.0.clone(), contracts, runtime,
        creation_hex: chosen.1["evm"]["bytecode"]["object"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
        abi: chosen.1["abi"].clone(),
    })
}

fn write_all(dir: &Path, entries: &[(&str, &str)]) -> io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src"))?;
    for (name, content) in entries { std::fs::write(dir.join(name), content)?; }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn write_evm_shim(dir: &Path, name: &str, contract_c: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    let f = evm2rv::shim::files(name, SHIM_UP);
    write_all(dir, &[("contract.c", contract_c), ("Cargo.toml", &f.cargo_toml), ("Cargo.lock", &f.cargo_lock), ("build.rs", &f.build_rs), ("src/main.rs", &f.main_rs), ("shim.ld", &f.ld)])
}

pub fn write_sbpf_shim(dir: &Path, name: &str, program_c: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    use sbpf2rv::shim;
    write_all(dir, &[("program.c", program_c), ("Cargo.toml", &shim::cargo_toml(name, SHIM_UP)), ("Cargo.lock", &shim::cargo_lock(name)), ("build.rs", &shim::build_rs(SHIM_UP)), ("src/main.rs", shim::MAIN_RS), ("shim.ld", shim::LINKER_SCRIPT)])
}

pub fn rustc_diagnostics(stdout: &str) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    stdout.lines().filter_map(|l| serde_json::from_str::<Value>(l).ok()).filter(|v| v["reason"] == "compiler-message").map(|v| {
        let m = &v["message"];
        let span = m["spans"].as_array().and_then(|s| s.iter().find(|s| s["is_primary"] == true).or_else(|| s.first())).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        Diagnostic {
            file: span["file_name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            line: span["line_start"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as u32,
            col: span["column_start"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as u32,
            severity: m["level"].as_str().unwrap_or("error").to_string(),
            message: m["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
        }
    }).collect()
}
```

The `solc_input` `outputSelection` lists three outputs; solc also needs `metadata` only if the UI shows it, which v1 does not.

Add `pub mod stages;` to `lib.rs`.

  ```rust
  pub enum Tool { Solc, BuildSbf, RandGuestBuild { debug: bool } }
  pub struct RunSpec { pub tool: Tool, pub work: PathBuf, pub timeout: Duration }
  pub struct RunOutput { pub status: i32, pub stdout: String, pub stderr: String, pub timed_out: bool }
  #[derive(thiserror::Error, Debug)] pub enum SandboxError { #[error("spawn: {0}")] Spawn(String), #[error("io: {0}")] Io(#[from] std::io::Error) }
  #[async_trait] pub trait Runner: Send + Sync { async fn run(&self, spec: RunSpec) -> Result<RunOutput, SandboxError>; }
  pub struct DockerRunner { pub program: String, pub image: String, pub memory: String, pub cpus: String }
  impl DockerRunner { pub fn new(image: impl Into<String>) -> Self; pub fn args(&self, spec: &RunSpec, name: &str) -> Vec<String>; }
  pub struct FakeRunner<F> (wraps Fn(&RunSpec) -> RunOutput)
  pub const MAX_CAPTURE: usize = 1 << 20;
  pub fn timeout_for(tool: &Tool) -> Duration  // solc 30 s, build-sbf 300 s, rand-guest 180 s
  pub fn mount_point(tool: &Tool) -> &'static str // "/work" for solc and build-sbf, "/circuits/jobs/job" for rand-guest
  ```

```rust
use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
use std::time::Duration;
use terminal_sandbox::*;

#[test]
fn docker_arguments_are_the_locked_down_set() {
    let r = DockerRunner::new("terminal-sandbox:test");
    let spec = RunSpec { tool: Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: true }, work: "/data/jobs/abc/build".into(), timeout: Duration::from_secs(5) };
    let a = r.args(&spec, "terminal-abc");
    let joined = a.join(" ");
    for want in ["run", "--rm", "--name terminal-abc", "--network none", "--memory 4g", "--memory-swap 4g", "--cpus 2", "--pids-limit 512",
                 "--read-only", "--tmpfs /tmp:rw,exec,size=2g", "--security-opt no-new-privileges", "--cap-drop ALL", "--user 10001:10001",
                 "-v /data/jobs/abc/build:/circuits/jobs/job:rw", "terminal-sandbox:test", "rand-guest-build debug"] {
        assert!(joined.contains(want), "missing `{want}` in `{joined}`");
    }
    assert!(joined.ends_with("terminal-sandbox:test rand-guest-build debug"));
    let spec = RunSpec { tool: Tool::Solc, work: "/w".into(), timeout: Duration::from_secs(5) };
    assert!(r.args(&spec, "n").join(" ").contains("-v /w:/work:rw"));
    assert!(r.args(&spec, "n").join(" ").ends_with(" solc"));
}

#[test]
fn timeouts() {
    assert_eq!(timeout_for(&Tool::Solc), Duration::from_secs(30));
    assert_eq!(timeout_for(&Tool::BuildSbf), Duration::from_secs(300));
    assert_eq!(timeout_for(&Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: false }), Duration::from_secs(180));
}

/// A stand-in for `docker`: prints a lot, or sleeps, depending on the last argument.
fn fake_docker(dir: &std::path::Path) -> String {
    let p = dir.join("fake-docker");
    std::fs::write(&p, "#!/bin/sh\ncase \"$1\" in kill) exit 0;; esac\nlast=\"\"; for a in \"$@\"; do last=\"$a\"; done\ncase \"$last\" in\n  solc) head -c 3000000 /dev/zero | tr '\\0' 'x'; echo err >&2; exit 3;;\n  build-sbf) sleep 30;;\nesac\n").unwrap();
    std::fs::set_permissions(&p, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    p.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn captures_are_capped_and_status_is_returned() {
    let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut r = DockerRunner::new("img");
    r.program = fake_docker(d.path());
    let out = r.run(RunSpec { tool: Tool::Solc, work: d.path().into(), timeout: Duration::from_secs(10) }).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 3);
    assert!(!out.timed_out);
    assert_eq!(out.stdout.len(), MAX_CAPTURE);
    assert_eq!(out.stderr.trim(), "err");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn timeouts_kill_and_report() {
    let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut r = DockerRunner::new("img");
    r.program = fake_docker(d.path());
    let t = std::time::Instant::now();
    let out = r.run(RunSpec { tool: Tool::BuildSbf, work: d.path().into(), timeout: Duration::from_millis(300) }).await.unwrap();
    assert!(out.timed_out);
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(5));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn fake_runner_calls_the_closure() {
    let r = FakeRunner::new(|spec: &RunSpec| { std::fs::write(spec.work.join("out.txt"), "hi").unwrap(); RunOutput { status: 0, stdout: "ok".into(), stderr: String::new(), timed_out: false } });
    let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let out = r.run(RunSpec { tool: Tool::Solc, work: d.path().into(), timeout: Duration::from_secs(1) }).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.stdout, "ok");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(d.path().join("out.txt")).unwrap(), "hi");
}
```

```rust
//! Runs the untrusted tool steps (solc, cargo build-sbf, rand-guest build) in a container with no
//! network, capped memory, cpu and pids, a read-only root and one writable bind mount. One fixed
//! entrypoint per tool; nothing from the user reaches the command line.
use async_trait::async_trait;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::AsyncReadExt;
use tokio::process::Command;

pub const MAX_CAPTURE: usize = 1 << 20;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Tool { Solc, BuildSbf, RandGuestBuild { debug: bool } }

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RunSpec { pub tool: Tool, pub work: PathBuf, pub timeout: Duration }

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct RunOutput { pub status: i32, pub stdout: String, pub stderr: String, pub timed_out: bool }

#[derive(thiserror::Error, Debug)]
pub enum SandboxError {
    #[error("spawn: {0}")]
    Spawn(String),
    #[error("io: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait Runner: Send + Sync {
    async fn run(&self, spec: RunSpec) -> Result<RunOutput, SandboxError>;
}

pub fn timeout_for(tool: &Tool) -> Duration {
    match tool { Tool::Solc => Duration::from_secs(30), Tool::BuildSbf => Duration::from_secs(300), Tool::RandGuestBuild { .. } => Duration::from_secs(180) }
}

pub fn mount_point(tool: &Tool) -> &'static str {
    match tool { Tool::RandGuestBuild { .. } => "/circuits/jobs/job", _ => "/work" }
}

fn entry(tool: &Tool) -> Vec<String> {
    match tool {
        Tool::Solc => vec!["solc".into()],
        Tool::BuildSbf => vec!["build-sbf".into()],
        Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug } => vec!["rand-guest-build".into(), if *debug { "debug" } else { "release" }.into()],
    }
}

pub struct DockerRunner { pub program: String, pub image: String, pub memory: String, pub cpus: String }

impl DockerRunner {
    pub fn new(image: impl Into<String>) -> Self { Self { program: "docker".into(), image: image.into(), memory: "4g".into(), cpus: "2".into() } }

    pub fn args(&self, spec: &RunSpec, name: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut a: Vec<String> = [
            "run", "--rm", "--name", name, "--network", "none",
            "--memory", &self.memory, "--memory-swap", &self.memory, "--cpus", &self.cpus, "--pids-limit", "512",
            "--read-only", "--tmpfs", "/tmp:rw,exec,size=2g", "--security-opt", "no-new-privileges", "--cap-drop", "ALL",
            "--user", "10001:10001",
        ].iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
        a.push("-v".into());
        a.push(format!("{}:{}:rw", spec.work.display(), mount_point(&spec.tool)));
        a.push(self.image.clone());
        a.extend(entry(&spec.tool));
        a
    }
}

async fn read_capped(mut r: impl tokio::io::AsyncRead + Unpin) -> String {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
    loop {
        match r.read(&mut buf).await {
            Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
            Ok(n) => { let room = MAX_CAPTURE.saturating_sub(out.len()); out.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n.min(room)]); }
        }
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&out).into_owned()
}

#[async_trait]
impl Runner for DockerRunner {
    async fn run(&self, spec: RunSpec) -> Result<RunOutput, SandboxError> {
        let name = format!("terminal-{:016x}", rand_id());
        let mut child = Command::new(&self.program).args(self.args(&spec, &name))
            .stdin(Stdio::null()).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).kill_on_drop(true)
            .spawn().map_err(|e| SandboxError::Spawn(format!("{}: {e}", self.program)))?;
        let out = tokio::spawn(read_capped(child.stdout.take().unwrap()));
        let err = tokio::spawn(read_capped(child.stderr.take().unwrap()));
        let (status, timed_out) = match tokio::time::timeout(spec.timeout, child.wait()).await {
            Ok(s) => (s?.code().unwrap_or(-1), false),
            Err(_) => {
                let _ = Command::new(&self.program).args(["kill", &name]).output().await;
                let _ = child.kill().await;
                (-1, true)
            }
        };
        Ok(RunOutput { status, stdout: out.await.unwrap_or_default(), stderr: err.await.unwrap_or_default(), timed_out })
    }
}

fn rand_id() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u128(std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_nanos());
    h.finish()
}

pub struct FakeRunner<F: Fn(&RunSpec) -> RunOutput + Send + Sync> { f: F }

impl<F: Fn(&RunSpec) -> RunOutput + Send + Sync> FakeRunner<F> { pub fn new(f: F) -> Self { Self { f } } }

#[async_trait]
impl<F: Fn(&RunSpec) -> RunOutput + Send + Sync> Runner for FakeRunner<F> {
    async fn run(&self, spec: RunSpec) -> Result<RunOutput, SandboxError> { Ok((self.f)(&spec)) }
}
```

Remove `serde` and `tracing` from this crate's dependencies if unused (clippy will say).

### Task 14: The sandbox image and its entrypoints

**Files:**
- Create: `sandbox/Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `sandbox/entrypoints/{solc.sh,build-sbf.sh,rand-guest-build.sh,dispatch.sh}`, `sandbox/solana-template/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs}`, `sandbox/build.sh`
- Test: `api/crates/terminal-sandbox/tests/image.rs` (gated on `TERMINAL_SANDBOX`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 13's `DockerRunner`, `Tool`; Task 12's `solc_input`, `write_evm_shim`, `write_sbpf_shim`; Task 5's `analyse`; Task 4's `transpile`.
- Produces: image `terminal-sandbox:<circuits-rev>` whose entrypoint `dispatch.sh <tool> [arg]` reads and writes only the mounted directory:
  - `solc`: `/work/input.json` → `/work/output.json`.
  - `build-sbf`: `/work/src/{lib.rs,Cargo.toml}` → `/work/program.so`; cargo JSON messages on stdout.
  - `rand-guest-build release`: `/circuits/jobs/job/shim` → `/circuits/jobs/job/image.bin`; stdout is rand-guest's.
  - `rand-guest-build debug`: copies `shim` to `shim-dbg`, adds `-g` to the C flags, builds `/circuits/jobs/job/image-dbg.bin` and writes `/circuits/jobs/job/debug-line.txt`.

The image is `linux/amd64` (solc's static Linux build is x86-64 only). On Apple Silicon it runs under emulation: slow, but correct for local testing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the entrypoints**

`sandbox/entrypoints/dispatch.sh`:
```bash
#!/bin/sh
# The one entrypoint. Every tool step runs with HOME and CARGO_HOME on the tmpfs: the root is read-only.
set -eu
export HOME=/tmp/home CARGO_HOME=/tmp/cargo CARGO_NET_OFFLINE=true
mkdir -p "$HOME" "$CARGO_HOME"
case "${1:-}" in
  solc) exec /opt/entrypoints/solc.sh ;;
  build-sbf) exec /opt/entrypoints/build-sbf.sh ;;
  rand-guest-build) exec /opt/entrypoints/rand-guest-build.sh "${2:-release}" ;;
  *) echo "usage: dispatch.sh solc|build-sbf|rand-guest-build [release|debug]" >&2; exit 64 ;;
esac
```

`sandbox/entrypoints/solc.sh`:
```bash
#!/bin/sh
set -eu
/usr/local/bin/solc --standard-json --allow-paths /work < /work/input.json > /work/output.json
```

`sandbox/entrypoints/build-sbf.sh`:
```bash
#!/bin/sh
# /work/src/{lib.rs,Cargo.toml} → /work/program.so. Only the vendored crates resolve (no network).
set -eu
rm -rf /tmp/crate && cp -r /opt/solana-template /tmp/crate
cp /work/src/lib.rs /tmp/crate/src/lib.rs
[ -f /work/src/Cargo.toml ] && cp /work/src/Cargo.toml /tmp/crate/Cargo.toml
cp -r /opt/sbf-home/.cache "$HOME/"
cd /tmp/crate
if ! cargo-build-sbf --tools-version v1.54 --offline --sbf-out-dir /tmp/out -- --message-format=json; then
  echo "build failed. The sandbox has no network; only these crates are available:" >&2
  ls /opt/solana-vendor | sed 's/^/  /' >&2
  exit 1
fi
set -- /tmp/out/*.so
[ "$#" -eq 1 ] && [ -f "$1" ] || { echo "expected exactly one .so in the output, got: $*" >&2; exit 65; }
cp "$1" /work/program.so
```

`sandbox/entrypoints/rand-guest-build.sh`:
```bash
#!/bin/sh
# release: build the shim exactly as published (hc reproducible).
# debug:   a copy with -g added after -Os, for the DWARF line table only.
set -eu
J=/circuits/jobs/job
cp -r /opt/cargo-registry "$CARGO_HOME/registry"
elf_of() { find "$1/target/riscv32im-unknown-none-elf/release" -maxdepth 1 -type f -perm -u+x | head -1; }
case "$1" in
  release)
    rand-guest build "$J/shim" --max-words 65535 --out "$J/image.bin" ;;
  debug)
    rm -rf "$J/shim-dbg" && cp -r "$J/shim" "$J/shim-dbg" && rm -rf "$J/shim-dbg/target"
    sed -i -e 's/\.flag("-Os")/.flag("-Os").flag("-g")/' -e 's/"-Os",/"-Os", "-g",/' "$J/shim-dbg/build.rs"
    grep -q '"-g"' "$J/shim-dbg/build.rs" || { echo "could not add -g to build.rs" >&2; exit 66; }
    rand-guest build "$J/shim-dbg" --max-words 65535 --out "$J/image-dbg.bin"
    /opt/llvm/bin/llvm-dwarfdump --debug-line "$(elf_of "$J/shim-dbg")" > "$J/debug-line.txt" ;;
  *) echo "release|debug" >&2; exit 64 ;;
esac
```

The `-g` edit targets the literal flag in the generated `build.rs`; both generators write `-Os` as a string literal (Task 12's shim tests confirm the generated text). The grep refuses to continue if the edit matched nothing, and the API then reports "no debug build" in the image view.

`sandbox/solana-template/Cargo.toml`:
```toml
[package]
name = "program"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[lib]
crate-type = ["cdylib", "lib"]

[dependencies]
solana-program = "=2.3.0"

[features]
no-entrypoint = []
```

`sandbox/solana-template/src/lib.rs` (the Solana counter sample, also the template's warm-up build):
```rust
use solana_program::{account_info::{next_account_info, AccountInfo}, entrypoint, entrypoint::ProgramResult, msg, program_error::ProgramError, pubkey::Pubkey};

entrypoint!(process_instruction);

/// Adds the instruction's u64 to the u64 stored in the first account.
pub fn process_instruction(_program_id: &Pubkey, accounts: &[AccountInfo], data: &[u8]) -> ProgramResult {
    let acc = next_account_info(&mut accounts.iter())?;
    let by = u64::from_le_bytes(data.get(..8).ok_or(ProgramError::InvalidInstructionData)?.try_into().unwrap());
    let mut d = acc.try_borrow_mut_data()?;
    let now = u64::from_le_bytes(d.get(..8).ok_or(ProgramError::AccountDataTooSmall)?.try_into().unwrap());
    d[..8].copy_from_slice(&now.wrapping_add(by).to_le_bytes());
    msg!("counter");
    Ok(())
}
```

`solana-program = "=2.3.0"` must build with platform-tools v1.54's rustc 1.89; the Dockerfile's warm-up build proves it. If it fails on an MSRV error, step down one minor version at a time and record the version that builds in this file.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the Dockerfile**

`sandbox/Dockerfile` (build context is the repo root):
```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1.7
# Rand Terminal's sandbox: every untrusted tool step runs here, without network.
# Build: sandbox/build.sh  (docker build --platform linux/amd64 -f sandbox/Dockerfile -t terminal-sandbox:<rev> .)
FROM --platform=linux/amd64 debian:bookworm-slim
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      ca-certificates curl xz-utils bzip2 build-essential pkg-config libssl-dev libudev-dev git \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

ENV RUSTUP_HOME=/opt/rustup CARGO_HOME=/opt/cargo PATH=/opt/cargo/bin:/opt/solana-release/bin:/opt/llvm/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin
RUN curl --proto '=https' -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y --profile minimal --default-toolchain 1.98.1 \
    && rustup target add riscv32im-unknown-none-elf --toolchain 1.98.1

# clang 23.1.1: the hc of every image is a function of this compiler.
RUN curl -sSfL https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/releases/download/llvmorg-23.1.1/LLVM-23.1.1-Linux-X64.tar.xz \
      | tar -xJ -C /opt && ln -s /opt/LLVM-23.1.1-Linux-X64 /opt/llvm
ENV CLANG=/opt/llvm/bin/clang

# solc 0.8.37, pinned by hash (the same pin as fullnode's ci.yml).
RUN curl -sSfL -o /usr/local/bin/solc https://github.com/ethereum/solidity/releases/download/v0.8.37/solc-static-linux \
    && echo "5de843c2c93563cc66425c99a4fb13fdbf32b4c4ae07469480faaf126e14404a  /usr/local/bin/solc" | sha256sum -c \
    && chmod 0755 /usr/local/bin/solc

# cargo-build-sbf from agave v4.3.0, platform-tools v1.54 fetched once into /opt/sbf-home.
RUN curl -sSfL https://github.com/anza-xyz/agave/releases/download/v4.3.0/solana-release-x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu.tar.bz2 \
      | tar -xj -C /opt
COPY sandbox/solana-template /opt/solana-template
RUN cd /opt/solana-template && HOME=/opt/sbf-home cargo vendor /opt/solana-vendor > /tmp/vendor.toml \
    && mkdir -p .cargo && cp /tmp/vendor.toml .cargo/config.toml \
    && HOME=/opt/sbf-home cargo-build-sbf --tools-version v1.54 --offline --sbf-out-dir /tmp/warm \
    && test -f /tmp/warm/program.so && rm -rf /tmp/warm target

# circuits at the pinned revision; rand-guest built once; the shim crates' registry warmed by
# translating and building the two reference programs, whose hc must equal the published ones.
COPY circuits /circuits
RUN cd /circuits/rand-guest && cargo build --release --locked && install -m 0755 target/release/rand-guest /usr/local/bin/rand-guest \
 && cd /circuits/evm2rv && cargo build --release --locked && cd /circuits/sbpf2rv && cargo build --release --locked
RUN set -e; mkdir -p /circuits/jobs/warm; \
    /circuits/evm2rv/target/release/evm2rv /circuits/guests-compiled/evm/contracts/erc20.runtime.hex --out /circuits/jobs/warm/erc20 --chain-id 13; \
    rand-guest build /circuits/jobs/warm/erc20 --max-words 65535 | tee /tmp/erc20.txt; \
    grep -q "hc a0feae92a7311c9562495717100eb7aea71270a38ed435d06dc31387e0ea8ff6" /tmp/erc20.txt; \
    /circuits/sbpf2rv/target/release/sbpf2rv /circuits/guests-compiled/sbpf/programs/spl_token.so --out /circuits/jobs/warm/spl --name spl-token; \
    rand-guest build /circuits/jobs/warm/spl --max-words 65535 | tee /tmp/spl.txt; \
    grep -q "hc 8ca905ae3c62f503de7de86829040f323e098b53dba5fffe09b42f1aad16758b" /tmp/spl.txt; \
    rm -rf /circuits/jobs/warm /circuits/*/target; \
    cp -r /opt/cargo/registry /opt/cargo-registry

COPY sandbox/entrypoints /opt/entrypoints
RUN chmod 0755 /opt/entrypoints/*.sh && mkdir -p /circuits/jobs/job && chown 10001:10001 /circuits/jobs/job \
 && useradd -u 10001 -M -d /tmp/home sandbox && chmod -R a+rX /opt /circuits
USER 10001:10001
WORKDIR /tmp
ENTRYPOINT ["/opt/entrypoints/dispatch.sh"]
```

The `grep -q "hc …"` lines fail the image build if the pinned compilers ever stop reproducing the published images. Those two hashes come from `transpiled/{ethereum,solana}/out/info.txt`, built at chain id 13. `rm -rf /circuits/*/target` keeps the image small; the API never runs those binaries in the container except `rand-guest`, which is installed in `/usr/local/bin`.

`.dockerignore` (repo root):
```
**/target
**/node_modules
web/.next
data
.git
circuits/.git
```

`sandbox/build.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the sandbox image tagged with the circuits revision it embeds.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
REV=$(git -C circuits rev-parse --short=7 HEAD)
docker build --platform linux/amd64 -f sandbox/Dockerfile -t "terminal-sandbox:$REV" .
echo "terminal-sandbox:$REV"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the gated integration test**

`api/crates/terminal-sandbox/tests/image.rs`:
```rust
//! Real builds in the real image. Skips unless TERMINAL_SANDBOX names the image tag.
use std::time::Duration;
use terminal_core::{evm_analysis, sbpf_analysis, stages};
use terminal_sandbox::*;

fn image() -> Option<DockerRunner> {
    match std::env::var("TERMINAL_SANDBOX") { Ok(t) => Some(DockerRunner::new(t)), Err(_) => { eprintln!("skipping: TERMINAL_SANDBOX unset"); None } }
}
fn fixtures() -> std::path::PathBuf { std::path::PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../terminal-core/tests/fixtures") }
fn info_hc(name: &str) -> String { std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures().join(name)).unwrap().lines().find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("hc ").map(str::to_string)).unwrap() }
fn spec(tool: Tool, work: &std::path::Path) -> RunSpec { RunSpec { tool, work: work.into(), timeout: timeout_for(&tool) } }
/// A scratch directory the container's uid 10001 can write.
fn work() -> tempfile::TempDir {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    let d = work();
    std::fs::set_permissions(d.path(), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o777)).unwrap();
    d
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn erc20_sol_compiles_to_the_fixture_bytecode() {
    let Some(r) = image() else { return };
    let d = work();
    let src = std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures().join("ERC20.sol")).unwrap();
    let files = vec![("ERC20.sol".to_string(), src)];
    std::fs::write(d.path().join("input.json"), stages::solc_input(&files).to_string()).unwrap();
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::Solc, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 0, "{}", out.stderr);
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(d.path().join("output.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let res = stages::parse_solc_output(&v, &files, Some("ERC20")).unwrap();
    let want = std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures().join("erc20.runtime.hex")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(hex::encode(&res.runtime), want.trim().trim_start_matches("0x"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn erc20_shim_builds_to_the_published_hc_and_the_debug_map_lines_up() {
    let Some(r) = image() else { return };
    let d = work();
    let code = hex::decode(std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures().join("erc20.runtime.hex")).unwrap().trim()).unwrap();
    let (view, c) = evm_analysis::transpile(&code, 13, 2).unwrap();
    stages::write_evm_shim(&d.path().join("shim"), "contract", &c).unwrap();
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: false }, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 0, "{}\n{}", out.stdout, out.stderr);
    let img = std::fs::read(d.path().join("image.bin")).unwrap();
    let iv = terminal_core::image::view(&img, 65_535, &view.op_map, None, "contract.c").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(iv.hc, info_hc("erc20.info.txt"));
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: true }, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 0, "{}\n{}", out.stdout, out.stderr);
    let dbg = std::fs::read(d.path().join("image-dbg.bin")).unwrap();
    let (_, t1, _) = rand_guest::pack::split(&img).unwrap();
    let (_, t2, _) = rand_guest::pack::split(&dbg).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(t1, t2, "-g changed the code: the debug map would not describe the production image");
    let dl = std::fs::read_to_string(d.path().join("debug-line.txt")).unwrap();
    let rows = terminal_core::source_map::parse_debug_line(&dl, "contract.c");
    assert!(rows.len() > 500, "{} rows", rows.len());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn spl_shim_builds_to_the_published_hc() {
    let Some(r) = image() else { return };
    let d = work();
    let a = sbpf_analysis::analyse(&std::fs::read(fixtures().join("spl_token.so")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    stages::write_sbpf_shim(&d.path().join("shim"), "spl-token", &a.program_c).unwrap();
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: false }, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 0, "{}\n{}", out.stdout, out.stderr);
    let iv = terminal_core::image::view(&std::fs::read(d.path().join("image.bin")).unwrap(), 65_535, &[], None, "program.c").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(iv.hc, info_hc("spl.info.txt"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn solana_counter_builds_from_source_and_transpiles() {
    let Some(r) = image() else { return };
    let d = work();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(d.path().join("src")).unwrap();
    let tpl = std::path::PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../../sandbox/solana-template");
    std::fs::copy(tpl.join("src/lib.rs"), d.path().join("src/lib.rs")).unwrap();
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::BuildSbf, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status, 0, "{}\n{}", out.stdout, out.stderr);
    let a = sbpf_analysis::analyse(&std::fs::read(d.path().join("program.so")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(a.compile.syscalls.iter().any(|s| s.name == "sol_log_"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_rust_error_comes_back_as_a_diagnostic() {
    let Some(r) = image() else { return };
    let d = work();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(d.path().join("src")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(d.path().join("src/lib.rs"), "pub fn f() -> u8 { y }\n").unwrap();
    let out = r.run(spec(Tool::BuildSbf, d.path())).await.unwrap();
    assert_ne!(out.status, 0);
    let diags = stages::rustc_diagnostics(&out.stdout);
    assert!(diags.iter().any(|d| d.severity == "error" && d.line == 1), "{diags:?}\n{}", out.stderr);
}
```

Add to `terminal-sandbox`'s `[dev-dependencies]`: `terminal-core = { workspace = true }`, `rand-guest = { workspace = true }`, `serde_json = { workspace = true }`, `hex = { workspace = true }`.

`work()` makes each scratch directory writable by the container's uid 10001; `shim/` subdirectories created by the test inherit the host user, so the build entrypoint writes only beside them and into `shim/target`, which `rand-guest` creates itself. If that fails with a permission error, `chmod -R a+rwX` the directory before each run.

```bash
chmod +x sandbox/build.sh sandbox/entrypoints/*.sh
TAG=$(sandbox/build.sh | tail -1)
```

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use axum::{body::Body, http::{HeaderMap, Request, StatusCode}, Router};
use http_body_util::BodyExt;
use serde_json::Value;
use tower::ServiceExt;

pub fn json_req(method: &str, uri: &str, body: Option<Value>) -> Request<Body> {
    let b = Request::builder().method(method).uri(uri).header("x-forwarded-for", "198.51.100.7");
    match body {
        Some(v) => b.header("content-type", "application/json").body(Body::from(v.to_string())).unwrap(),
        None => b.body(Body::empty()).unwrap(),
    }
}

pub async fn call(app: &Router, req: Request<Body>) -> (StatusCode, HeaderMap, Value) {
    let res = app.clone().oneshot(req).await.unwrap();
    let status = res.status();
    let headers = res.headers().clone();
    let bytes = res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes();
    let json = if bytes.is_empty() { Value::Null } else { serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap_or_else(|_| Value::String(String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).into())) };
    (status, headers, json)
}
```

```rust
//! Rand Terminal API (axum).
pub mod error;
pub mod handlers;
pub mod ratelimit;
pub mod routes;
pub mod state;

use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::path::PathBuf;

/// The circuits revision this binary links, from the submodule at build time (`build.rs`).
pub const CIRCUITS_REV: &str = env!("TERMINAL_CIRCUITS_REV");

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ApiConfig {
    pub listen_addr: SocketAddr,
    pub data_dir: PathBuf,
    pub samples_dir: PathBuf,
    pub sandbox_image: String,
    pub max_program_words: usize,
    pub max_builds: usize,
    pub max_runs: usize,
    pub data_cap_bytes: u64,
    pub job_ttl_secs: u64,
    pub trust_proxy: bool,
    pub jobs_rpm: u32,
    pub runs_rpm: u32,
    pub api_rpm: u32,
}

impl Default for ApiConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            listen_addr: ([127, 0, 0, 1], 8080).into(),
            data_dir: PathBuf::from("data"),
            samples_dir: PathBuf::from("../samples"),
            sandbox_image: format!("terminal-sandbox:{CIRCUITS_REV}"),
            max_program_words: 65_535,
            max_builds: 2,
            max_runs: 4,
            data_cap_bytes: 20 << 30,
            job_ttl_secs: 3600,
            trust_proxy: false,
            jobs_rpm: 10,
            runs_rpm: 60,
            api_rpm: 600,
        }
    }
}

impl ApiConfig {
    pub fn from_env() -> Self {
        let d = Self::default();
        let var = |k: &str| std::env::var(k).ok().map(|v| v.trim().to_string()).filter(|v| !v.is_empty());
        fn num<T: std::str::FromStr>(v: Option<String>, d: T) -> T { v.and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(d) }
        let host = var("API_HOST").unwrap_or_else(|| "127.0.0.1".into());
        let port: u16 = num(var("API_PORT"), 8080);
        Self {
            listen_addr: format!("{host}:{port}").parse().unwrap_or(d.listen_addr),
            data_dir: var("TERMINAL_DATA").map(PathBuf::from).unwrap_or(d.data_dir),
            samples_dir: var("TERMINAL_SAMPLES").map(PathBuf::from).unwrap_or(d.samples_dir),
            sandbox_image: var("TERMINAL_SANDBOX_IMAGE").unwrap_or(d.sandbox_image),
            max_program_words: num(var("TERMINAL_MAX_PROGRAM_WORDS"), d.max_program_words),
            max_builds: num(var("TERMINAL_MAX_CONCURRENT_BUILDS"), d.max_builds),
            max_runs: num(var("TERMINAL_MAX_CONCURRENT_RUNS"), d.max_runs),
            data_cap_bytes: num::<u64>(var("TERMINAL_DATA_CAP_GB"), 20) << 30,
            job_ttl_secs: d.job_ttl_secs,
            trust_proxy: var("TRUST_PROXY").map(|v| !matches!(v.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(), "0" | "false" | "no" | "off")).unwrap_or(false),
            jobs_rpm: num(var("TERMINAL_JOBS_RPM"), d.jobs_rpm),
            runs_rpm: num(var("TERMINAL_RUNS_RPM"), d.runs_rpm),
            api_rpm: num(var("TERMINAL_API_RPM"), d.api_rpm),
        }
    }
}
```

`src/error.rs`:
```rust
use axum::{http::StatusCode, response::{IntoResponse, Response}, Json};
use serde_json::json;

#[derive(thiserror::Error, Debug)]
pub enum AppError {
    #[error("not found: {0}")] NotFound(String),
    #[error("bad request: {0}")] BadRequest(String),
    #[error("too large: {0}")] PayloadTooLarge(String),
    #[error("conflict: {0}")] Conflict(String),
    #[error("rate limited")] TooManyRequests { retry_after: u64 },
    #[error("unavailable: {0}")] Unavailable(String),
    #[error("internal: {0}")] Internal(String),
}

impl From<std::io::Error> for AppError { fn from(e: std::io::Error) -> Self { AppError::Internal(e.to_string()) } }
impl From<serde_json::Error> for AppError { fn from(e: serde_json::Error) -> Self { AppError::Internal(e.to_string()) } }

impl IntoResponse for AppError {
    fn into_response(self) -> Response {
        let (status, tag, message) = match self {
            AppError::NotFound(m) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "not_found", m),
            AppError::BadRequest(m) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "bad_request", m),
            AppError::PayloadTooLarge(m) => (StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE, "payload_too_large", m),
            AppError::Conflict(m) => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "conflict", m),
            AppError::Unavailable(m) => (StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "unavailable", m),
            AppError::TooManyRequests { retry_after } => {
                let body = Json(json!({ "error": "rate_limited", "message": format!("too many requests; retry in {retry_after} seconds") }));
                return (StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, [("retry-after", retry_after.to_string())], body).into_response();
            }
            AppError::Internal(m) => { tracing::error!("internal error: {m}"); (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "internal", "internal error".to_string()) }
        };
        (status, Json(json!({ "error": tag, "message": message }))).into_response()
    }
}

pub type ApiResult<T> = Result<T, AppError>;
```

`src/ratelimit.rs` — randscan's fixed 60-second window (`randscan/crates/randscan-api/src/ratelimit.rs`), trimmed to IPs and three buckets:
```rust
//! Fixed-window rate limiting per client IP, in memory, in three buckets: job submissions,
//! run submissions, and everything else.
use crate::{error::AppError, state::AppState};
use axum::{extract::{ConnectInfo, Request, State}, http::{HeaderMap, Method}, middleware::Next, response::{IntoResponse, Response}};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

const WINDOW_SECS: u64 = 60;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Decision { Allowed { remaining: u32 }, Limited { retry_after: u64 } }

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct RateLimiter { windows: Mutex<HashMap<String, (u64, u32)>> }

impl RateLimiter {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self::default() }
    pub fn check(&self, id: &str, limit: u32, now: u64) -> Decision {
        if limit == 0 { return Decision::Allowed { remaining: u32::MAX }; }
        let start = now - now % WINDOW_SECS;
        let mut map = self.windows.lock().unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
        let e = map.entry(id.to_string()).or_insert((start, 0));
        if e.0 != start { *e = (start, 0); }
        if e.1 >= limit { return Decision::Limited { retry_after: start + WINDOW_SECS - now }; }
        e.1 += 1;
        Decision::Allowed { remaining: limit - e.1 }
    }
    pub fn sweep(&self, now: u64) { self.windows.lock().unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner()).retain(|_, (s, _)| *s + WINDOW_SECS > now); }
    pub fn spawn_sweeper(self: Arc<Self>) {
        tokio::spawn(async move { loop { tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(300)).await; self.sweep(unix_now()); } });
    }
}

pub fn unix_now() -> u64 { SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0) }

/// The client address: the first `X-Forwarded-For` hop when behind nginx (`trust_proxy`), else the peer.
pub fn client_ip(headers: &HeaderMap, peer: Option<SocketAddr>, trust_proxy: bool) -> String {
    if trust_proxy {
        if let Some(ip) = headers.get("x-forwarded-for").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).and_then(|v| v.split(',').next()).map(|s| s.trim().to_string()).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) { return ip; }
    }
    peer.map(|p| p.ip().to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".into())
}

pub async fn limit(State(state): State<AppState>, req: Request, next: Next) -> Response {
    let peer = req.extensions().get::<ConnectInfo<SocketAddr>>().map(|c| c.0);
    let ip = client_ip(req.headers(), peer, state.config.trust_proxy);
    let path = req.uri().path();
    let (bucket, rpm) = match (req.method() == Method::POST, path) {
        (true, p) if p.ends_with("/jobs") => ("jobs", state.config.jobs_rpm),
        (true, p) if p.ends_with("/runs") => ("runs", state.config.runs_rpm),
        _ => ("api", state.config.api_rpm),
    };
    match state.limiter.check(&format!("{bucket}:{ip}"), rpm, unix_now()) {
        Decision::Allowed { .. } => next.run(req).await,
        Decision::Limited { retry_after } => AppError::TooManyRequests { retry_after }.into_response(),
    }
}
```

```rust
use crate::{handlers, ratelimit, state::AppState};
use axum::{extract::DefaultBodyLimit, middleware, routing::get, Router};
use tower_http::{compression::CompressionLayer, cors::{Any, CorsLayer}, trace::TraceLayer};

/// Request bodies: 512 KB of source or a 2 MB ELF as base64 (≈ 2.7 MB), with room for JSON.
pub const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4 << 20;

pub fn create_router(state: AppState) -> Router {
    let api = Router::new()
        .route("/health", get(handlers::health))
        .fallback(handlers::not_found)
        .layer(middleware::from_fn_with_state(state.clone(), ratelimit::limit));
    Router::new()
        .nest("/api/v1", api)
        .fallback(handlers::not_found)
        .layer(DefaultBodyLimit::max(BODY_LIMIT))
        .layer(CompressionLayer::new())
        .layer(TraceLayer::new_for_http())
        .layer(CorsLayer::new().allow_origin(Any).allow_methods(Any).allow_headers(Any))
        .with_state(state)
}
```

```rust
use terminal_api::{routes::create_router, state::AppState, ApiConfig};
use tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt().with_env_filter(EnvFilter::try_from_default_env().unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("info"))).with_target(false).init();
    let cfg = ApiConfig::from_env();
    tracing::info!("terminal-api {} (circuits {}) on {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), terminal_api::CIRCUITS_REV, cfg.listen_addr);
    let state = AppState::for_tests(cfg.clone());
    state.limiter.clone().spawn_sweeper();
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(cfg.listen_addr).await?;
    axum::serve(listener, create_router(state).into_make_service_with_connect_info::<std::net::SocketAddr>()).await?;
    Ok(())
}
```

### Task 16: Job store and the pipeline driver

**Files:**
- Create: `api/crates/terminal-api/src/jobs.rs`, `api/crates/terminal-api/tests/pipeline.rs`, `api/crates/terminal-api/tests/common/fake.rs`
- Modify: `api/crates/terminal-api/src/{lib.rs,state.rs,main.rs}`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything in `terminal_core` (Tasks 2–12), `terminal_sandbox::{Runner, RunSpec, Tool, timeout_for, RunOutput}`.
- Produces:
  - `jobs::JobEvent { Stage { stage: StageName, state: StageState }, Console(ConsoleLine), Done }` (serde, `#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]`)
  - `jobs::JobStore` with:
    - `JobStore::new(config: Arc<ApiConfig>, runner: Arc<dyn Runner>) -> Arc<JobStore>`
    - `create(self: &Arc<Self>, req: NewJob) -> ApiResult<String>` — validates, writes `source/`, `job.json`, spawns the pipeline, returns the id
    - `view(&self, id: &str) -> ApiResult<JobView>`
    - `stage_view(&self, id: &str, stage: &str) -> ApiResult<serde_json::Value>` (`compile` | `transpile` | `image`)
    - `file(&self, id: &str, name: &str) -> ApiResult<(Vec<u8>, &'static str)>` (bytes, content type) for `contract.c`, `program.c`, `image.bin`, `program.so`, `runtime.hex`, `abi.json`
    - `subscribe(&self, id: &str) -> Option<tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<JobEvent>>`
    - `dir(&self, id: &str) -> PathBuf`; `touch(&self, id: &str)`
    - `sweep(&self, now: u64) -> usize` (removes expired jobs and, past the disk cap, least recently used ones; returns how many)
  - `AppState::new(config: ApiConfig, runner: Arc<dyn Runner>) -> AppState` with new field `store: Arc<JobStore>`; `AppState::for_tests(config)` keeps working with a runner that always fails (`status 127`).
  - Test helper `tests/common/fake.rs`: `fake_runner() -> Arc<dyn Runner>`, `fixtures_dir() -> PathBuf`, `wait_finished(&JobStore, &str) -> JobView`.

Pipeline rules (from the spec, §4 and §8):
- Each stage sets `running` with `started`, then `done` or `failed` with `ended` and a `StageError`; after a failure the pipeline stops, later stages stay `queued`, and `finished` becomes `true`.
- Sandbox steps hold one permit of `Semaphore::new(config.max_builds)` for their duration.
- CPU work in-process (`analyse`, `transpile`, `image::view`) runs in `spawn_blocking`.
- Every tool's stdout and stderr go to the console (each capped at its last 64 KB).
- A sandbox timeout is `kind: "timeout"`; a nonzero exit is `kind` = the stage name; an `Unsupported` transpiler warning is `kind: "unsupported"`.
- Files written: see the job directory layout at the top of this plan. Also `transpile/op_map.json` (`Vec<OpLine>`) and `build/source_map.json` (`Option<SourceMap>`), so the run endpoints never parse the large views.

Validation (`create`), all `BadRequest` unless noted:
- `solidity`: one to 16 files, every name matches `^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}\.sol$`, `encoding: text`.
- `solana-rust`: a `lib.rs` (text) and optionally a `Cargo.toml` (text); nothing else.
- `solana-elf`: exactly one `program.so` with `encoding: base64`; decoded size ≤ 2 MB (`PayloadTooLarge`).
- Text files ≤ 512 KB in total (`PayloadTooLarge`); `options.stage` ∈ {1, 2}.

```rust
//! A sandbox stand-in that answers each tool step from the checked-in fixtures.
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use terminal_api::jobs::JobStore;
use terminal_core::views::JobView;
use terminal_sandbox::{FakeRunner, RunOutput, RunSpec, Runner, Tool};

pub fn fixtures_dir() -> PathBuf { PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../terminal-core/tests/fixtures") }

fn ok(stdout: &str) -> RunOutput { RunOutput { status: 0, stdout: stdout.into(), stderr: String::new(), timed_out: false } }

pub fn fake_runner() -> Arc<dyn Runner> {
    Arc::new(FakeRunner::new(|spec: &RunSpec| {
        let w = &spec.work;
        match spec.tool {
            Tool::Solc => {
                let input = std::fs::read_to_string(w.join("input.json")).unwrap();
                let out = if input.contains("SYNTAX_ERROR") {
                    serde_json::json!({"errors": [{"severity": "error", "message": "Expected ';' but got identifier", "sourceLocation": {"file": "ERC20.sol", "start": 0, "end": 1}}]})
                } else if input.contains("SLOW") {
                    return RunOutput { status: -1, stdout: String::new(), stderr: String::new(), timed_out: true };
                } else {
                    let hex = std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures_dir().join("erc20.runtime.hex")).unwrap();
                    serde_json::json!({"contracts": {"ERC20.sol": {"ERC20": {"abi": [{"type": "function", "name": "transfer", "inputs": [{"name": "to", "type": "address"}, {"name": "amount", "type": "uint256"}], "outputs": [{"name": "", "type": "bool"}], "stateMutability": "nonpayable"}], "evm": {"bytecode": {"object": "6080"}, "deployedBytecode": {"object": hex.trim()}}}}}})
                };
                std::fs::write(w.join("output.json"), out.to_string()).unwrap();
                ok("")
            }
            Tool::BuildSbf => {
                let src = std::fs::read_to_string(w.join("src/lib.rs")).unwrap();
                if src.contains("compile_error") {
                    return RunOutput { status: 101, stdout: r#"{"reason":"compiler-message","message":{"level":"error","message":"boom","spans":[{"file_name":"src/lib.rs","line_start":1,"column_start":1,"is_primary":true}]}}"#.into(), stderr: "error: could not compile".into(), timed_out: false };
                }
                std::fs::copy(fixtures_dir().join("spl_token.so"), w.join("program.so")).unwrap();
                ok("Finished release")
            }
            Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: false } => {
                let fixture = if w.join("shim/contract.c").exists() { "erc20.image.bin" } else { "spl.image.bin" };
                std::fs::copy(fixtures_dir().join(fixture), w.join("image.bin")).unwrap();
                ok("wrote image.bin")
            }
            Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: true } => RunOutput { status: 66, stdout: String::new(), stderr: "could not add -g to build.rs".into(), timed_out: false },
        }
    }))
}

pub async fn wait_finished(store: &JobStore, id: &str) -> JobView {
    for _ in 0..600 {
        let v = store.view(id).unwrap();
        if v.finished { return v; }
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
    panic!("job {id} did not finish in 30 s");
}
```

The fake build for Solidity returns the ERC-20 image built at chain id 13. The pipeline transpiles at the job's chain id (default 20), so `contract.c` and the image disagree in the fake; the tests below therefore only check the image view's own numbers, never C-to-pc consistency (that is the gated sandbox test's job).

`api/crates/terminal-api/tests/pipeline.rs`:
```rust
mod common;
use common::fake::*;
use std::sync::Arc;
use terminal_api::{jobs::JobStore, ApiConfig};
use terminal_core::views::*;

fn store() -> (Arc<JobStore>, tempfile::TempDir) {
    let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cfg = ApiConfig { data_dir: d.path().into(), ..ApiConfig::default() };
    (JobStore::new(Arc::new(cfg), fake_runner()), d)
}

fn sol(src: &str) -> NewJob {
    NewJob { lang: Lang::Solidity, files: vec![FileIn { name: "ERC20.sol".into(), encoding: "text".into(), content: src.into() }], options: JobOptions::default() }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn solidity_runs_every_stage() {
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let id = s.create(sol("contract ERC20 {}")).unwrap();
    let v = wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    for st in [&v.compile, &v.transpile, &v.build] { assert_eq!(st.status, StageStatus::Done, "{v:?}"); assert!(st.ended >= st.started); }
    let c: CompileView = serde_json::from_value(s.stage_view(&id, "compile").unwrap()).unwrap();
    let CompileView::Evm(c) = c else { panic!() };
    assert_eq!((c.contract.as_str(), c.size_bytes, c.blocks), ("ERC20", 1296, 74));
    let t: TranspileView = serde_json::from_value(s.stage_view(&id, "transpile").unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(t.c_source.contains("CHAINID 20"), "the default chain id reached the transpiler");
    let i: ImageView = serde_json::from_value(s.stage_view(&id, "image").unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(i.words, 11_686);
    assert!(i.source_map.is_none());
    assert!(i.source_map_note.unwrap().contains("-g"));
    assert!(v.console.iter().any(|l| l.stage == StageName::Build && l.text.contains("wrote image.bin")));
    let (bytes, ty) = s.file(&id, "contract.c").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(ty, "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap(), t.c_source);
    assert!(s.file(&id, "../job.json").is_err());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_compile_error_stops_the_pipeline_with_diagnostics() {
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let id = s.create(sol("SYNTAX_ERROR")).unwrap();
    let v = wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    assert_eq!(v.compile.status, StageStatus::Failed);
    let e = v.compile.error.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.kind, "compile");
    assert_eq!((e.diagnostics[0].line, e.diagnostics[0].col), (1, 1));
    assert_eq!(v.transpile.status, StageStatus::Queued);
    assert_eq!(v.build.status, StageStatus::Queued);
    assert!(s.stage_view(&id, "transpile").is_err());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_sandbox_timeout_is_reported_as_such() {
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let id = s.create(sol("SLOW")).unwrap();
    let v = wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    assert_eq!(v.compile.error.unwrap().kind, "timeout");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn solana_elf_skips_the_compiler() {
    use base64::Engine;
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let elf = std::fs::read(fixtures_dir().join("spl_token.so")).unwrap();
    let id = s.create(NewJob { lang: Lang::SolanaElf, files: vec![FileIn { name: "program.so".into(), encoding: "base64".into(), content: base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&elf) }], options: JobOptions::default() }).unwrap();
    let v = wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    assert_eq!(v.build.status, StageStatus::Done, "{v:?}");
    let CompileView::Sbpf(c) = serde_json::from_value(s.stage_view(&id, "compile").unwrap()).unwrap() else { panic!() };
    assert!(c.functions.len() > 50);
    let i: ImageView = serde_json::from_value(s.stage_view(&id, "image").unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(i.words, 65_096);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn solana_rust_errors_are_diagnostics() {
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let id = s.create(NewJob { lang: Lang::SolanaRust, files: vec![FileIn { name: "lib.rs".into(), encoding: "text".into(), content: "compile_error!(\"x\");".into() }], options: JobOptions::default() }).unwrap();
    let v = wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    let e = v.compile.error.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.kind, "compile");
    assert_eq!(e.diagnostics[0].message, "boom");
}

#[test]
fn validation() {
    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().unwrap();
    let _g = rt.enter();
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let bad = |j: NewJob| s.create(j).unwrap_err().to_string();
    assert!(bad(NewJob { lang: Lang::Solidity, files: vec![], options: JobOptions::default() }).contains("bad request"));
    assert!(bad(NewJob { lang: Lang::Solidity, files: vec![FileIn { name: "../x.sol".into(), encoding: "text".into(), content: "".into() }], options: JobOptions::default() }).contains("bad request"));
    assert!(bad(sol(&"x".repeat(512 * 1024 + 1))).contains("too large"));
    assert!(bad(NewJob { lang: Lang::SolanaRust, files: vec![FileIn { name: "main.rs".into(), encoding: "text".into(), content: "".into() }], options: JobOptions::default() }).contains("lib.rs"));
    let mut j = sol("contract A {}"); j.options.stage = 3;
    assert!(bad(j).contains("stage"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn sweep_drops_expired_jobs() {
    let (s, _d) = store();
    let id = s.create(sol("contract ERC20 {}")).unwrap();
    wait_finished(&s, &id).await;
    assert_eq!(s.sweep(terminal_api::ratelimit::unix_now() + 10), 0);
    assert_eq!(s.sweep(terminal_api::ratelimit::unix_now() + 3601), 1);
    assert!(s.view(&id).is_err());
}
```
