- [ ] **Step 3: Write `jobs.rs`**

```rust
//! Jobs on disk and the pipeline that fills them. One directory per job (layout at the top of the
//! plan); `job.json` is rewritten after every change and every change is broadcast for SSE.
use crate::error::{ApiResult, AppError};
use crate::ratelimit::unix_now;
use crate::ApiConfig;
use base64::Engine;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use terminal_core::views::*;
use terminal_core::{evm_analysis, image, sbpf_analysis, stages};
use terminal_sandbox::{timeout_for, RunOutput, RunSpec, Runner, Tool};
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Semaphore};

pub const MAX_TEXT_BYTES: usize = 512 * 1024;
pub const MAX_ELF_BYTES: usize = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
const CONSOLE_KEEP: usize = 64 * 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum JobEvent { Stage { stage: StageName, state: StageState }, Console(ConsoleLine), Done }

pub struct JobStore {
    root: PathBuf,
    config: Arc<ApiConfig>,
    runner: Arc<dyn Runner>,
    builds: Arc<Semaphore>,
    chans: Mutex<HashMap<String, broadcast::Sender<JobEvent>>>,
    access: Mutex<HashMap<String, u64>>,
}

fn queued() -> StageState { StageState { status: StageStatus::Queued, started: None, ended: None, error: None } }
fn stage_err(kind: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> StageError { StageError { kind: kind.into(), message: message.into(), diagnostics: vec![] } }
fn valid_name(n: &str) -> bool { !n.is_empty() && n.len() <= 64 && n.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "_.-".contains(c)) && !n.starts_with('.') }
fn tail(s: &str) -> String { if s.len() <= CONSOLE_KEEP { s.to_string() } else { let mut i = s.len() - CONSOLE_KEEP; while !s.is_char_boundary(i) { i += 1; } s[i..].to_string() } }

impl JobStore {
    pub fn new(config: Arc<ApiConfig>, runner: Arc<dyn Runner>) -> Arc<Self> {
        let root = config.data_dir.join("jobs");
        let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&root);
        Arc::new(Self { root, builds: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(config.max_builds.max(1))), config, runner, chans: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), access: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) })
    }

    pub fn dir(&self, id: &str) -> PathBuf { self.root.join(id) }

    fn check_id(&self, id: &str) -> ApiResult<PathBuf> {
        if id.len() != 32 || !id.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) { return Err(AppError::NotFound(format!("job {id}"))); }
        let d = self.dir(id);
        if !d.join("job.json").exists() { return Err(AppError::NotFound(format!("job {id}"))); }
        Ok(d)
    }

    pub fn touch(&self, id: &str) { self.access.lock().unwrap().insert(id.to_string(), unix_now()); }

    pub fn create(self: &Arc<Self>, req: NewJob) -> ApiResult<String> {
        if !(1..=2).contains(&req.options.stage) { return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("stage must be 1 or 2, not {}", req.options.stage))); }
        let mut text_total = 0usize;
        let mut files: Vec<(String, Vec<u8>)> = Vec::new();
        for f in &req.files {
            if !valid_name(&f.name) { return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("bad file name {:?}", f.name))); }
            let bytes = match f.encoding.as_str() {
                "text" => { text_total += f.content.len(); f.content.clone().into_bytes() }
                "base64" => base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.decode(&f.content).map_err(|e| AppError::BadRequest(format!("{}: {e}", f.name)))?,
                e => return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("encoding {e:?}"))),
            };
            files.push((f.name.clone(), bytes));
        }
        if text_total > MAX_TEXT_BYTES { return Err(AppError::PayloadTooLarge(format!("{text_total} bytes of source; the limit is {MAX_TEXT_BYTES}"))); }
        let names: Vec<&str> = req.files.iter().map(|f| f.name.as_str()).collect();
        match req.lang {
            Lang::Solidity => {
                if files.is_empty() || files.len() > 16 { return Err(AppError::BadRequest("a Solidity job has 1 to 16 .sol files".into())); }
                if let Some(f) = req.files.iter().find(|f| !f.name.ends_with(".sol") || f.encoding != "text") { return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("{}: Solidity jobs take .sol text files", f.name))); }
            }
            Lang::SolanaRust => {
                if !names.contains(&"lib.rs") || names.iter().any(|n| *n != "lib.rs" && *n != "Cargo.toml") || req.files.iter().any(|f| f.encoding != "text") {
                    return Err(AppError::BadRequest("a Solana Rust job is lib.rs and optionally Cargo.toml, as text".into()));
                }
            }
            Lang::SolanaElf => {
                if names != ["program.so"] || req.files[0].encoding != "base64" { return Err(AppError::BadRequest("a Solana ELF job is one base64 file named program.so".into())); }
                if files[0].1.len() > MAX_ELF_BYTES { return Err(AppError::PayloadTooLarge(format!("{} bytes of ELF; the limit is {MAX_ELF_BYTES}", files[0].1.len()))); }
            }
        }
        let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
        let dir = self.dir(&id);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("source"))?;
        for (n, b) in &files { std::fs::write(dir.join("source").join(n), b)?; }
        let view = JobView { id: id.clone(), lang: req.lang, options: req.options, created: unix_now(), compile: queued(), transpile: queued(), build: queued(), console: vec![], finished: false };
        write_json(&dir.join("job.json"), &view)?;
        let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(256);
        self.chans.lock().unwrap().insert(id.clone(), tx);
        self.touch(&id);
        let me = self.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move { Pipeline { store: me, dir, view }.run().await });
        Ok(id)
    }

    pub fn view(&self, id: &str) -> ApiResult<JobView> {
        let d = self.check_id(id)?;
        self.touch(id);
        Ok(serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(d.join("job.json"))?)?)
    }

    pub fn stage_view(&self, id: &str, stage: &str) -> ApiResult<serde_json::Value> {
        let d = self.check_id(id)?;
        let sub = match stage { "compile" => "compile", "transpile" => "transpile", "image" => "build", _ => return Err(AppError::NotFound(stage.into())) };
        let p = d.join(sub).join("view.json");
        if !p.exists() { return Err(AppError::Conflict(format!("the {stage} view is not ready"))); }
        self.touch(id);
        Ok(serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(p)?)?)
    }

    pub fn file(&self, id: &str, name: &str) -> ApiResult<(Vec<u8>, &'static str)> {
        let d = self.check_id(id)?;
        let (rel, ty) = match name {
            "contract.c" => ("transpile/contract.c", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
            "program.c" => ("transpile/program.c", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
            "runtime.hex" => ("compile/runtime.hex", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
            "abi.json" => ("compile/abi.json", "application/json"),
            "program.so" => ("compile/program.so", "application/octet-stream"),
            "image.bin" => ("build/image.bin", "application/octet-stream"),
            _ => return Err(AppError::NotFound(name.into())),
        };
        let p = d.join(rel);
        if !p.exists() { return Err(AppError::NotFound(name.into())); }
        Ok((std::fs::read(p)?, ty))
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self, id: &str) -> Option<broadcast::Receiver<JobEvent>> { self.chans.lock().unwrap().get(id).map(|t| t.subscribe()) }

    fn last_access(&self, id: &str) -> u64 {
        if let Some(t) = self.access.lock().unwrap().get(id) { return *t; }
        std::fs::metadata(self.dir(id).join("job.json")).and_then(|m| m.modified()).ok()
            .and_then(|t| t.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).ok()).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0)
    }

    fn remove(&self, id: &str) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(self.dir(id));
        self.access.lock().unwrap().remove(id);
        self.chans.lock().unwrap().remove(id);
    }

    pub fn sweep(&self, now: u64) -> usize {
        let Ok(rd) = std::fs::read_dir(&self.root) else { return 0 };
        let mut jobs: Vec<(String, u64, u64)> = rd.filter_map(|e| e.ok()).map(|e| {
            let id = e.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
            let size = dir_size(&e.path());
            let at = self.last_access(&id);
            (id, at, size)
        }).collect();
        let mut removed = 0;
        jobs.retain(|(id, at, _)| if now.saturating_sub(*at) > self.config.job_ttl_secs { self.remove(id); removed += 1; false } else { true });
        let mut total: u64 = jobs.iter().map(|j| j.2).sum();
        jobs.sort_by_key(|j| j.1);
        for (id, _, size) in jobs {
            if total <= self.config.data_cap_bytes { break; }
            self.remove(&id); removed += 1; total -= size;
        }
        removed
    }

    pub fn spawn_sweeper(self: Arc<Self>) {
        tokio::spawn(async move { loop { tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(300)).await; let n = self.sweep(unix_now()); if n > 0 { tracing::info!("swept {n} job(s)"); } } });
    }
}

fn dir_size(p: &Path) -> u64 {
    std::fs::read_dir(p).map(|rd| rd.filter_map(|e| e.ok()).map(|e| { let m = e.metadata().ok(); if m.as_ref().map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false) { dir_size(&e.path()) } else { m.map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0) } }).sum()).unwrap_or(0)
}

pub fn write_json<T: Serialize>(p: &Path, v: &T) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let tmp = p.with_extension("tmp");
    std::fs::write(&tmp, serde_json::to_vec(v)?)?;
    std::fs::rename(tmp, p)
}

struct Pipeline { store: Arc<JobStore>, dir: PathBuf, view: JobView }

/// What the compile stage hands the transpile stage.
enum Compiled { Evm(Vec<u8>), Sbpf(sbpf_analysis::Analysis) }
/// What the transpile stage hands the build stage.
struct Transpiled { c_file: &'static str, op_map: Vec<OpLine> }

impl Pipeline {
    fn emit(&self, e: JobEvent) { if let Some(tx) = self.store.chans.lock().unwrap().get(&self.view.id) { let _ = tx.send(e); } }
    fn save(&self) { let _ = write_json(&self.dir.join("job.json"), &self.view); }
    fn state(&mut self, s: StageName) -> &mut StageState { match s { StageName::Compile => &mut self.view.compile, StageName::Transpile => &mut self.view.transpile, StageName::Build => &mut self.view.build } }

    fn set(&mut self, s: StageName, status: StageStatus, error: Option<StageError>) {
        let now = unix_now();
        let st = self.state(s);
        st.status = status;
        if status == StageStatus::Running { st.started = Some(now); } else { st.ended = Some(now); }
        st.error = error;
        let snapshot = st.clone();
        self.save();
        self.emit(JobEvent::Stage { stage: s, state: snapshot });
    }

    fn console(&mut self, stage: StageName, out: &RunOutput) {
        for (stream, text) in [("stdout", &out.stdout), ("stderr", &out.stderr)] {
            if text.trim().is_empty() { continue; }
            let line = ConsoleLine { stage, stream: stream.into(), text: tail(text) };
            self.view.console.push(line.clone());
            self.emit(JobEvent::Console(line));
        }
        self.save();
    }

    async fn sandbox(&mut self, stage: StageName, tool: Tool, work: PathBuf) -> Result<RunOutput, StageError> {
        let _permit = self.store.builds.clone().acquire_owned().await.map_err(|_| stage_err("build", "the build queue is closed"))?;
        let out = self.store.runner.run(RunSpec { tool, work, timeout: timeout_for(&tool) }).await.map_err(|e| stage_err(stage_kind(stage), e.to_string()))?;
        self.console(stage, &out);
        if out.timed_out { return Err(stage_err("timeout", format!("{} took longer than {} s", tool_name(&tool), timeout_for(&tool).as_secs()))); }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn run(mut self) {
        let result = async {
            self.set(StageName::Compile, StageStatus::Running, None);
            let compiled = self.compile().await.map_err(|e| (StageName::Compile, e))?;
            self.set(StageName::Compile, StageStatus::Done, None);
            self.set(StageName::Transpile, StageStatus::Running, None);
            let transpiled = self.transpile(compiled).await.map_err(|e| (StageName::Transpile, e))?;
            self.set(StageName::Transpile, StageStatus::Done, None);
            self.set(StageName::Build, StageStatus::Running, None);
            self.build(transpiled).await.map_err(|e| (StageName::Build, e))?;
            self.set(StageName::Build, StageStatus::Done, None);
            Ok::<(), (StageName, StageError)>(())
        }.await;
        if let Err((stage, e)) = result { self.set(stage, StageStatus::Failed, Some(e)); }
        self.view.finished = true;
        self.save();
        self.emit(JobEvent::Done);
    }

    async fn compile(&mut self) -> Result<Compiled, StageError> {
        let cdir = self.dir.join("compile");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&cdir).map_err(io_err)?;
        let src = self.dir.join("source");
        match self.view.lang {
            Lang::Solidity => {
                let mut files = Vec::new();
                for e in std::fs::read_dir(&src).map_err(io_err)? { let e = e.map_err(io_err)?; files.push((e.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned(), std::fs::read_to_string(e.path()).map_err(io_err)?)); }
                files.sort();
                std::fs::write(cdir.join("input.json"), stages::solc_input(&files).to_string()).map_err(io_err)?;
                let out = self.sandbox(StageName::Compile, Tool::Solc, cdir.clone()).await?;
                let output: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read(cdir.join("output.json")).ok().and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok())
                    .ok_or_else(|| stage_err("compile", format!("solc exited {} without output: {}", out.status, out.stderr.trim())))?;
                let r = stages::parse_solc_output(&output, &files, self.view.options.contract.as_deref())?;
                std::fs::write(cdir.join("runtime.hex"), hex::encode(&r.runtime)).map_err(io_err)?;
                std::fs::write(cdir.join("creation.hex"), &r.creation_hex).map_err(io_err)?;
                write_json(&cdir.join("abi.json"), &r.abi).map_err(io_err)?;
                let code = r.runtime.clone();
                let view = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || evm_analysis::compile_view(&r.runtime, &r.contract, r.contracts, r.creation_hex, r.abi)).await.map_err(join_err)?;
                write_json(&cdir.join("view.json"), &CompileView::Evm(view)).map_err(io_err)?;
                Ok(Compiled::Evm(code))
            }
            Lang::SolanaRust | Lang::SolanaElf => {
                if self.view.lang == Lang::SolanaRust {
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(cdir.join("src")).map_err(io_err)?;
                    for n in ["lib.rs", "Cargo.toml"] { if src.join(n).exists() { std::fs::copy(src.join(n), cdir.join("src").join(n)).map_err(io_err)?; } }
                    let out = self.sandbox(StageName::Compile, Tool::BuildSbf, cdir.clone()).await?;
                    if out.status != 0 || !cdir.join("program.so").exists() {
                        let diagnostics = stages::rustc_diagnostics(&out.stdout);
                        let message = diagnostics.iter().find(|d| d.severity == "error").map(|d| format!("{}:{}:{}: {}", d.file, d.line, d.col, d.message)).unwrap_or_else(|| format!("cargo build-sbf exited {}", out.status));
                        return Err(StageError { kind: "compile".into(), message, diagnostics });
                    }
                } else {
                    std::fs::copy(src.join("program.so"), cdir.join("program.so")).map_err(io_err)?;
                }
                let elf = std::fs::read(cdir.join("program.so")).map_err(io_err)?;
                let a = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || sbpf_analysis::analyse(&elf)).await.map_err(join_err)?.map_err(|e| match e {
                    sbpf_analysis::SbpfError::Unsupported(w) => stage_err("unsupported", w.detail),
                    other => stage_err("compile", other.to_string()),
                })?;
                write_json(&cdir.join("view.json"), &CompileView::Sbpf(a.compile.clone())).map_err(io_err)?;
                Ok(Compiled::Sbpf(a))
            }
        }
    }

    async fn transpile(&mut self, c: Compiled) -> Result<Transpiled, StageError> {
        let tdir = self.dir.join("transpile");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&tdir).map_err(io_err)?;
        let (view, c_text, c_file) = match c {
            Compiled::Evm(code) => {
                let (chain, stage) = (self.view.options.chain_id, self.view.options.stage);
                let (v, c) = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || evm_analysis::transpile(&code, chain, stage)).await.map_err(join_err)?.map_err(|e| match e {
                    evm_analysis::EvmTranspileError::Unsupported(w) => StageError { kind: "unsupported".into(), message: format!("{}: {}", w.detail, w.explanation), diagnostics: vec![] },
                    other => stage_err("transpile", other.to_string()),
                })?;
                (v, c, "contract.c")
            }
            Compiled::Sbpf(a) => (a.transpile, a.program_c, "program.c"),
        };
        std::fs::write(tdir.join(c_file), &c_text).map_err(io_err)?;
        write_json(&tdir.join("op_map.json"), &view.op_map).map_err(io_err)?;
        write_json(&tdir.join("view.json"), &view).map_err(io_err)?;
        Ok(Transpiled { c_file, op_map: view.op_map })
    }

    async fn build(&mut self, t: Transpiled) -> Result<(), StageError> {
        let bdir = self.dir.join("build");
        let shim = bdir.join("shim");
        let c_text = std::fs::read_to_string(self.dir.join("transpile").join(t.c_file)).map_err(io_err)?;
        if t.c_file == "contract.c" { stages::write_evm_shim(&shim, "contract", &c_text) } else { stages::write_sbpf_shim(&shim, "program", &c_text) }.map_err(io_err)?;
        let out = self.sandbox(StageName::Build, Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: false }, bdir.clone()).await?;
        if out.status != 0 || !bdir.join("image.bin").exists() {
            return Err(stage_err("build", format!("rand-guest build exited {}: {}", out.status, out.stderr.lines().last().unwrap_or(""))));
        }
        let dbg = self.sandbox(StageName::Build, Tool::RandGuestBuild { debug: true }, bdir.clone()).await;
        let image = std::fs::read(bdir.join("image.bin")).map_err(io_err)?;
        let debug_line = match dbg {
            Ok(o) if o.status == 0 => {
                let same = std::fs::read(bdir.join("image-dbg.bin")).ok().and_then(|d| Some((rand_guest::pack::split(&image).ok()?.1, rand_guest::pack::split(&d).ok()?.1))).map(|(a, b)| a == b).unwrap_or(false);
                if same { std::fs::read_to_string(bdir.join("debug-line.txt")).ok() } else { None }
            }
            _ => None,
        };
        let (cap, op_map, c_file) = (self.store.config.max_program_words, t.op_map, t.c_file);
        let had_debug = debug_line.is_some();
        let mut view = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || image::view(&image, cap, &op_map, debug_line.as_deref(), c_file)).await.map_err(join_err)?.map_err(|e| stage_err("build", e))?;
        if !had_debug { view.source_map_note = Some("the -g debug build did not run or did not reproduce the production code, so there is no pc-level source map; the opcode-to-C map still works".into()); }
        write_json(&bdir.join("source_map.json"), &view.source_map).map_err(io_err)?;
        write_json(&bdir.join("view.json"), &view).map_err(io_err)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn io_err(e: std::io::Error) -> StageError { stage_err("internal", e.to_string()) }
fn join_err(e: tokio::task::JoinError) -> StageError { stage_err("internal", e.to_string()) }
fn stage_kind(s: StageName) -> &'static str { match s { StageName::Compile => "compile", StageName::Transpile => "transpile", StageName::Build => "build" } }
fn tool_name(t: &Tool) -> &'static str { match t { Tool::Solc => "solc", Tool::BuildSbf => "cargo build-sbf", Tool::RandGuestBuild { .. } => "rand-guest build" } }
```

```rust
use crate::{jobs::JobStore, ratelimit::RateLimiter, ApiConfig};
use std::sync::Arc;
use terminal_sandbox::{FakeRunner, RunOutput, RunSpec, Runner};
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AppState {
    pub config: Arc<ApiConfig>,
    pub limiter: Arc<RateLimiter>,
    pub store: Arc<JobStore>,
    pub runs: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

impl AppState {
    pub fn new(config: ApiConfig, runner: Arc<dyn Runner>) -> Self {
        let config = Arc::new(config);
        Self { store: JobStore::new(config.clone(), runner), runs: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(config.max_runs.max(1))), limiter: Arc::new(RateLimiter::new()), config }
    }
    /// For router tests that never reach the sandbox: every tool step fails with status 127.
    pub fn for_tests(config: ApiConfig) -> Self {
        Self::new(config, Arc::new(FakeRunner::new(|_: &RunSpec| RunOutput { status: 127, stdout: String::new(), stderr: "no sandbox in tests".into(), timed_out: false })))
    }
}
```

- Modify: `api/crates/terminal-api/src/{handlers/mod.rs,routes.rs}`, `api/crates/terminal-api/tests/common/{mod.rs,fake.rs}`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `JobStore` (Task 16).
- Produces routes under `/api/v1`:
  - `POST /jobs` body `NewJob` → `201 {"id": "<32 hex>"}`
  - `GET /jobs/:id` → `JobView`
  - `GET /jobs/:id/events` → `text/event-stream`: first `event: job` (the `JobView`), then `event: stage` / `event: console` (the `JobEvent` JSON), and finally `event: done`; the stream ends after `done`.
  - `GET /jobs/:id/compile`, `/transpile`, `/image` → the stage views (`409` while not ready)
  - `GET /jobs/:id/files/:name` → raw bytes with the content type from `JobStore::file`
  - Test helper `common::test_app(runner) -> (Router, AppState, TempDir)`; `fake::slow_runner()` (the fake runner, with solc sleeping 400 ms).

```rust
pub fn test_app(runner: std::sync::Arc<dyn terminal_sandbox::Runner>) -> (Router, terminal_api::state::AppState, tempfile::TempDir) {
    let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cfg = terminal_api::ApiConfig { data_dir: d.path().into(), samples_dir: std::path::PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../../samples"), ..Default::default() };
    let state = terminal_api::state::AppState::new(cfg, runner);
    (terminal_api::routes::create_router(state.clone()), state, d)
}

pub async fn wait_job(app: &Router, id: &str) -> Value {
    for _ in 0..600 {
        let (_, _, v) = call(app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}"), None)).await;
        if v["finished"] == true { return v; }
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
    panic!("job {id} did not finish");
}

pub fn erc20_job() -> Value {
    serde_json::json!({"lang": "solidity", "files": [{"name": "ERC20.sol", "content": "contract ERC20 {}"}]})
}
```

Append to `tests/common/fake.rs`:
```rust
pub fn slow_runner() -> Arc<dyn Runner> {
    let inner = fake_runner();
    Arc::new(FakeRunner::new(move |spec: &RunSpec| {
        if spec.tool == Tool::Solc { std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(400)); }
        futures_executor_block_on(inner.run(spec.clone())).unwrap()
    }))
}

fn futures_executor_block_on<F: std::future::Future>(f: F) -> F::Output {
    tokio::task::block_in_place(|| tokio::runtime::Handle::current().block_on(f))
}
```

```rust
mod common;
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use common::{fake::*, *};
use http_body_util::BodyExt;
use tower::ServiceExt;

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn submit_poll_and_read_every_view() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let (st, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CREATED, "{v}");
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
    let job = wait_job(&app, &id).await;
    assert_eq!(job["build"]["status"], "done", "{job}");
    let (st, _, c) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/compile"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::OK);
    assert_eq!(c["lang"], "evm");
    assert_eq!(c["blocks"], 74);
    let (_, _, t) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/transpile"), None)).await;
    assert!(t["op_map"].as_array().unwrap().len() > 700);
    let (_, _, i) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/image"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(i["words"], 11_686);
    let res = app.clone().oneshot(json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/files/image.bin"), None)).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(res.headers()["content-type"], "application/octet-stream");
    assert_eq!(res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes().len(), 46_760);
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn views_are_409_until_ready_and_unknown_jobs_404() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(slow_runner());
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap();
    let (st, _, e) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/image"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CONFLICT, "{e}");
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{}", "0".repeat(32)), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/jobs/..%2F..%2Fetc", None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn events_stream_stage_changes_and_ends_with_done() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(slow_runner());
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap();
    let res = app.clone().oneshot(json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/events"), None)).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(res.headers()["content-type"], "text/event-stream");
    let body = tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), res.into_body().collect()).await.unwrap().unwrap().to_bytes();
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&body);
    assert!(text.starts_with("event: job\n"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("event: stage\n"));
    assert!(text.contains("\"status\":\"done\""));
    assert!(text.trim_end().ends_with("event: done\ndata: {}"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn events_on_a_finished_job_are_snapshot_then_done() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap();
    wait_job(&app, id).await;
    let res = app.clone().oneshot(json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/events"), None)).await.unwrap();
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes()).into_owned();
    assert_eq!(text.matches("event: ").count(), 2, "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn bad_jobs_and_the_job_rate_limit() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let (st, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(serde_json::json!({"lang": "solidity", "files": []})))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, "{v}");
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(serde_json::json!({"lang": "cobol", "files": []})))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);
    let huge = serde_json::json!({"lang": "solidity", "files": [{"name": "A.sol", "content": "x".repeat(5 << 20)}]});
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(huge))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE);
    // jobs_rpm defaults to 10: the eleventh submission in a minute from one address is refused.
    let mut last = StatusCode::OK;
    for _ in 0..11 { last = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await.0; }
    assert_eq!(last, StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS);
}
```

`test_app` uses `trust_proxy: false` by default, so every request has the same `unknown` client and shares one bucket; that is what the rate-limit test relies on. The three earlier submissions in that test (two refused with 4xx before reaching the handler, one 413) still count, because the limiter runs before the body is read; adjust the loop count to `8` if the assertion trips on the tenth rather than the eleventh request, and state the arithmetic in a comment.

`src/handlers/jobs.rs`:
```rust
use crate::{error::ApiResult, jobs::JobEvent, state::AppState};
use axum::{extract::{Path, State}, http::{header, StatusCode}, response::{sse::{Event, KeepAlive, Sse}, IntoResponse, Response}, Json};
use futures_util::stream::{self, Stream, StreamExt};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::convert::Infallible;
use terminal_core::views::{JobView, NewJob};
use tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError;

pub async fn create(State(s): State<AppState>, Json(req): Json<NewJob>) -> ApiResult<(StatusCode, Json<Value>)> {
    let id = s.store.create(req)?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(json!({ "id": id }))))
}

pub async fn get(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<JobView>> { Ok(Json(s.store.view(&id)?)) }

pub async fn compile(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<Value>> { Ok(Json(s.store.stage_view(&id, "compile")?)) }
pub async fn transpile(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<Value>> { Ok(Json(s.store.stage_view(&id, "transpile")?)) }
pub async fn image(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<Value>> { Ok(Json(s.store.stage_view(&id, "image")?)) }

pub async fn file(State(s): State<AppState>, Path((id, name)): Path<(String, String)>) -> ApiResult<Response> {
    let (bytes, ty) = s.store.file(&id, &name)?;
    Ok(([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, ty), (header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION, "inline")], bytes).into_response())
}

fn event_name(e: &JobEvent) -> &'static str { match e { JobEvent::Stage { .. } => "stage", JobEvent::Console(_) => "console", JobEvent::Done => "done" } }

pub async fn events(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Sse<impl Stream<Item = Result<Event, Infallible>>>> {
    // Subscribe before reading the snapshot, so no change falls between the two.
    let rx = s.store.subscribe(&id);
    let view = s.store.view(&id)?;
    let head = stream::once(async move { Ok(Event::default().event("job").json_data(&view).unwrap()) });
    let finished = view_finished(&s, &id);
    let tail = stream::unfold(if finished { None } else { rx }, |rx| async move {
        let mut rx = rx?;
        loop {
            match rx.recv().await {
                Ok(JobEvent::Done) | Err(RecvError::Closed) => return Some((Ok(Event::default().event("done").data("{}")), None)),
                Ok(e) => return Some((Ok(Event::default().event(event_name(&e)).json_data(&e).unwrap()), Some(rx))),
                Err(RecvError::Lagged(_)) => continue,
            }
        }
    });
    let done = stream::once(async { Ok(Event::default().event("done").data("{}")) });
    let body = if finished { head.chain(done).boxed() } else { head.chain(tail).boxed() };
    Ok(Sse::new(body).keep_alive(KeepAlive::default()))
}

fn view_finished(s: &AppState, id: &str) -> bool { s.store.view(id).map(|v| v.finished).unwrap_or(true) }
```

`Sse` sets `content-type: text/event-stream`; nginx must not buffer it (`proxy_buffering off` for `/api/v1/jobs/`, Task 27). `CompressionLayer` does not compress `text/event-stream` by default; if the test sees a gzip body, exclude the events route from compression with `CompressionLayer::new().compress_when(...)` using `tower_http::compression::predicate::NotForContentType::const_new("text/event-stream")`.

### Task 18: Run routes (execute, page the trace, read state)

**Files:**
- Create: `api/crates/terminal-api/src/runs.rs`, `api/crates/terminal-api/src/handlers/runs.rs`, `api/crates/terminal-api/tests/runs.rs`
- Modify: `api/crates/terminal-api/src/{lib.rs,handlers/mod.rs,routes.rs}`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `trace::{write, Trace, summarize, trapped}`, `evm_call::build`, `sbpf_call::build`, `image::load`, `rv_disasm::text`, `source_map::src_pc_for_line`, `views::{RunRequest, CallForm, RunSummary, CyclePage, StateView, MemoryView, SourceMap, OpLine}`.
- Produces:
  - Runs live at `$DATA/jobs/<job>/runs/<rid>/`; `$DATA/runs/<rid>` is a one-line file holding the job id (so `/runs/:rid` resolves, and removing a job removes its runs).
  - `runs::MAX_CYCLES: usize = (1 << 20) - 1`, `runs::WALL_CLOCK: Duration = 60 s`.
  - `runs::create(state: &AppState, job: &str, req: RunRequest) -> ApiResult<RunSummary>`
  - `runs::open(state: &AppState, rid: &str) -> ApiResult<(PathBuf /*run dir*/, PathBuf /*job dir*/)>`
  - Routes: `POST /jobs/:id/runs` → `201 RunSummary`; `GET /runs/:rid` → `RunSummary`; `GET /runs/:rid/cycles?from=&count=` → `CyclePage`; `GET /runs/:rid/state?at=` → `StateView`; `GET /runs/:rid/memory?at=&addr=&words=` → `MemoryView`; `GET /runs/:rid/syscalls` → `Vec<SyscallView>`.
  - Errors: build not done → `409`; form for the wrong language or a call the encoder refuses → `400` with the encoder's message; all run slots busy → `503`; a run longer than 60 s → `503`; a trapped run's trace endpoints → `409 "the run trapped at …; there is no trace"`; `at`/`count`/`words` out of range → `400`.

`at` semantics (spec §4.4 and Review Focus 5): `state?at=N` is valid for `0 ≤ N < cycles` and shows registers *before* cycle N plus cycle N's record; `memory?at=N` is valid for `0 ≤ N ≤ cycles`.

```rust
mod common;
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use common::{fake::*, *};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use terminal_core::{evm_call, sbpf_call};

async fn finished_erc20(app: &axum::Router) -> String {
    let (_, _, v) = call(app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
    assert_eq!(wait_job(app, &id).await["build"]["status"], "done");
    id
}

fn evm_run(form: &terminal_core::views::EvmCallForm) -> Value { json!({ "call": { "lang": "evm", "function": form.function, "args": form.args, "caller": form.caller, "storage": form.storage } }) }

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn erc20_transfer_runs_and_matches_the_host_interpreter() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let id = finished_erc20(&app).await;
    let form = evm_call::erc20_transfer_form();
    let (st, _, sum) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(evm_run(&form)))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CREATED, "{sum}");
    let code = hex::decode(std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures_dir().join("erc20.runtime.hex")).unwrap().trim()).unwrap();
    let (call_, _) = evm_call::build(&code, &form).unwrap();
    let (want, _, _) = call_.expected();
    assert_eq!(sum["outputs"], json!(want), "the RV32 image and the EVM interpreter agree");
    assert_eq!(sum["halted"], true);
    assert_eq!(sum["tier"], 18);
    let total = sum["cycles"].as_u64().unwrap();
    assert!(total > 60_000);
    let rid = sum["rid"].as_str().unwrap();

    let (_, _, again) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(again["cycles"], sum["cycles"]);
    let (_, _, page) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/cycles?from=0&count=10"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(page["cycles"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 10);
    assert_eq!(page["total"], total);
    let (st, _, s0) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/state?at=0"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::OK);
    assert!(s0["regs"].as_array().unwrap().iter().all(|r| r == 0));
    assert!(!s0["text"].as_str().unwrap().is_empty());
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/state?at={}", total - 1), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::OK);
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/state?at={total}"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    let (st, _, m) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/memory?at={total}&addr=0&words=16"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::OK);
    assert_eq!(m["words"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 16);
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/memory?at=0&addr=0&words=257"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/cycles?from=0&count=4097"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    let (_, _, sys) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/syscalls"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(sys.as_array().unwrap().last().unwrap()["kind"], "halt");
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn a_trap_is_a_summary_without_a_trace() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let id = finished_erc20(&app).await;
    let mut body = evm_run(&evm_call::erc20_transfer_form());
    body["max_cycles"] = json!(1000);
    let (st, _, sum) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(body))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CREATED);
    assert_eq!(sum["trap"], "OutOfCycles(1000)");
    assert_eq!(sum["trap_clk"], 1000);
    let rid = sum["rid"].as_str().unwrap();
    let (st, _, e) = call(&app, json_req("GET", &format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/cycles?from=0&count=1"), None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CONFLICT);
    assert!(e["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("trapped"));
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn bad_calls_are_400_and_unfinished_jobs_409() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(slow_runner());
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(erc20_job()))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(evm_run(&evm_call::erc20_transfer_form())))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::CONFLICT);
    wait_job(&app, &id).await;
    let bad = json!({"call": {"lang": "evm", "function": "f(string)", "args": ["x"], "caller": "0x01"}});
    let (st, _, e) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(bad))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    assert!(e["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("string"));
    let sbpf = json!({"call": {"lang": "sbpf", "program_id": "11111111111111111111111111111111", "accounts": [], "data": "0x"}});
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(sbpf))).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/runs/nope", None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);
}
```

`src/runs.rs`:
```rust
//! Runs: build the input vectors from the call form, emulate, fold the trace to disk.
use crate::error::{ApiResult, AppError};
use crate::jobs::write_json;
use crate::state::AppState;
use rand_zkvm::emulator::execute;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Duration;
use terminal_core::views::*;
use terminal_core::{evm_call, image, sbpf_call, trace};

pub const MAX_CYCLES: usize = (1 << 20) - 1;
pub const WALL_CLOCK: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct RunMeta { job: String, trapped: Option<String> }

fn rid_ok(rid: &str) -> bool { rid.len() == 32 && rid.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) }

pub async fn create(s: &AppState, job: &str, req: RunRequest) -> ApiResult<RunSummary> {
    let view = s.store.view(job)?;
    if view.build.status != StageStatus::Done { return Err(AppError::Conflict("the image is not built yet".into())); }
    let jdir = s.store.dir(job);
    let program = image::load(&std::fs::read(jdir.join("build/image.bin"))?).map_err(AppError::Internal)?;
    let (inputs, public, discovery) = match (view.lang, req.call) {
        (Lang::Solidity, CallForm::Evm(form)) => {
            let code = hex::decode(std::fs::read_to_string(jdir.join("compile/runtime.hex"))?.trim()).map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;
            let (c, steps) = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || evm_call::build(&code, &form)).await.map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))?.map_err(AppError::BadRequest)?;
            (c.input_words(), vec![], steps)
        }
        (Lang::SolanaRust | Lang::SolanaElf, CallForm::Sbpf(form)) => {
            let elf = std::fs::read(jdir.join("compile/program.so"))?;
            let c = sbpf_call::build(&elf, &form).map_err(AppError::BadRequest)?;
            (c.input_words(), c.public_words(), vec![])
        }
        (lang, _) => return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("this job is {lang:?}; send the matching call form"))),
    };
    let max = req.max_cycles.unwrap_or(MAX_CYCLES).min(MAX_CYCLES);
    let permit = s.runs.clone().try_acquire_owned().map_err(|_| AppError::Unavailable("every emulator slot is busy; try again shortly".into()))?;
    let rid = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
    let rdir = jdir.join("runs").join(&rid);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&rdir)?;
    let (n_in, n_pub) = (inputs.len(), public.len());
    let rd = rdir.clone();
    let rid2 = rid.clone();
    let work = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let _permit = permit;
        match execute(&program, &inputs, &public, max) {
            Ok(exec) => { trace::write(&rd, &exec)?; Ok::<_, std::io::Error>((trace::summarize(&rid2, &program, n_in, n_pub, &exec, discovery), None)) }
            Err(e) => Ok((trace::trapped(&rid2, &e, n_in, n_pub, discovery), Some(format!("{e:?}")))),
        }
    });
    let (summary, trapped) = tokio::time::timeout(WALL_CLOCK, work).await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Unavailable("the run took longer than 60 s".into()))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))??;
    write_json(&rdir.join("summary.json"), &summary)?;
    write_json(&rdir.join("run.json"), &RunMeta { job: job.to_string(), trapped })?;
    let index = s.config.data_dir.join("runs");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&index)?;
    std::fs::write(index.join(&rid), job)?;
    Ok(summary)
}

pub fn open(s: &AppState, rid: &str) -> ApiResult<(PathBuf, PathBuf)> {
    if !rid_ok(rid) { return Err(AppError::NotFound(format!("run {rid}"))); }
    let index = s.config.data_dir.join("runs").join(rid);
    let job = std::fs::read_to_string(&index).map_err(|_| AppError::NotFound(format!("run {rid}")))?;
    let jdir = s.store.dir(job.trim());
    let rdir = jdir.join("runs").join(rid);
    if !rdir.join("summary.json").exists() { let _ = std::fs::remove_file(index); return Err(AppError::NotFound(format!("run {rid}"))); }
    s.store.touch(job.trim());
    Ok((rdir, jdir))
}

pub fn trace_of(rdir: &std::path::Path) -> ApiResult<trace::Trace> {
    let meta: RunMeta = serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(rdir.join("run.json"))?)?;
    if let Some(t) = meta.trapped { return Err(AppError::Conflict(format!("the run trapped at {t}; there is no trace"))); }
    Ok(trace::Trace::open(rdir)?)
}

/// 400 for `InvalidInput` from the trace reader (out-of-range `at`, oversized page or window).
pub fn trace_err(e: std::io::Error) -> AppError {
    if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput { AppError::BadRequest(e.to_string()) } else { AppError::Internal(e.to_string()) }
}
```

`src/handlers/runs.rs`:
```rust
use crate::{error::{ApiResult, AppError}, runs, state::AppState};
use axum::{extract::{Path, Query, State}, http::StatusCode, Json};
use rand_zkvm::isa::Instr;
use serde::Deserialize;
use terminal_core::source_map::src_pc_for_line;
use terminal_core::views::*;
use terminal_core::{rv_disasm, trace::Trace};

pub async fn create(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>, Json(req): Json<RunRequest>) -> ApiResult<(StatusCode, Json<RunSummary>)> {
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(runs::create(&s, &id, req).await?)))
}

pub async fn summary(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(rid): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<RunSummary>> {
    let (rdir, _) = runs::open(&s, &rid)?;
    Ok(Json(serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(rdir.join("summary.json"))?)?))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)] pub struct Page { #[serde(default)] from: u32, #[serde(default = "page")] count: u32 }
fn page() -> u32 { 1024 }

pub async fn cycles(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(rid): Path<String>, Query(q): Query<Page>) -> ApiResult<Json<CyclePage>> {
    let (rdir, _) = runs::open(&s, &rid)?;
    let t = runs::trace_of(&rdir)?;
    Ok(Json(CyclePage { from: q.from, cycles: t.cycles(q.from, q.count).map_err(runs::trace_err)?, total: t.total }))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)] pub struct At { at: u32 }

fn c_line_for(map: &Option<SourceMap>, pc: u32) -> Option<usize> {
    let rows = &map.as_ref()?.pc_to_c;
    let i = rows.partition_point(|r| r.0 <= pc);
    (i > 0).then(|| rows[i - 1].1)
}

pub async fn state(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(rid): Path<String>, Query(q): Query<At>) -> ApiResult<Json<StateView>> {
    let (rdir, jdir) = runs::open(&s, &rid)?;
    let t: Trace = runs::trace_of(&rdir)?;
    if q.at >= t.total { return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!("at {} is past the last cycle ({})", q.at, t.total.saturating_sub(1)))); }
    let cycle = t.cycle(q.at).map_err(runs::trace_err)?;
    let regs = t.regs_at(q.at).map_err(runs::trace_err)?;
    let map: Option<SourceMap> = std::fs::read(jdir.join("build/source_map.json")).ok().and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).flatten();
    let op_map: Vec<OpLine> = std::fs::read(jdir.join("transpile/op_map.json")).ok().and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
    let c_line = c_line_for(&map, cycle.pc);
    let text = Instr::decode(cycle.word).map(|i| rv_disasm::text(&i)).unwrap_or_else(|_| format!(".word {:#010x}", cycle.word));
    Ok(Json(StateView { clk: q.at, regs, cycle, text, c_line, src_pc: c_line.and_then(|l| src_pc_for_line(&op_map, l)) }))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)] pub struct Mem { at: u32, addr: u32, #[serde(default = "words")] words: u32 }
fn words() -> u32 { 64 }

pub async fn memory(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(rid): Path<String>, Query(q): Query<Mem>) -> ApiResult<Json<MemoryView>> {
    let (rdir, _) = runs::open(&s, &rid)?;
    let t = runs::trace_of(&rdir)?;
    Ok(Json(MemoryView { clk: q.at, addr: q.addr, words: t.memory_at(q.at, q.addr, q.words).map_err(runs::trace_err)? }))
}

pub async fn syscalls(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(rid): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<Vec<SyscallView>>> {
    let (rdir, _) = runs::open(&s, &rid)?;
    Ok(Json(runs::trace_of(&rdir)?.syscalls().map_err(runs::trace_err)?))
}
```

```solidity
pragma solidity ^0.8.20;

/// The smallest stateful contract: one storage slot, one event.
contract Counter {
    uint256 public count;

    event Incremented(uint256 by, uint256 count);

    function increment(uint256 by) external {
        count += by;
        emit Incremented(by, count);
    }
}
```

The SPL `.so` is uploaded by the web app as a base64 `program.so`; the sample loader serves it under its file name with `encoding: base64`, and the web app renames it to `program.so` when it submits (Task 21).

```rust
mod common;
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use common::{fake::*, *};
use terminal_core::views::{CallForm, SampleView};

#[tokio::test]
async fn samples_list_and_load() {
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let (st, _, list) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/samples", None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::OK);
    let ids: Vec<&str> = list.as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|m| m["id"].as_str().unwrap()).collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, ["counter", "erc20", "solana-counter", "spl-token"]);
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/samples/erc20", None)).await;
    let s: SampleView = serde_json::from_value(v).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(s.files[0].content, std::fs::read_to_string(fixtures_dir().join("ERC20.sol")).unwrap());
    assert!(matches!(s.meta.default_call, Some(CallForm::Evm(_))));
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/samples/spl-token", None)).await;
    let s: SampleView = serde_json::from_value(v).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(s.files[0].encoding, "base64");
    assert!(matches!(s.meta.default_call, Some(CallForm::Sbpf(_))));
    let (st, _, _) = call(&app, json_req("GET", "/api/v1/samples/nope", None)).await;
    assert_eq!(st, StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);
}

#[test]
fn every_default_call_is_well_formed() {
    let dir = std::path::PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../../samples");
    for s in terminal_api::samples::load(&dir).unwrap() {
        match s.meta.default_call {
            Some(CallForm::Sbpf(f)) => {
                terminal_core::sbpf_call::parse_key(&f.program_id).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", s.meta.id));
                for a in &f.accounts { terminal_core::sbpf_call::parse_key(&a.key).unwrap(); terminal_core::sbpf_call::parse_key(&a.owner).unwrap(); }
            }
            Some(CallForm::Evm(f)) => { terminal_core::evm_call::encode_calldata(&f.function, &f.args).unwrap(); }
            None => panic!("{} has no default call", s.meta.id),
        }
    }
}
```

`src/samples.rs`:
```rust
//! The bundled samples, loaded once at start-up from `TERMINAL_SAMPLES`.
use base64::Engine;
use std::path::Path;
use terminal_core::views::{CallForm, SampleFile, SampleMeta, SampleView};
use terminal_core::{evm_call, sbpf_call};

pub fn load(dir: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<SampleView>> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for e in std::fs::read_dir(dir)? {
        let d = e?.path();
        let Ok(meta_bytes) = std::fs::read(d.join("meta.json")) else { continue };
        let mut meta: SampleMeta = serde_json::from_slice(&meta_bytes)?;
        meta.default_call = match meta.id.as_str() {
            "erc20" => Some(CallForm::Evm(evm_call::erc20_transfer_form())),
            "spl-token" => Some(CallForm::Sbpf(sbpf_call::spl_transfer_form(250))),
            _ => meta.default_call,
        };
        let files = meta.files.iter().map(|n| {
            let b = std::fs::read(d.join(n))?;
            Ok(match String::from_utf8(b.clone()) {
                Ok(t) if !n.ends_with(".so") => SampleFile { name: n.clone(), encoding: "text".into(), content: t },
                _ => SampleFile { name: n.clone(), encoding: "base64".into(), content: base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&b) },
            })
        }).collect::<anyhow::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
        out.push(SampleView { meta, files });
    }
    out.sort_by(|a, b| a.meta.id.cmp(&b.meta.id));
    Ok(out)
}
```

`src/handlers/samples.rs`:
```rust
use crate::{error::{ApiResult, AppError}, state::AppState};
use axum::{extract::{Path, State}, Json};
use terminal_core::views::{SampleMeta, SampleView};

pub async fn list(State(s): State<AppState>) -> Json<Vec<SampleMeta>> { Json(s.samples.iter().map(|x| x.meta.clone()).collect()) }

pub async fn get(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<SampleView>> {
    s.samples.iter().find(|x| x.meta.id == id).cloned().map(Json).ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound(format!("sample {id}")))
}
```

In `state.rs`, add `pub samples: Arc<Vec<SampleView>>` and set it in `AppState::new` with `Arc::new(crate::samples::load(&config.samples_dir).unwrap_or_else(|e| { tracing::warn!("samples: {e}"); vec![] }))`. Routes: `.route("/samples", get(handlers::samples::list)).route("/samples/:id", get(handlers::samples::get))`. Add `pub mod samples;` to `lib.rs` and `handlers/mod.rs`.

### Task 20: Web scaffold, theme, types and the API client

**Files:**
- Create: `web/{package.json,next.config.mjs,tsconfig.json,postcss.config.mjs,vitest.config.ts,next-env.d.ts}`, `web/app/{layout.tsx,page.tsx,globals.css}`, `web/app/fonts/{JetBrainsMono-Variable.woff2,JetBrainsMono-OFL.txt,SOURCES.txt}`, `web/lib/{types.ts,api.ts,format.ts}`, `web/test/{api.test.ts,format.test.ts}`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `web/lib/types.ts`: TypeScript mirrors of every type in `views.rs` (same field names, snake_case, same tags).
  - `web/lib/api.ts`: `class ApiError extends Error { status: number; code: string }`; `api.samples()`, `api.sample(id)`, `api.createJob(req: NewJob): Promise<string>`, `api.job(id)`, `api.compile(id)`, `api.transpile(id)`, `api.image(id)`, `api.fileUrl(id, name)`, `api.createRun(id, req: RunRequest)`, `api.run(rid)`, `api.cycles(rid, from, count)`, `api.state(rid, at)`, `api.memory(rid, at, addr, words)`, `api.syscalls(rid)`, `subscribeJob(id, onEvent: (e: JobStreamEvent) => void): () => void`.
  - `web/lib/format.ts`: `hex32(n)`, `hexAddr(n)`, `groupDigits(n)` (`66 235`), `short(hc, n = 8)`, `byteAddr(wordAddr)`.
- The browser always calls the same origin (`/api/v1/...`); in development `next.config.mjs` rewrites `/api/:path*` to `TERMINAL_API_URL` (default `http://127.0.0.1:8080`), in production nginx routes `/api/` to the Rust service directly.

```css
@import "tailwindcss";

/* Rand Terminal — the wallet's ink/bone palette (clients/design/tokens.json) with
   randprotocol.org's cobalt as the one accent. Dark by default; light under data-theme="light"
   or a light OS when data-theme="system". Stage colours are the only other hues, and each is
   paired with a label, never colour alone. */
:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --bg: #0E1220; --bg-soft: #121726; --surface: #161C2C; --surface-2: #1E2538;
  --border: #2B3350; --border-soft: #20283D;
  --text: #ECE9E2; --text-soft: #ABAFBF; --text-mute: #8C91A5; --text-strong: #FFFDF8;
  --accent: #5B8DEF; --accent-2: #8FB0FF; --on-accent: #0E1220;
  --positive: #3DDC97; --negative: #FF7A59; --warning: #F5C451;
  --hl: rgba(91, 141, 239, .16);
  --r-sm: 8px; --r-md: 12px; --r-lg: 16px;
  --font: var(--font-inter, "Inter"), ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --mono: var(--font-jetbrains, "JetBrains Mono"), ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;
}
:root[data-theme="light"] {
  color-scheme: light;
  --bg: #F2F3F7; --bg-soft: #E9EBF1; --surface: #FFFFFF; --surface-2: #F1F2F6;
  --border: #D9DCE6; --border-soft: #E6E8EF;
  --text: #121521; --text-soft: #474C5E; --text-mute: #62677A; --text-strong: #07090F;
  --accent: #2F5FCC; --accent-2: #244CA3; --on-accent: #FFFFFF;
  --positive: #0B7A51; --negative: #C2410C; --warning: #8F6200;
  --hl: rgba(47, 95, 204, .12);
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) {
  :root[data-theme="system"] {
    color-scheme: light;
    --bg: #F2F3F7; --bg-soft: #E9EBF1; --surface: #FFFFFF; --surface-2: #F1F2F6;
    --border: #D9DCE6; --border-soft: #E6E8EF;
    --text: #121521; --text-soft: #474C5E; --text-mute: #62677A; --text-strong: #07090F;
    --accent: #2F5FCC; --accent-2: #244CA3; --on-accent: #FFFFFF;
    --positive: #0B7A51; --negative: #C2410C; --warning: #8F6200;
    --hl: rgba(47, 95, 204, .12);
  }
}
@theme inline {
  --color-bg: var(--bg); --color-bg-soft: var(--bg-soft); --color-surface: var(--surface); --color-surface-2: var(--surface-2);
  --color-border: var(--border); --color-border-soft: var(--border-soft);
  --color-text: var(--text); --color-text-soft: var(--text-soft); --color-text-mute: var(--text-mute); --color-text-strong: var(--text-strong);
  --color-accent: var(--accent); --color-accent-2: var(--accent-2); --color-on-accent: var(--on-accent);
  --color-positive: var(--positive); --color-negative: var(--negative); --color-warning: var(--warning); --color-hl: var(--hl);
  --font-sans: var(--font); --font-mono: var(--mono);
  --radius-sm: var(--r-sm); --radius-md: var(--r-md); --radius-lg: var(--r-lg);
}
@layer base {
  html, body { height: 100%; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font-family: var(--font); font-size: 14px; }
  code, pre, .mono { font-family: var(--mono); font-variant-ligatures: none; }
  :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 2px; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: .01ms !important; transition-duration: .01ms !important; } }
```

```tsx
import './globals.css';
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from 'next';
import { Inter } from 'next/font/google';
import localFont from 'next/font/local';
import Script from 'next/script';

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ['latin'], display: 'swap', variable: '--font-inter' });
const mono = localFont({ src: './fonts/JetBrainsMono-Variable.woff2', weight: '100 800', display: 'swap', variable: '--font-jetbrains' });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Rand Terminal',
  description: 'Compile Solidity or Solana programs, transpile them to RV32IM, and step through them on the Rand zkVM.',
  metadataBase: new URL('https://randterminal.org'),
};
export const viewport: Viewport = { themeColor: [{ media: '(prefers-color-scheme: light)', color: '#F2F3F7' }, { media: '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)', color: '#0E1220' }] };

const THEME = `(function(){var t='system';try{var s=localStorage.getItem('randterminal.theme');if(s==='light'||s==='dark')t=s}catch(e){}document.documentElement.dataset.theme=t})()`;

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" data-theme="system" suppressHydrationWarning className={`${inter.variable} ${mono.variable}`}>
      <body>
        <Script id="theme" strategy="beforeInteractive">{THEME}</Script>
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

```ts
});
```

`web/lib/types.ts` — mirrors `api/crates/terminal-core/src/views.rs`; keep the two in step:
```ts
export type Lang = 'solidity' | 'solana-rust' | 'solana-elf';
export type StageStatus = 'queued' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed';
export type StageName = 'compile' | 'transpile' | 'build';
export interface JobOptions { contract?: string | null; chain_id: number; stage: number }
export interface Diagnostic { file: string; line: number; col: number; severity: string; message: string }
export interface StageError { kind: string; message: string; diagnostics: Diagnostic[] }
export interface StageState { status: StageStatus; started?: number | null; ended?: number | null; error?: StageError | null }
export interface ConsoleLine { stage: StageName; stream: string; text: string }
export interface JobView { id: string; lang: Lang; options: JobOptions; created: number; compile: StageState; transpile: StageState; build: StageState; console: ConsoleLine[]; finished: boolean }

export interface EvmOpcode { pc: number; opcode: number; mnemonic: string; push_hex?: string | null; static_gas: number; jumpdest: boolean; block: number }
export interface EvmCompileView { lang: 'evm'; contract: string; contracts: string[]; creation_hex: string; runtime_hex: string; size_bytes: number; abi: AbiItem[]; opcodes: EvmOpcode[]; blocks: number; jumpdests: number }
export interface SbpfInsnView { pc: number; text: string; opc: number }
export interface SbpfBlockView { start: number; end: number; term: string; insns: SbpfInsnView[] }
export interface SbpfFunctionView { entry: number; blocks: SbpfBlockView[] }
export interface SbpfSyscallView { hash: number; name: string; count: number; supported: boolean }
export interface SbpfCompileView { lang: 'sbpf'; elf_bytes: number; text_bytes: number; rodata_bytes: number; entry_pc: number; functions: SbpfFunctionView[]; instructions: number; syscalls: SbpfSyscallView[]; warnings: WarningView[] }
export type CompileView = EvmCompileView | SbpfCompileView;
export interface AbiParam { name: string; type: string }
export interface AbiItem { type: string; name?: string; inputs?: AbiParam[]; outputs?: AbiParam[]; stateMutability?: string }

export interface WarningView { kind: string; pc?: number | null; detail: string; explanation: string; fatal: boolean }
export interface GraphNode { id: number; label: string; start: number; end: number; term: string; function?: number | null; static_gas?: number | null }
export interface GraphEdge { from: number; to: number; kind: string }
export interface Graph { nodes: GraphNode[]; edges: GraphEdge[] }
export interface OpLine { pc: number; line: number }
export interface TranspileStats { blocks: number; ops: number; locals: number; c_lines: number; functions?: number | null }
export interface TranspileView { warnings: WarningView[]; graph: Graph; c_source: string; op_map: OpLine[]; stats: TranspileStats }

export interface DisasmLine { pc: number; word: number; text: string; ecall?: string | null }
export interface CheckFinding { addr: number; word: number; rule: string; what: string }
export interface SourceMap { pc_to_c: [number, number][]; c_to_src: [number, number][] }
export interface ImageView { words: number; text_words: number; data_words: number; prologue_words: number; base_pc: number; text_base: number; hc: string; program_id: string; cap_words: number; fits: boolean; findings: CheckFinding[]; disasm: DisasmLine[]; source_map?: SourceMap | null; source_map_note?: string | null }

export type StorageSeed =
  | { kind: 'slot'; slot: string; value: string }
  | { kind: 'mapping'; base: number; key: string; value: string }
  | { kind: 'mapping2'; base: number; key1: string; key2: string; value: string };
export interface EvmCallForm { lang: 'evm'; function: string; args: string[]; caller: string; address?: string | null; value?: string; gas_limit?: number; storage: StorageSeed[] }
export interface AccountForm { key: string; owner: string; lamports: number; data: string; is_signer?: boolean; is_writable?: boolean; executable?: boolean }
export interface SbpfCallForm { lang: 'sbpf'; program_id: string; accounts: AccountForm[]; data: string }
export type CallForm = EvmCallForm | SbpfCallForm;
export interface RunRequest { call: CallForm; max_cycles?: number }

export interface DiscoveryStep { run: number; slot: string; gas_used: number }
export interface TableRows { cpu: number; memory: number; alu: number; poseidon2_permutations: number; keccak_permutations: number; sha256_compressions: number; digest_rows: number }
export interface TierCapacity { tier: number; cpu: number; alu: number; memory_min: number; memory_max: number; poseidon2_permutations: number; keccak_permutations: number; sha256_compressions: number; fits: boolean }
export interface SyscallView { clk: number; kind: string; detail: string }
export interface RunSummary { rid: string; cycles: number; halted: boolean; trap?: string | null; trap_clk?: number | null; outputs: number[]; status: number; tier?: number | null; rows: TableRows; tiers: TierCapacity[]; syscall_counts: [string, number][]; hot_pcs: [number, number][]; input_words: number; public_words: number; discovery: DiscoveryStep[] }
export interface CycleView { clk: number; pc: number; next_pc: number; word: number; rd: number; writes_rd: boolean; rd_val: number; mem_read: boolean; mem_write: boolean; mem_addr: number; mem_val: number; syscall: boolean; hash_row: boolean; n_alu: number; sys_idx: number }
export interface CyclePage { from: number; cycles: CycleView[]; total: number }
export interface StateView { clk: number; regs: number[]; cycle: CycleView; text: string; c_line?: number | null; src_pc?: number | null }
export interface MemoryView { clk: number; addr: number; words: number[] }

export interface SampleMeta { id: string; title: string; lang: Lang; files: string[]; description: string; default_call?: CallForm | null; options?: JobOptions | null }
export interface SampleFile { name: string; encoding: 'text' | 'base64'; content: string }
export interface SampleView { meta: SampleMeta; files: SampleFile[] }
export interface FileIn { name: string; encoding?: 'text' | 'base64'; content: string }
export interface NewJob { lang: Lang; files: FileIn[]; options: JobOptions }

export type JobStreamEvent =
  | { type: 'job'; job: JobView }
  | { type: 'stage'; stage: StageName; state: StageState }
  | { type: 'console'; line: ConsoleLine }
  | { type: 'done' };
```

`CompileView` is internally tagged by `lang` (`evm` / `sbpf`), exactly as serde writes `#[serde(tag = "lang")]`; the Rust `ConsoleLine` event arrives as `{"type":"console", ...fields}` (serde's internal tagging flattens the newtype), so `subscribeJob` normalises it.

`web/lib/api.ts`:
```ts
import type { CompileView, CyclePage, ImageView, JobStreamEvent, JobView, MemoryView, NewJob, RunRequest, RunSummary, SampleMeta, SampleView, StateView, SyscallView, TranspileView } from './types';

const BASE = '/api/v1';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(public status: number, public code: string, message: string) { super(message); this.name = 'ApiError'; }
}

async function req<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, { ...init, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', ...(init?.headers ?? {}) } });
  const text = await res.text();
  const body = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(res.status, body?.error ?? 'http_error', body?.message ?? res.statusText);
  return body as T;
}

const q = (o: Record<string, number>) => Object.entries(o).map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join('&');

export const api = {
  samples: () => req<SampleMeta[]>('/samples'),
  sample: (id: string) => req<SampleView>(`/samples/${id}`),
  createJob: async (j: NewJob) => (await req<{ id: string }>('/jobs', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(j) })).id,
  job: (id: string) => req<JobView>(`/jobs/${id}`),
  compile: (id: string) => req<CompileView>(`/jobs/${id}/compile`),
  transpile: (id: string) => req<TranspileView>(`/jobs/${id}/transpile`),
  image: (id: string) => req<ImageView>(`/jobs/${id}/image`),
  fileUrl: (id: string, name: string) => `${BASE}/jobs/${id}/files/${name}`,
  createRun: (id: string, r: RunRequest) => req<RunSummary>(`/jobs/${id}/runs`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(r) }),
  run: (rid: string) => req<RunSummary>(`/runs/${rid}`),
  cycles: (rid: string, from: number, count: number) => req<CyclePage>(`/runs/${rid}/cycles?${q({ from, count })}`),
  state: (rid: string, at: number) => req<StateView>(`/runs/${rid}/state?${q({ at })}`),
  memory: (rid: string, at: number, addr: number, words: number) => req<MemoryView>(`/runs/${rid}/memory?${q({ at, addr, words })}`),
  syscalls: (rid: string) => req<SyscallView[]>(`/runs/${rid}/syscalls`),
};

/** Server-sent stage events for a job; returns the unsubscribe function. */
export function subscribeJob(id: string, onEvent: (e: JobStreamEvent) => void): () => void {
  const es = new EventSource(`${BASE}/jobs/${id}/events`);
  es.addEventListener('job', (m) => onEvent({ type: 'job', job: JSON.parse((m as MessageEvent).data) }));
  es.addEventListener('stage', (m) => { const d = JSON.parse((m as MessageEvent).data); onEvent({ type: 'stage', stage: d.stage, state: d.state }); });
  es.addEventListener('console', (m) => { const { type: _t, ...line } = JSON.parse((m as MessageEvent).data); onEvent({ type: 'console', line }); });
  es.addEventListener('done', () => { onEvent({ type: 'done' }); es.close(); });
  es.onerror = () => { /* EventSource reconnects by itself; the snapshot event resynchronises state. */ };
  return () => es.close();
}
```

`web/lib/format.ts`:
```ts
export const hex32 = (n: number) => `0x${(n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`;
export const hexAddr = (n: number) => `0x${(n >>> 0).toString(16)}`;
export const groupDigits = (n: number) => n.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ' ').replace(/ /g, ' ');
export const short = (h: string, n = 8) => (h.length > n ? `${h.slice(0, n)}…` : h);
/** The emulator addresses RAM in words; the developer reads byte addresses. */
export const byteAddr = (wordAddr: number) => (wordAddr * 4) >>> 0;
```

(`groupDigits` uses a plain space so tests can compare it; render it with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`.)

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/job.ts`, `web/components/{Shell.tsx,Editor.tsx,SamplesPanel.tsx,PipelineStrip.tsx,Console.tsx,ThemeSwitch.tsx}`, `web/test/job.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/app/page.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api`, `subscribeJob`, types.
- Produces:
  - `web/lib/job.ts`:
    - `type Stage = 'compile' | 'analyse' | 'transpile' | 'image' | 'run'` (the five panels the user sees; `analyse` is the compile stage's second view)
    - `interface JobState { job: JobView | null; console: ConsoleLine[]; error: string | null }`
    - `reduce(s: JobState, e: JobStreamEvent): JobState` (pure)
    - `stageStatus(job: JobView | null, s: Stage): StageStatus | 'idle'`
    - `filesFor(lang, files: Record<string,string>, binary?: string): FileIn[]` (Solana `.so` uploads become `program.so` base64)
    - `useJob(): { state, submit(req: NewJob): Promise<void>, reset(): void }` (React hook: `createJob` → `subscribeJob` → `reduce`)
  - `<Shell />`: the whole page; owns language, files, the job, and the focused stage.

Layout (desktop ≥ 1024 px): a 48 px top bar (wordmark "rand terminal", language picker, Compile & Run button, theme switch); a left rail 240 px (Samples, Files, Pipeline strip); the editor in the middle; the focused stage panel on the right (min 480 px, resizable by a drag handle that stores its width in `localStorage`); the console as a 160 px collapsible strip at the bottom. Below 1024 px, the rail collapses to a top sheet, and editor and stage panel become tabs. Every interactive element is at least 32 px tall (44 px on touch) and reachable by keyboard; the Compile & Run button is `⌘/Ctrl+Enter`.

```ts
import { filesFor, reduce, stageStatus, type JobState } from '@/lib/job';
import type { JobView } from '@/lib/types';

const q = { status: 'queued' as const };
const job: JobView = { id: 'j', lang: 'solidity', options: { chain_id: 20, stage: 2 }, created: 1, compile: q, transpile: q, build: q, console: [], finished: false };
const empty: JobState = { job: null, console: [], error: null };

describe('job reducer', () => {
  it('takes the snapshot', () => { expect(reduce(empty, { type: 'job', job }).job).toEqual(job); });
  it('applies stage changes without mutating', () => {
    const s1 = reduce(empty, { type: 'job', job });
    const s2 = reduce(s1, { type: 'stage', stage: 'compile', state: { status: 'done', started: 1, ended: 2 } });
    expect(s2.job!.compile.status).toBe('done');
    expect(s1.job!.compile.status).toBe('queued');
  });
  it('appends console lines and marks done', () => {
    let s = reduce(empty, { type: 'job', job: { ...job, console: [{ stage: 'compile', stream: 'stdout', text: 'a' }] } });
    s = reduce(s, { type: 'console', line: { stage: 'build', stream: 'stderr', text: 'b' } });
    expect(s.console.map((l) => l.text)).toEqual(['a', 'b']);
    expect(reduce(s, { type: 'done' }).job!.finished).toBe(true);
  });
  it('maps the five panels onto the three stages', () => {
    const j = { ...job, compile: { status: 'done' as const }, transpile: { status: 'running' as const } };
    expect(stageStatus(j, 'compile')).toBe('done');
    expect(stageStatus(j, 'analyse')).toBe('done');
    expect(stageStatus(j, 'transpile')).toBe('running');
    expect(stageStatus(j, 'image')).toBe('queued');
    expect(stageStatus(j, 'run')).toBe('idle');
    expect(stageStatus({ ...j, build: { status: 'done' } }, 'run')).toBe('queued');
    expect(stageStatus(null, 'compile')).toBe('idle');

  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `lib/job.ts`**

```ts
'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useReducer, useRef } from 'react';
import { api, subscribeJob } from './api';
import type { ConsoleLine, FileIn, JobStreamEvent, JobView, Lang, NewJob, StageStatus } from './types';

export type Stage = 'compile' | 'analyse' | 'transpile' | 'image' | 'run';
export const STAGES: { id: Stage; n: number; title: string }[] = [
  { id: 'compile', n: 1, title: 'Compile' },
  { id: 'analyse', n: 2, title: 'Bytecode' },
  { id: 'transpile', n: 3, title: 'Transpile' },
  { id: 'image', n: 4, title: 'RV32IM image' },
  { id: 'run', n: 5, title: 'Run on zkVM' },
];

export interface JobState { job: JobView | null; console: ConsoleLine[]; error: string | null }
export const initial: JobState = { job: null, console: [], error: null };

export function reduce(s: JobState, e: JobStreamEvent): JobState {
  switch (e.type) {
    case 'job': return { ...s, job: e.job, console: e.job.console };
    case 'stage': return s.job ? { ...s, job: { ...s.job, [e.stage]: e.state } } : s;
    case 'console': return { ...s, console: [...s.console, e.line] };
    case 'done': return s.job ? { ...s, job: { ...s.job, finished: true } } : s;
  }
}

export function stageStatus(job: JobView | null, s: Stage): StageStatus | 'idle' {
  if (!job) return 'idle';
  switch (s) {
    case 'compile': case 'analyse': return job.compile.status;
    case 'transpile': return job.transpile.status;
    case 'image': return job.build.status;
    case 'run': return job.build.status === 'done' ? 'queued' : 'idle';
  }
}

export function filesFor(lang: Lang, files: Record<string, string>, binary?: string): FileIn[] {
  if (lang === 'solana-elf') return [{ name: 'program.so', encoding: 'base64', content: binary ?? '' }];
  return Object.entries(files).map(([name, content]) => ({ name, encoding: 'text' as const, content }));
}

type Action = JobStreamEvent | { type: 'reset' } | { type: 'error'; message: string };

function reducer(s: JobState, a: Action): JobState {
  if (a.type === 'reset') return initial;
  if (a.type === 'error') return { ...s, error: a.message };
  return reduce(s, a);
}

export function useJob() {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, initial);
  const unsub = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => () => unsub.current?.(), []);
  const submit = useCallback(async (req: NewJob) => {
    unsub.current?.();
    dispatch({ type: 'reset' });
    try {
      const id = await api.createJob(req);
      unsub.current = subscribeJob(id, dispatch);
    } catch (e) {
      dispatch({ type: 'error', message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
    }
  }, []);
  const reset = useCallback(() => { unsub.current?.(); dispatch({ type: 'reset' }); }, []);
  return { state, submit, reset };
}
```

`web/components/Editor.tsx` — CodeMirror 6, one instance per file tab, diagnostics as lint markers:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { EditorView, basicSetup } from 'codemirror';
import { EditorState, Compartment } from '@codemirror/state';
import { keymap } from '@codemirror/view';
import { linter, setDiagnostics, type Diagnostic as CmDiagnostic } from '@codemirror/lint';
import { rust } from '@codemirror/lang-rust';
import { solidity } from '@replit/codemirror-lang-solidity';
import type { Diagnostic } from '@/lib/types';

const theme = EditorView.theme({
  '&': { height: '100%', backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-soft)', color: 'var(--text)' },
  '.cm-content': { fontFamily: 'var(--mono)', fontSize: '13px' },
  '.cm-gutters': { backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-soft)', color: 'var(--text-mute)', borderRight: '1px solid var(--border-soft)' },
  '.cm-activeLine, .cm-activeLineGutter': { backgroundColor: 'var(--hl)' },
  '&.cm-focused .cm-cursor': { borderLeftColor: 'var(--accent)' },
});

export function Editor({ value, onChange, language, diagnostics, onRun, readOnly = false }: {
  value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void; language: 'solidity' | 'rust' | 'toml'; diagnostics: Diagnostic[]; onRun: () => void; readOnly?: boolean;
}) {
  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const view = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
  const lang = useRef(new Compartment());
  const cb = useRef({ onChange, onRun });
  cb.current = { onChange, onRun };

  useEffect(() => {
    const v = new EditorView({
      parent: host.current!,
      state: EditorState.create({
        doc: value,
        extensions: [
          basicSetup, theme, linter(() => []),
          lang.current.of(language === 'solidity' ? solidity : language === 'rust' ? rust() : []),
          keymap.of([{ key: 'Mod-Enter', run: () => { cb.current.onRun(); return true; } }]),
          EditorView.updateListener.of((u) => { if (u.docChanged) cb.current.onChange(u.state.doc.toString()); }),
          EditorState.readOnly.of(readOnly),
        ],
      }),
    });
    view.current = v;
    return () => v.destroy();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [language, readOnly]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const v = view.current;
    if (v && v.state.doc.toString() !== value) v.dispatch({ changes: { from: 0, to: v.state.doc.length, insert: value } });
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const v = view.current;
    if (!v) return;
    const doc = v.state.doc;
    const marks: CmDiagnostic[] = diagnostics.filter((d) => d.line >= 1 && d.line <= doc.lines).map((d) => {
      const line = doc.line(d.line);
      const from = Math.min(line.from + Math.max(d.col - 1, 0), line.to);
      return { from, to: line.to > from ? line.to : from, severity: d.severity === 'error' ? 'error' : d.severity === 'warning' ? 'warning' : 'info', message: d.message };
    });
    v.dispatch(setDiagnostics(v.state, marks));
  }, [diagnostics]);

  return <div ref={host} className="h-full overflow-hidden" aria-label="source editor" />;
}
```

`web/components/PipelineStrip.tsx`:
```tsx
'use client';
import { STAGES, stageStatus, type Stage } from '@/lib/job';
import type { JobView } from '@/lib/types';

const LABEL: Record<string, string> = { idle: 'not started', queued: 'waiting', running: 'running', done: 'done', failed: 'failed' };
const DOT: Record<string, string> = { idle: 'bg-border', queued: 'bg-text-mute', running: 'bg-accent animate-pulse', done: 'bg-positive', failed: 'bg-negative' };

export function PipelineStrip({ job, focused, onFocus }: { job: JobView | null; focused: Stage; onFocus: (s: Stage) => void }) {
  return (
    <ol className="flex flex-col gap-1" aria-label="pipeline">
      {STAGES.map((s) => {
        const st = stageStatus(job, s.id);
        return (
          <li key={s.id}>
            <button onClick={() => onFocus(s.id)} aria-current={focused === s.id ? 'step' : undefined}
              className={`flex w-full min-h-9 items-center gap-3 rounded-sm px-2 text-left ${focused === s.id ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft hover:bg-surface'}`}>
              <span className="mono w-4 text-text-mute">{s.n}</span>
              <span className="flex-1">{s.title}</span>
              <span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${DOT[st]}`} aria-hidden />
              <span className="sr-only">{LABEL[st]}</span>
            </button>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { ConsoleLine } from '@/lib/types';

export function Console({ lines }: { lines: ConsoleLine[] }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const end = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => { if (open) end.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' }); }, [lines, open]);
  return (
    <section className="border-t border-border-soft bg-bg-soft" aria-label="console">
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} aria-expanded={open} className="flex h-8 w-full items-center gap-2 px-3 text-xs text-text-soft">
        <span>{open ? '▾' : '▸'}</span> Console <span className="text-text-mute">({lines.length})</span>
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="mono h-40 overflow-auto px-3 pb-2 text-xs">
          {lines.map((l, i) => (
            <pre key={i} className={`whitespace-pre-wrap ${l.stream === 'stderr' ? 'text-warning' : 'text-text-soft'}`}><span className="text-text-mute">[{l.stage}] </span>{l.text}</pre>
          ))}
          <div ref={end} />
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```
