```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { SampleMeta, SampleView } from '@/lib/types';

export function SamplesPanel({ onLoad }: { onLoad: (s: SampleView) => void }) {
  const [list, setList] = useState<SampleMeta[]>([]);
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => { api.samples().then(setList).catch((e) => setErr(String(e.message ?? e))); }, []);
  return (
    <section aria-label="samples">
      <h2 className="px-2 pb-1 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-text-mute">Samples</h2>
      {err && <p className="px-2 text-xs text-negative">{err}</p>}
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {list.map((m) => (
          <li key={m.id}>
            <button title={m.description} onClick={() => api.sample(m.id).then(onLoad)} className="min-h-9 w-full rounded-sm px-2 text-left text-text-soft hover:bg-surface">
              {m.title} <span className="text-xs text-text-mute">{m.lang === 'solidity' ? 'Solidity' : m.lang === 'solana-rust' ? 'Solana Rust' : 'Solana .so'}</span>
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/ThemeSwitch.tsx`:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
const KEY = 'randterminal.theme';
const NEXT: Record<string, string> = { system: 'dark', dark: 'light', light: 'system' };
export function ThemeSwitch() {
  const [t, setT] = useState('system');
  useEffect(() => { setT(document.documentElement.dataset.theme ?? 'system'); }, []);
  const flip = () => { const n = NEXT[t] ?? 'system'; setT(n); document.documentElement.dataset.theme = n; try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, n); } catch { /* private mode */ } };
  return <button onClick={flip} className="h-8 rounded-sm px-2 text-xs text-text-soft hover:bg-surface" aria-label={`theme: ${t}`}>{t}</button>;
}
```

`web/components/Shell.tsx` — owns the editor files, language, contract choice and focused panel; renders the stage panels from Tasks 22–24 (import them as they land; until then render a placeholder `<p>` per stage):
```tsx
'use client';
import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Editor } from './Editor';
import { SamplesPanel } from './SamplesPanel';
import { PipelineStrip } from './PipelineStrip';
import { Console } from './Console';
import { ThemeSwitch } from './ThemeSwitch';
import { StagePanel } from './StagePanel';
import { filesFor, useJob, type Stage } from '@/lib/job';
import type { CallForm, Diagnostic, JobOptions, Lang, SampleView } from '@/lib/types';

const STARTER: Record<Lang, Record<string, string>> = {
  solidity: { 'Contract.sol': '// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\ncontract Hello {\n    uint256 public n;\n    function bump() external { n += 1; }\n}\n' },
  'solana-rust': { 'lib.rs': '// Pick the "Solana counter" sample to start from a working program.\n', 'Cargo.toml': '' },
  'solana-elf': {},
};

export function Shell() {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<Lang>('solidity');
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<Record<string, string>>(STARTER.solidity);
  const [active, setActive] = useState('Contract.sol');
  const [binary, setBinary] = useState<string | undefined>();
  const [options, setOptions] = useState<JobOptions>({ chain_id: 20, stage: 2 });
  const [defaultCall, setDefaultCall] = useState<CallForm | null>(null);
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState<Stage>('compile');
  const { state, submit } = useJob();

  const run = useCallback(() => { setFocused('compile'); void submit({ lang, files: filesFor(lang, files, binary), options }); }, [lang, files, binary, options, submit]);

  const load = (s: SampleView) => {
    setLang(s.meta.lang);
    setOptions(s.meta.options ?? { chain_id: 20, stage: 2 });
    setDefaultCall(s.meta.default_call ?? null);
    if (s.meta.lang === 'solana-elf') { setFiles({}); setBinary(s.files[0]?.content); setActive(''); }
    else { const f = Object.fromEntries(s.files.map((x) => [x.name, x.content])); setFiles(f); setBinary(undefined); setActive(s.files[0]?.name ?? ''); }
  };

  const onUpload = async (f: File) => {
    const buf = new Uint8Array(await f.arrayBuffer());
    let s = ''; for (const b of buf) s += String.fromCharCode(b);
    setBinary(btoa(s)); setLang('solana-elf'); setFiles({}); setActive('');
  };

  const diagnostics: Diagnostic[] = useMemo(() => (state.job?.compile.error?.diagnostics ?? []).filter((d) => d.file === active || d.file.endsWith(`/${active}`)), [state.job, active]);
  const busy = !!state.job && !state.job.finished;

  return (
    <div className="grid h-screen grid-rows-[48px_1fr_auto]">
      <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-border-soft px-4">
        <span className="mono font-semibold text-text-strong">rand terminal</span>
        <select aria-label="language" value={lang} onChange={(e) => { const l = e.target.value as Lang; setLang(l); setFiles(STARTER[l]); setActive(Object.keys(STARTER[l])[0] ?? ''); setDefaultCall(null); }}
          className="h-8 rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-text">
          <option value="solidity">Solidity</option><option value="solana-rust">Solana (Rust)</option><option value="solana-elf">Solana (.so upload)</option>
        </select>
        {lang === 'solidity' && <label className="text-xs text-text-mute">chain id <input type="number" value={options.chain_id} onChange={(e) => setOptions({ ...options, chain_id: Number(e.target.value) })} className="mono ml-1 h-8 w-20 rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-text" /></label>}
        <button onClick={run} disabled={busy} className="ml-auto h-8 rounded-sm bg-accent px-4 font-medium text-on-accent disabled:opacity-50">{busy ? 'Working…' : 'Compile & Run'}</button>
        <ThemeSwitch />
      </header>
      <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-cols-[240px_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(480px,45%)] max-lg:grid-cols-1">
        <aside className="flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-4 overflow-auto border-r border-border-soft p-2">
          <SamplesPanel onLoad={load} />
          <section aria-label="files">
            <h2 className="px-2 pb-1 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-text-mute">Files</h2>
            {Object.keys(files).map((n) => <button key={n} onClick={() => setActive(n)} className={`mono block min-h-8 w-full rounded-sm px-2 text-left text-xs ${n === active ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft hover:bg-surface'}`}>{n}</button>)}
            {lang === 'solana-elf' && <label className="block px-2 text-xs text-text-soft">Upload .so <input type="file" accept=".so" onChange={(e) => e.target.files?.[0] && onUpload(e.target.files[0])} className="mt-1 block w-full text-xs" /></label>}
          </section>
          <section aria-label="stages"><h2 className="px-2 pb-1 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-text-mute">Pipeline</h2><PipelineStrip job={state.job} focused={focused} onFocus={setFocused} /></section>
        </aside>
        <main className="min-h-0 border-r border-border-soft">
          {lang === 'solana-elf'
            ? <div className="p-6 text-text-soft">{binary ? `program.so loaded (${Math.round((binary.length * 3) / 4 / 1024)} KB). Press Compile & Run.` : 'Upload a Solana program (.so) built with cargo build-sbf, or pick the SPL Token sample.'}</div>
            : <Editor key={`${lang}:${active}`} value={files[active] ?? ''} onChange={(v) => setFiles({ ...files, [active]: v })} language={active.endsWith('.sol') ? 'solidity' : active.endsWith('.rs') ? 'rust' : 'toml'} diagnostics={diagnostics} onRun={run} />}
        </main>
        <section className="min-h-0 overflow-auto" aria-label="stage panel">
          {state.error && <p className="m-3 rounded-sm border border-negative p-3 text-negative">{state.error}</p>}
          <StagePanel stage={focused} job={state.job} defaultCall={defaultCall} onFocus={setFocused} />
        </section>
      </div>
      <Console lines={state.console} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 22: Compile, bytecode, transpile and image panels

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/{graph-layout.ts,virtual.ts,useStageView.ts}`, `web/components/{VirtualList.tsx,CompilePanel.tsx,AnalysePanel.tsx,TranspilePanel.tsx,BlockGraph.tsx,CViewer.tsx,ImagePanel.tsx,StageError.tsx}`, `web/test/{graph-layout.test.ts,virtual.test.ts}`
- Modify: `web/components/StagePanel.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `layout(graph: Graph, opts?: { nodeW?: number; nodeH?: number; gapX?: number; gapY?: number; maxNodes?: number }): { nodes: (GraphNode & { x: number; y: number })[]; edges: { from: number; to: number; kind: string; points: [number, number][] }[]; width: number; height: number; truncated: boolean }` — layered layout: rank = longest path from node 0 over forward edges (back edges, where `to ≤ from` in pc order, are drawn but do not rank); within a rank, nodes keep pc order; capped at `maxNodes` (default 400) with `truncated: true`.
  - `visibleRange(scrollTop: number, viewport: number, rowH: number, n: number, overscan = 8): [number, number]`.
  - `<VirtualList rows={n} rowHeight={20} render={(i) => …} scrollTo?={i} />` — fixed-height windowing (65 096-line listings scroll at 60 fps).
  - `useStageView<T>(jobId, stage: 'compile'|'transpile'|'image', ready: boolean): { data: T | null; error: string | null }` (fetches once when the stage turns `done`).
  - The C viewer and the opcode listing share a selection: clicking an opcode (EVM pc / sBPF slot) highlights its C lines (from `op_map`: the marker line up to the line before the next marker) and scrolls the C viewer; clicking a C line selects the opcode returned by the last marker at or before it.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { layout } from '@/lib/graph-layout';
import type { Graph } from '@/lib/types';

const node = (id: number, start: number) => ({ id, label: String(start), start, end: start + 1, term: '' });

describe('layout', () => {
  it('ranks by longest forward path and keeps pc order in a rank', () => {
    const g: Graph = { nodes: [node(0, 0), node(1, 10), node(2, 20), node(3, 30)], edges: [
      { from: 0, to: 1, kind: 'fallthrough' }, { from: 0, to: 2, kind: 'taken' }, { from: 1, to: 3, kind: 'fallthrough' }, { from: 2, to: 3, kind: 'fallthrough' }, { from: 3, to: 0, kind: 'jump' },
    ] };
    const l = layout(g, { nodeW: 100, nodeH: 40, gapX: 20, gapY: 30 });
    const y = Object.fromEntries(l.nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n.y]));
    expect(y[0]).toBe(0);
    expect(y[1]).toBe(70);
    expect(y[2]).toBe(70);
    expect(y[3]).toBe(140);
    const r1 = l.nodes.filter((n) => n.y === 70).map((n) => n.id);
    expect(r1).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(l.edges).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(l.truncated).toBe(false);
  });
  it('places unreachable nodes after the reachable ones', () => {
    const l = layout({ nodes: [node(0, 0), node(1, 5)], edges: [] });
    expect(l.nodes.find((n) => n.id === 1)!.y).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it('caps the node count', () => {
    const nodes = Array.from({ length: 1000 }, (_, i) => node(i, i));
    const l = layout({ nodes, edges: [] }, { maxNodes: 400 });
    expect(l.nodes).toHaveLength(400);
    expect(l.truncated).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the pure functions**

`web/lib/virtual.ts`:
```ts
export function visibleRange(scrollTop: number, viewport: number, rowH: number, n: number, overscan = 8): [number, number] {
  if (n === 0) return [0, 0];
  const first = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowH) - overscan);
  const last = Math.min(n, Math.ceil((scrollTop + viewport) / rowH) + overscan);
  return [first, last];
}
```

`web/lib/graph-layout.ts`:
```ts
import type { Graph, GraphNode } from './types';

export interface Placed extends GraphNode { x: number; y: number }
export interface Routed { from: number; to: number; kind: string; points: [number, number][] }

export function layout(g: Graph, o: { nodeW?: number; nodeH?: number; gapX?: number; gapY?: number; maxNodes?: number } = {}) {
  const W = o.nodeW ?? 120, H = o.nodeH ?? 36, GX = o.gapX ?? 24, GY = o.gapY ?? 36, MAX = o.maxNodes ?? 400;
  const truncated = g.nodes.length > MAX;
  const nodes = g.nodes.slice(0, MAX);
  const ids = new Set(nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const byId = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));
  const edges = g.edges.filter((e) => ids.has(e.from) && ids.has(e.to));
  const forward = edges.filter((e) => byId.get(e.to)!.start > byId.get(e.from)!.start);
  // Longest path over forward edges, processed in pc order (forward edges only go up in pc, so this is topological).
  const rank = new Map<number, number>();
  const order = [...nodes].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const reachable = new Set<number>();
  if (order.length) { rank.set(order[0]!.id, 0); reachable.add(order[0]!.id); }
  for (const n of order) {
    if (!reachable.has(n.id)) continue;
    for (const e of forward.filter((e) => e.from === n.id)) {
      rank.set(e.to, Math.max(rank.get(e.to) ?? 0, rank.get(n.id)! + 1));
      reachable.add(e.to);
    }
  }
  let maxRank = Math.max(0, ...rank.values());
  for (const n of order) if (!reachable.has(n.id)) rank.set(n.id, ++maxRank);
  const rows = new Map<number, GraphNode[]>();
  for (const n of order) { const r = rank.get(n.id)!; rows.set(r, [...(rows.get(r) ?? []), n]); }
  const placed: Placed[] = [];
  let width = 0;
  for (const [r, row] of rows) row.forEach((n, i) => { placed.push({ ...n, x: i * (W + GX), y: r * (H + GY) }); width = Math.max(width, (i + 1) * (W + GX)); });
  const at = new Map(placed.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
  const routed: Routed[] = edges.map((e) => {
    const a = at.get(e.from)!, b = at.get(e.to)!;
    const back = b.y <= a.y;
    const pts: [number, number][] = back
      ? [[a.x + W, a.y + H / 2], [Math.max(a.x, b.x) + W + GX / 2, a.y + H / 2], [Math.max(a.x, b.x) + W + GX / 2, b.y + H / 2], [b.x + W, b.y + H / 2]]
      : [[a.x + W / 2, a.y + H], [a.x + W / 2, a.y + H + GY / 2], [b.x + W / 2, a.y + H + GY / 2], [b.x + W / 2, b.y]];
    return { ...e, points: pts };
  });
  const height = (Math.max(0, ...placed.map((p) => p.y)) + H);
  return { nodes: placed, edges: routed, width, height, truncated };
}
```

Run: `cd web && npx vitest run 2>&1 | tail -3`
Expected: all pass. (The ranking test's expected `y` values are `rank × (nodeH + gapY)`.)

`web/lib/useStageView.ts`:
```ts
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from './api';

export function useStageView<T>(jobId: string | undefined, stage: 'compile' | 'transpile' | 'image', ready: boolean) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setData(null); setError(null);
    if (!jobId || !ready) return;
    let live = true;
    (api[stage](jobId) as Promise<T>).then((d) => live && setData(d)).catch((e) => live && setError(e.message));
    return () => { live = false; };
  }, [jobId, stage, ready]);
  return { data, error };
}
```

`web/components/VirtualList.tsx`:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { visibleRange } from '@/lib/virtual';

export function VirtualList({ rows, rowHeight = 20, render, scrollTo, className = '' }: { rows: number; rowHeight?: number; render: (i: number) => React.ReactNode; scrollTo?: number | null; className?: string }) {
  const el = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [top, setTop] = useState(0);
  const [vh, setVh] = useState(600);
  useEffect(() => { const e = el.current; if (!e) return; const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => setVh(e.clientHeight)); ro.observe(e); return () => ro.disconnect(); }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const e = el.current;
    if (e == null || scrollTo == null) return;
    const y = scrollTo * rowHeight;
    if (y < e.scrollTop || y > e.scrollTop + e.clientHeight - rowHeight) e.scrollTop = Math.max(0, y - e.clientHeight / 3);
  }, [scrollTo, rowHeight]);
  const [a, b] = visibleRange(top, vh, rowHeight, rows);
  return (
    <div ref={el} onScroll={(e) => setTop(e.currentTarget.scrollTop)} className={`relative overflow-auto ${className}`}>
      <div style={{ height: rows * rowHeight }} />
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: a * rowHeight, left: 0, right: 0 }}>
        {Array.from({ length: b - a }, (_, k) => <div key={a + k} style={{ height: rowHeight }}>{render(a + k)}</div>)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { StageError as E } from '@/lib/types';

}
```

`web/components/CompilePanel.tsx` — stage 1. EVM: contract name (and a select when `contracts.length > 1`, which re-submits with `options.contract`), size, a creation/runtime toggle over the hex (wrapped, monospace, Copy button), the ABI as a table (name, inputs, outputs, mutability). Solana: ELF size, text/rodata sizes, entry pc, function and instruction counts, the syscall table (name, count, supported ✓/✗ with text), warnings with their explanations.
```tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { CompileView } from '@/lib/types';
import { groupDigits } from '@/lib/format';

export function CompilePanel({ view }: { view: CompileView }) {
  const [which, setWhich] = useState<'runtime' | 'creation'>('runtime');
  if (view.lang === 'evm') {
    const hex = which === 'runtime' ? view.runtime_hex : view.creation_hex;
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
          <dt className="text-text-mute">contract</dt><dd>{view.contract}{view.contracts.length > 1 && <span className="text-text-mute"> (of {view.contracts.join(', ')})</span>}</dd>
          <dt className="text-text-mute">runtime bytecode</dt><dd className="mono">{groupDigits(view.size_bytes)} bytes · {view.blocks} blocks · {view.jumpdests} jump destinations</dd>
        </dl>
        <div>
          <div className="mb-2 flex gap-1" role="tablist">
            {(['runtime', 'creation'] as const).map((w) => <button key={w} role="tab" aria-selected={which === w} onClick={() => setWhich(w)} className={`h-8 rounded-sm px-3 text-xs ${which === w ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft'}`}>{w}</button>)}
            <button onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(hex)} className="ml-auto h-8 rounded-sm px-3 text-xs text-text-soft hover:bg-surface">Copy</button>
          </div>
          <pre className="mono max-h-64 overflow-auto rounded-sm bg-bg-soft p-3 text-xs break-all whitespace-pre-wrap">{hex || '(empty)'}</pre>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-mute">The transpiler takes the runtime code: what the chain stores, not the constructor that deploys it.</p>
        </div>
        <table className="text-xs"><caption className="pb-1 text-left text-text-mute">ABI</caption>
          <thead><tr className="text-left text-text-mute"><th>function</th><th>inputs</th><th>outputs</th><th>mutability</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>{view.abi.filter((a) => a.type === 'function').map((a, i) => <tr key={i} className="mono"><td>{a.name}</td><td>{a.inputs?.map((p) => p.type).join(', ')}</td><td>{a.outputs?.map((p) => p.type).join(', ')}</td><td>{a.stateMutability}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm mono">
        <dt className="text-text-mute">ELF</dt><dd>{groupDigits(view.elf_bytes)} bytes</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-mute">.text / .rodata</dt><dd>{groupDigits(view.text_bytes)} / {groupDigits(view.rodata_bytes)} bytes</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-mute">entry</dt><dd>slot {view.entry_pc}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-mute">functions</dt><dd>{view.functions.length} · {groupDigits(view.instructions)} instructions</dd>
      </dl>
      <table className="text-xs"><caption className="pb-1 text-left text-text-mute">Syscalls</caption>
        <tbody>{view.syscalls.map((s) => <tr key={s.hash} className="mono"><td>{s.name}</td><td>{s.count} call site(s)</td><td className={s.supported ? 'text-positive' : 'text-negative'}>{s.supported ? 'supported' : 'traps on Rand'}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
      </table>
      {view.warnings.map((w, i) => <p key={i} className="text-xs text-warning">{w.detail}: {w.explanation}</p>)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/AnalysePanel.tsx` — stage 2: the listing the transpiler sees, virtualised. EVM rows: `pc · mnemonic · push · gas`, jump destinations marked `◆`, alternate block shading; sBPF rows: function header rows, then `slot · text`, block terminators in `text-text-mute`. Clicking a row sets the shared selection (`selected: number | null`, a source pc) that `TranspilePanel` also reads; pass it down from `StagePanel`.
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { VirtualList } from './VirtualList';
import type { CompileView } from '@/lib/types';

type Row = { pc: number; text: string; note?: string; shade: boolean; header?: boolean };

export function rowsOf(view: CompileView): Row[] {
  if (view.lang === 'evm') return view.opcodes.map((o) => ({ pc: o.pc, text: `${o.jumpdest ? '◆ ' : '  '}${o.mnemonic}${o.push_hex ? ` 0x${o.push_hex}` : ''}`, note: `gas ${o.static_gas}`, shade: o.block % 2 === 1 }));
  const out: Row[] = [];
  view.functions.forEach((f, fi) => {
    out.push({ pc: f.entry, text: `function @${f.entry}`, shade: false, header: true });
    f.blocks.forEach((b, bi) => { b.insns.forEach((i) => out.push({ pc: i.pc, text: i.text, shade: bi % 2 === 1 })); out.push({ pc: b.end, text: `  └ ${b.term}`, shade: bi % 2 === 1, note: `fn ${fi}` }); });
  });
  return out;
}

export function AnalysePanel({ view, selected, onSelect }: { view: CompileView; selected: number | null; onSelect: (pc: number) => void }) {
  const rows = useMemo(() => rowsOf(view), [view]);
  const idx = selected == null ? null : rows.findIndex((r) => r.pc === selected && !r.header);
  return (
    <VirtualList className="h-full mono text-xs" rows={rows.length} scrollTo={idx} render={(i) => {
      const r = rows[i]!;
      return (
        <button onClick={() => !r.header && onSelect(r.pc)} className={`flex h-5 w-full items-center gap-3 px-3 text-left ${r.header ? 'font-semibold text-text-strong' : ''} ${r.pc === selected && !r.header ? 'bg-hl' : r.shade ? 'bg-bg-soft' : ''}`}>
          <span className="w-14 text-right text-text-mute">{view.lang === 'evm' ? `0x${r.pc.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}` : r.pc}</span>
          <span className="flex-1 truncate">{r.text}</span>
          {r.note && <span className="text-text-mute">{r.note}</span>}
        </button>
      );
    }} />
  );
}
```

`web/components/BlockGraph.tsx` — inline SVG of `layout(view.graph)`: nodes as rounded rects labelled with the start pc and terminator; edges as polylines with arrowheads, `taken`/`jump` in `var(--accent)`, `not-taken`/`fallthrough` in `var(--text-mute)`, back edges dashed; clicking a node selects its start pc; the selected node gets a 2 px accent stroke; a note "showing the first 400 of N blocks" when truncated; the SVG sits in a scroll container (pan by scrolling).
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { layout } from '@/lib/graph-layout';
import type { Graph } from '@/lib/types';

export function BlockGraph({ graph, selected, onSelect, pcLabel }: { graph: Graph; selected: number | null; onSelect: (pc: number) => void; pcLabel: (pc: number) => string }) {
  const l = useMemo(() => layout(graph), [graph]);
  const W = 120, H = 36;
  const sel = selected == null ? null : graph.nodes.find((n) => selected >= n.start && selected <= n.end)?.id;
  return (
    <div className="overflow-auto p-3">
      {l.truncated && <p className="pb-2 text-xs text-text-mute">Showing the first 400 of {graph.nodes.length} blocks.</p>}
      <svg width={l.width + 40} height={l.height + 20} role="img" aria-label={`control-flow graph, ${graph.nodes.length} blocks`}>
        <defs><marker id="arr" viewBox="0 0 6 6" refX="5" refY="3" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0L6,3L0,6z" fill="var(--text-mute)" /></marker></defs>
        {l.edges.map((e, i) => {
          const back = e.points[0]![1] === e.points[1]![1];
          return <polyline key={i} points={e.points.map((p) => p.join(',')).join(' ')} fill="none" markerEnd="url(#arr)" strokeWidth={1.2}
            stroke={e.kind === 'taken' || e.kind === 'jump' || e.kind === 'call' ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--text-mute)'} strokeDasharray={back ? '4 3' : undefined} />;
        })}
        {l.nodes.map((n) => (
          <g key={n.id} transform={`translate(${n.x},${n.y})`} onClick={() => onSelect(n.start)} className="cursor-pointer">
            <rect width={W} height={H} rx={6} fill="var(--surface)" stroke={n.id === sel ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border)'} strokeWidth={n.id === sel ? 2 : 1} />
            <text x={8} y={15} className="mono" fontSize={11} fill="var(--text-strong)">{pcLabel(n.start)}</text>
            <text x={8} y={29} fontSize={10} fill="var(--text-mute)">{n.term.length > 18 ? `${n.term.slice(0, 17)}…` : n.term}</text>
            <title>{`${pcLabel(n.start)}–${pcLabel(n.end)}: ${n.term}`}</title>
          </g>
        ))}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/CViewer.tsx` — the generated C, virtualised, with line numbers; `highlight: [from, to]` lines tinted; clicking a line calls `onLine(line)`.
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { VirtualList } from './VirtualList';

export function CViewer({ source, highlight, onLine }: { source: string; highlight: [number, number] | null; onLine: (line: number) => void }) {
  const lines = useMemo(() => source.split('\n'), [source]);
  return (
    <VirtualList className="h-full mono text-xs" rows={lines.length} scrollTo={highlight ? highlight[0] - 1 : null} render={(i) => {
      const n = i + 1;
      const on = highlight && n >= highlight[0] && n <= highlight[1];
      return (
        <div onClick={() => onLine(n)} className={`flex h-5 cursor-pointer gap-3 px-3 ${on ? 'bg-hl' : ''}`}>
          <span className="w-12 select-none text-right text-text-mute">{n}</span>
          <span className="whitespace-pre">{lines[i]}</span>
        </div>
      );
    }} />
  );
}
```

`web/components/TranspilePanel.tsx` — stage 3: stats line (blocks, ops, locals, C lines), warnings (fatal in `text-negative`, others `text-warning`, each with its explanation), a two-pane split: left tabs "Blocks" (`BlockGraph`) and "Listing" (`AnalysePanel` again, so the user can pick by opcode), right `CViewer`. Selection logic:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { AnalysePanel } from './AnalysePanel';
import { BlockGraph } from './BlockGraph';
import { CViewer } from './CViewer';
import type { CompileView, OpLine, TranspileView } from '@/lib/types';

/** The C lines an op owns: from its marker to the line before the next marker. */
export function linesOf(opMap: OpLine[], pc: number, total: number): [number, number] | null {
  const i = opMap.findIndex((o) => o.pc === pc);
  if (i < 0) return null;
  return [opMap[i]!.line, (opMap[i + 1]?.line ?? total + 1) - 1];
}
/** The op whose C contains `line`: the last marker at or before it. */
export function opAt(opMap: OpLine[], line: number): number | null {
  let lo = 0, hi = opMap.length - 1, best: number | null = null;
  while (lo <= hi) { const m = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (opMap[m]!.line <= line) { best = opMap[m]!.pc; lo = m + 1; } else hi = m - 1; }
  return best;
}

export function TranspilePanel({ view, compile, selected, onSelect }: { view: TranspileView; compile: CompileView | null; selected: number | null; onSelect: (pc: number) => void }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'blocks' | 'listing'>('blocks');
  const total = view.stats.c_lines;
  const hl = useMemo(() => (selected == null ? null : linesOf(view.op_map, selected, total)), [selected, view.op_map, total]);
  const evm = compile?.lang === 'evm';
  const pcLabel = (pc: number) => (evm ? `0x${pc.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}` : `${pc}`);
  return (
    <div className="grid h-full grid-rows-[auto_auto_1fr]">
      <p className="mono px-4 pt-3 text-xs text-text-soft">{view.stats.blocks} blocks · {view.stats.ops} {evm ? 'opcodes' : 'instructions'}{view.stats.functions ? ` · ${view.stats.functions} functions` : ''} · {view.stats.locals ? `${view.stats.locals} locals · ` : ''}{view.stats.c_lines} lines of C</p>
      <div className="px-4 py-2">{view.warnings.map((w, i) => <p key={i} className={`text-xs ${w.fatal ? 'text-negative' : 'text-warning'}`}>{w.detail}. {w.explanation}</p>)}</div>
      <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-cols-2 border-t border-border-soft">
        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col border-r border-border-soft">
          <div className="flex gap-1 p-2" role="tablist">{(['blocks', 'listing'] as const).map((t) => <button key={t} role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t} onClick={() => setTab(t)} className={`h-8 rounded-sm px-3 text-xs ${tab === t ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft'}`}>{t === 'blocks' ? 'Block graph' : evm ? 'Opcodes' : 'Instructions'}</button>)}</div>
          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">{tab === 'blocks' ? <BlockGraph graph={view.graph} selected={selected} onSelect={onSelect} pcLabel={pcLabel} /> : compile && <AnalysePanel view={compile} selected={selected} onSelect={onSelect} />}</div>
        </div>
        <CViewer source={view.c_source} highlight={hl} onLine={(l) => { const pc = opAt(view.op_map, l); if (pc != null) onSelect(pc); }} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/ImagePanel.tsx` — stage 4: a stats card (words against the cap with a thin bar, text/data/prologue words, base pc, `hc` and program id with Copy buttons and a one-line explanation of each: "hc is the zkVM's commitment to this exact program; the chain identifies the program by its program id"), `findings` as a list, the `source_map_note` when present, and the RV32IM listing virtualised with columns `pc · word · instruction · C line` (the C line from `source_map.pc_to_c` by binary search; blank when absent), a pc search box (hex) that scrolls to the row, and `ecall` rows annotated with the syscall name. Download link for `image.bin` (`api.fileUrl`).
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { VirtualList } from './VirtualList';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { groupDigits, hex32, hexAddr, short } from '@/lib/format';
import type { ImageView } from '@/lib/types';

export function cLineAt(map: [number, number][] | undefined, pc: number): number | null {
  if (!map?.length) return null;
  let lo = 0, hi = map.length - 1, best: number | null = null;
  while (lo <= hi) { const m = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (map[m]![0] <= pc) { best = map[m]![1]; lo = m + 1; } else hi = m - 1; }
  return best;
}

export function ImagePanel({ jobId, view, focusPc }: { jobId: string; view: ImageView; focusPc?: number | null }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const target = useMemo(() => { const pc = focusPc ?? (q ? parseInt(q.replace(/^0x/, ''), 16) : NaN); return Number.isFinite(pc) ? Math.floor((pc - view.base_pc) / 4) : null; }, [q, focusPc, view.base_pc]);
  const pct = Math.min(100, (view.words / view.cap_words) * 100);
  return (
    <div className="grid h-full grid-rows-[auto_1fr]">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4 text-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3"><span className="mono">{groupDigits(view.words)} words</span><span className="text-text-mute">of a {groupDigits(view.cap_words)}-word cap</span>
          <span className={view.fits ? 'text-positive' : 'text-negative'}>{view.fits ? 'fits' : 'does not fit'}</span>
          <a href={api.fileUrl(jobId, 'image.bin')} className="ml-auto text-xs text-accent">download image.bin</a></div>
        <div className="h-1.5 w-full rounded-full bg-surface-2"><div className={`h-1.5 rounded-full ${view.fits ? 'bg-accent' : 'bg-negative'}`} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} /></div>
        <p className="mono text-xs text-text-soft">text {groupDigits(view.text_words)} · data {groupDigits(view.data_words)} · prologue {view.prologue_words} · base pc {hexAddr(view.base_pc)}</p>
        <p className="text-xs"><span className="text-text-mute">hc</span> <span className="mono" title={view.hc}>{short(view.hc, 16)}</span> <button className="text-accent" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(view.hc)}>copy</button> <span className="text-text-mute">· the zkVM's commitment to this exact program</span></p>
        <p className="text-xs"><span className="text-text-mute">program id</span> <span className="mono" title={view.program_id}>{short(view.program_id, 16)}</span> <button className="text-accent" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(view.program_id)}>copy</button> <span className="text-text-mute">· how the chain names it</span></p>
        {view.findings.map((f, i) => <p key={i} className="mono text-xs text-warning">{hexAddr(f.addr)} {f.rule}: {f.what}</p>)}
        {view.source_map_note && <p className="text-xs text-text-mute">{view.source_map_note}</p>}
        <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="jump to pc (hex)" aria-label="jump to pc" className="mono h-8 w-48 rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-xs" />
      </div>
      <VirtualList className="mono border-t border-border-soft text-xs" rows={view.disasm.length} scrollTo={target} render={(i) => {
        const d = view.disasm[i]!;
        const line = cLineAt(view.source_map?.pc_to_c, d.pc);
        return (
          <div className={`flex h-5 gap-4 px-3 ${i === target ? 'bg-hl' : ''}`}>
            <span className="w-20 text-text-mute">{hexAddr(d.pc)}</span><span className="w-24 text-text-mute">{hex32(d.word)}</span>
            <span className="flex-1">{d.text}{d.ecall && <span className="text-accent"> ; {d.ecall}</span>}</span>
            <span className="w-16 text-right text-text-mute">{line ? `C:${line}` : ''}</span>
          </div>
        );
      }} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/StagePanel.tsx` (replace): routes the focused stage, shows `StageError` for a failed stage, a "waiting for stage N" note for queued/running ones, keeps the shared `selected` source pc, and renders `RunPanel` for `run` (Task 23; until then a placeholder).
```tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { CompilePanel } from './CompilePanel';
import { AnalysePanel } from './AnalysePanel';
import { TranspilePanel } from './TranspilePanel';
import { ImagePanel } from './ImagePanel';
import { StageError } from './StageError';
import { useStageView } from '@/lib/useStageView';
import type { Stage } from '@/lib/job';
import type { CallForm, CompileView, ImageView, JobView, TranspileView } from '@/lib/types';

export function StagePanel({ stage, job, defaultCall, onFocus }: { stage: Stage; job: JobView | null; defaultCall: CallForm | null; onFocus: (s: Stage) => void }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const compile = useStageView<CompileView>(job?.id, 'compile', job?.compile.status === 'done');
  const transpile = useStageView<TranspileView>(job?.id, 'transpile', job?.transpile.status === 'done');
  const image = useStageView<ImageView>(job?.id, 'image', job?.build.status === 'done');
  if (!job) return <p className="p-6 text-text-mute">Pick a sample or write a contract, then press Compile &amp; Run (⌘/Ctrl + Enter).</p>;
  const failed = (['compile', 'transpile', 'build'] as const).find((s) => job[s].status === 'failed');
  if (failed && job[failed].error) return <StageError title={failed} error={job[failed].error!} />;
  const wait = (what: string) => <p className="p-6 text-text-mute">Waiting for {what}…</p>;
  switch (stage) {
    case 'compile': return compile.data ? <CompilePanel view={compile.data} /> : wait('the compiler');
    case 'analyse': return compile.data ? <AnalysePanel view={compile.data} selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} /> : wait('the compiler');
    case 'transpile': return transpile.data ? <TranspilePanel view={transpile.data} compile={compile.data} selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} /> : wait('the transpiler');
    case 'image': return image.data ? <ImagePanel jobId={job.id} view={image.data} /> : wait('the RV32IM build');
    case 'run': return image.data ? <p className="p-6 text-text-mute">run</p> : wait('the RV32IM build');
  }
}
```

When the job finishes successfully, `Shell` moves the focus to `image` (add a `useEffect` on `state.job?.finished && state.job.build.status === 'done'`).

### Task 23: Run panel: call forms and the cycle debugger

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/replay.ts`, `web/components/{RunPanel.tsx,EvmCallForm.tsx,SbpfCallForm.tsx,Debugger.tsx,Timeline.tsx,Listing.tsx,Registers.tsx,MemoryPanel.tsx,SyscallLog.tsx}`, `web/test/replay.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/components/StagePanel.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api.createRun`, `api.cycles`, `api.state`, `api.memory`, `api.syscalls`; `ImageView` (for the listing and `source_map`); `TranspileView.op_map` and `CompileView` (to show the source op).
- Produces:
  - `web/lib/replay.ts`:
    - `class PageCache { constructor(fetch: (from: number, count: number) => Promise<CycleView[]>, pageSize = 4096); get(clk: number): Promise<CycleView>; peek(clk: number): CycleView | undefined }` (LRU of 32 pages)
    - `nextMatching(cache, from: number, dir: 1 | -1, pred: (c: CycleView) => boolean, total: number, limit = 1 << 20): Promise<number | null>` — scans page by page
    - `changedRegs(prev: number[] | null, next: number[]): Set<number>`
    - `REG_NAMES: string[]` (`zero, ra, sp, …`, same as `rv_disasm`)
    - `evmFormFromAbi(abi: AbiItem[], fn: string, caller: string): EvmCallForm` and `abiSignature(item: AbiItem): string`
  - Debugger controls: ◀◀ start, ◀ −1, ▶ +1, ▶▶ +100, "next syscall", "next source op" (next cycle whose `src_pc` differs: uses `state` lookups, so it steps by up to 64 cycles per request and says so if it gives up), "to end"; keyboard: ←/→ step, Shift+←/→ ±100, `s` next syscall, `o` next source op. The current clk lives in the URL hash (`#clk=1234`) so a position can be shared.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { abiSignature, changedRegs, evmFormFromAbi, nextMatching, PageCache, REG_NAMES } from '@/lib/replay';
import type { CycleView } from '@/lib/types';

const cyc = (clk: number, extra: Partial<CycleView> = {}): CycleView => ({ clk, pc: 0x10000 + 4 * clk, next_pc: 0, word: 0, rd: 0, writes_rd: false, rd_val: 0, mem_read: false, mem_write: false, mem_addr: 0, mem_val: 0, syscall: false, hash_row: false, n_alu: 0, sys_idx: 0xffffffff, ...extra });

function fakeFetch(total: number, syscalls: number[]) {
  return vi.fn(async (from: number, count: number) => Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, Math.min(count, total - from)) }, (_, k) => cyc(from + k, { syscall: syscalls.includes(from + k) })));
}

describe('PageCache', () => {
  it('fetches a page once and serves cycles from it', async () => {
    const f = fakeFetch(10_000, []);
    const c = new PageCache(f, 4096);
    expect((await c.get(5)).clk).toBe(5);
    expect((await c.get(4095)).clk).toBe(4095);
    expect(f).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect((await c.get(4096)).clk).toBe(4096);
    expect(f).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(f.mock.calls[1]).toEqual([4096, 4096]);
    expect(c.peek(4100)?.clk).toBe(4100);
  });
});

describe('nextMatching', () => {
  it('finds the next and previous syscall across pages', async () => {
    const c = new PageCache(fakeFetch(10_000, [100, 5000, 9999]), 4096);
    expect(await nextMatching(c, 100, 1, (x) => x.syscall, 10_000)).toBe(5000);
    expect(await nextMatching(c, 5000, -1, (x) => x.syscall, 10_000)).toBe(100);
    expect(await nextMatching(c, 9999, 1, (x) => x.syscall, 10_000)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('registers', () => {
  it('names and diffs', () => {
    expect(REG_NAMES[10]).toBe('a0');
    expect([...changedRegs([0, 1, 2], [0, 1, 3])]).toEqual([2]);
    expect(changedRegs(null, [1]).size).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('ABI forms', () => {
  const abi = [{ type: 'function', name: 'transfer', inputs: [{ name: 'to', type: 'address' }, { name: 'amount', type: 'uint256' }], stateMutability: 'nonpayable' }];
  it('signs and fills', () => {

    const f = evmFormFromAbi(abi, 'transfer(address,uint256)', '0x11');
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ lang: 'evm', function: 'transfer(address,uint256)', args: ['', ''], caller: '0x11', storage: [] });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `lib/replay.ts`**

```ts
import type { AbiItem, CycleView, EvmCallForm } from './types';

export const REG_NAMES = ['zero', 'ra', 'sp', 'gp', 'tp', 't0', 't1', 't2', 's0', 's1', 'a0', 'a1', 'a2', 'a3', 'a4', 'a5', 'a6', 'a7', 's2', 's3', 's4', 's5', 's6', 's7', 's8', 's9', 's10', 's11', 't3', 't4', 't5', 't6'];

export class PageCache {
  private pages = new Map<number, CycleView[]>();
  private inflight = new Map<number, Promise<CycleView[]>>();
  constructor(private fetchPage: (from: number, count: number) => Promise<CycleView[]>, private size = 4096, private keep = 32) {}
  private async page(p: number): Promise<CycleView[]> {
    const hit = this.pages.get(p);
    if (hit) { this.pages.delete(p); this.pages.set(p, hit); return hit; }
    let f = this.inflight.get(p);
    if (!f) { f = this.fetchPage(p * this.size, this.size); this.inflight.set(p, f); }
    const rows = await f;
    this.inflight.delete(p);
    this.pages.set(p, rows);
    while (this.pages.size > this.keep) this.pages.delete(this.pages.keys().next().value!);
    return rows;
  }
  async get(clk: number): Promise<CycleView> {
    const rows = await this.page(Math.floor(clk / this.size));
    const c = rows[clk % this.size];
    if (!c) throw new Error(`cycle ${clk} is past the end`);
    return c;
  }
  peek(clk: number): CycleView | undefined { return this.pages.get(Math.floor(clk / this.size))?.[clk % this.size]; }
  get pageSize() { return this.size; }
}

export async function nextMatching(cache: PageCache, from: number, dir: 1 | -1, pred: (c: CycleView) => boolean, total: number, limit = 1 << 20): Promise<number | null> {
  for (let clk = from + dir, n = 0; clk >= 0 && clk < total && n < limit; clk += dir, n++) {
    if (pred(await cache.get(clk))) return clk;
  }
  return null;
}

export function changedRegs(prev: number[] | null, next: number[]): Set<number> {
  const s = new Set<number>();
  if (!prev) return s;
  next.forEach((v, i) => { if (prev[i] !== v) s.add(i); });
  return s;
}

export const abiSignature = (a: AbiItem) => `${a.name}(${(a.inputs ?? []).map((p) => p.type).join(',')})`;

export function evmFormFromAbi(abi: AbiItem[], fn: string, caller: string): EvmCallForm {
  const item = abi.find((a) => a.type === 'function' && abiSignature(a) === fn);
  return { lang: 'evm', function: fn, args: (item?.inputs ?? []).map(() => ''), caller, value: '0', gas_limit: 1_000_000, storage: [] };
}
```

`web/components/EvmCallForm.tsx` — function picker (non-view functions first; `view`/`pure` ones too, since they also run), one input per parameter labelled `name: type` (placeholder `0x… or decimal`), caller (default ALICE `0x…1111` × 20 bytes), value, gas limit, and a storage editor: rows of `slot | mapping(base,key) | mapping2(base,key1,key2)` with a value; a short note: "Storage you don't set reads as zero. The terminal finds the storage slots the call touches by dry-running the EVM interpreter, so you never write Merkle witnesses." When the sample supplied a default call, the form starts from it.
```tsx
'use client';
import { abiSignature, evmFormFromAbi } from '@/lib/replay';
import type { AbiItem, EvmCallForm as Form, StorageSeed } from '@/lib/types';

const ALICE = '0x0000000000000000000000001111111111111111111111111111111111111111';
const input = 'mono h-8 w-full rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-xs';

export function EvmCallForm({ abi, value, onChange }: { abi: AbiItem[]; value: Form | null; onChange: (f: Form) => void }) {
  const fns = abi.filter((a) => a.type === 'function');
  const form = value ?? evmFormFromAbi(abi, fns[0] ? abiSignature(fns[0]) : 'f()', ALICE);
  const item = fns.find((a) => abiSignature(a) === form.function);
  const set = (p: Partial<Form>) => onChange({ ...form, ...p });
  const setSeed = (i: number, s: StorageSeed | null) => set({ storage: s ? form.storage.map((x, k) => (k === i ? s : x)) : form.storage.filter((_, k) => k !== i) });
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs">
      <label>function <select className={input} value={form.function} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...evmFormFromAbi(abi, e.target.value, form.caller), storage: form.storage })}>{fns.map((a) => <option key={abiSignature(a)}>{abiSignature(a)}</option>)}</select></label>
      {(item?.inputs ?? []).map((p, i) => <label key={i}>{p.name || `arg${i}`}: <span className="text-text-mute">{p.type}</span><input className={input} value={form.args[i] ?? ''} placeholder="0x… or decimal" onChange={(e) => set({ args: form.args.map((a, k) => (k === i ? e.target.value : a)) })} /></label>)}
      <label>caller <input className={input} value={form.caller} onChange={(e) => set({ caller: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <div className="flex gap-2"><label className="flex-1">value (wei) <input className={input} value={form.value ?? '0'} onChange={(e) => set({ value: e.target.value })} /></label><label className="flex-1">gas limit <input className={input} type="number" value={form.gas_limit ?? 1_000_000} onChange={(e) => set({ gas_limit: Number(e.target.value) })} /></label></div>
      <fieldset className="rounded-sm border border-border-soft p-2"><legend className="px-1 text-text-mute">initial storage</legend>
        {form.storage.map((s, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="mb-1 flex gap-1">
            <select className={`${input} w-28`} value={s.kind} onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, e.target.value === 'slot' ? { kind: 'slot', slot: '0', value: '0' } : e.target.value === 'mapping' ? { kind: 'mapping', base: 0, key: '', value: '0' } : { kind: 'mapping2', base: 0, key1: '', key2: '', value: '0' })}><option value="slot">slot</option><option value="mapping">mapping</option><option value="mapping2">mapping²</option></select>
            {s.kind === 'slot' && <input className={input} value={s.slot} placeholder="slot" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, slot: e.target.value })} />}
            {s.kind !== 'slot' && <input className={`${input} w-14`} type="number" value={s.base} title="base slot" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, base: Number(e.target.value) })} />}
            {s.kind === 'mapping' && <input className={input} value={s.key} placeholder="key" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, key: e.target.value })} />}
            {s.kind === 'mapping2' && <><input className={input} value={s.key1} placeholder="key 1" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, key1: e.target.value })} /><input className={input} value={s.key2} placeholder="key 2" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, key2: e.target.value })} /></>}
            <input className={`${input} w-28`} value={s.value} placeholder="value" onChange={(e) => setSeed(i, { ...s, value: e.target.value })} />
            <button aria-label="remove" className="px-2 text-text-mute" onClick={() => setSeed(i, null)}>×</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <button className="text-accent" onClick={() => set({ storage: [...form.storage, { kind: 'slot', slot: '0', value: '0' }] })}>+ add</button>
        <p className="mt-1 text-text-mute">Storage you don’t set reads as zero. The terminal finds the slots the call touches by dry-running the EVM interpreter, so you never write Merkle witnesses.</p>
      </fieldset>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/SbpfCallForm.tsx` — program id; an accounts table (key, owner, lamports, data hex, signer / writable / executable checkboxes, add/remove rows); instruction data hex; same `input` styles. Starts from the sample's default call or one empty writable account.
```tsx
'use client';
import type { AccountForm, SbpfCallForm as Form } from '@/lib/types';
const input = 'mono h-8 w-full rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-xs';
const blank: AccountForm = { key: '', owner: '', lamports: 0, data: '0x', is_signer: false, is_writable: true, executable: false };

export function SbpfCallForm({ value, onChange }: { value: Form | null; onChange: (f: Form) => void }) {
  const form = value ?? { lang: 'sbpf', program_id: '', accounts: [blank], data: '0x' };
  const setAcc = (i: number, a: AccountForm | null) => onChange({ ...form, accounts: a ? form.accounts.map((x, k) => (k === i ? a : x)) : form.accounts.filter((_, k) => k !== i) });
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs">
      <label>program id (base58) <input className={input} value={form.program_id} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...form, program_id: e.target.value })} /></label>
      {form.accounts.map((a, i) => (
        <fieldset key={i} className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 rounded-sm border border-border-soft p-2"><legend className="px-1 text-text-mute">account {i}</legend>
          <input className={input} value={a.key} placeholder="key" onChange={(e) => setAcc(i, { ...a, key: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input} value={a.owner} placeholder="owner" onChange={(e) => setAcc(i, { ...a, owner: e.target.value })} />
          <input className={input} type="number" value={a.lamports} placeholder="lamports" onChange={(e) => setAcc(i, { ...a, lamports: Number(e.target.value) })} />
          <input className={input} value={a.data} placeholder="data (hex)" onChange={(e) => setAcc(i, { ...a, data: e.target.value })} />
          <div className="col-span-2 flex gap-3">{(['is_signer', 'is_writable', 'executable'] as const).map((k) => <label key={k}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!a[k]} onChange={(e) => setAcc(i, { ...a, [k]: e.target.checked })} /> {k.replace('is_', '')}</label>)}
            <button className="ml-auto text-text-mute" onClick={() => setAcc(i, null)}>remove</button></div>
        </fieldset>
      ))}
      <button className="self-start text-accent" onClick={() => onChange({ ...form, accounts: [...form.accounts, blank] })}>+ account</button>
      <label>instruction data (hex) <input className={input} value={form.data} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...form, data: e.target.value })} /></label>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/RunPanel.tsx` — the form (EVM or sBPF by job language), a Run button, then the summary strip and the `Debugger` for the latest run. Summary strip: `cycles`, `halted`/`trap` (trap in `text-negative` with its text), status word (`out[0]`: 1 success, 0 revert, 2 error, with that label), the eight output words, input/public word counts, and the witness-discovery steps as "found storage slot 0x… after run n". A trapped run shows the summary and no debugger.
```tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { groupDigits } from '@/lib/format';
import { EvmCallForm } from './EvmCallForm';
import { SbpfCallForm } from './SbpfCallForm';
import { Debugger } from './Debugger';
import { TablesPanel } from './TablesPanel';
import type { CallForm, CompileView, EvmCallForm as EF, ImageView, JobView, RunSummary, SbpfCallForm as SF, TranspileView } from '@/lib/types';

const STATUS: Record<number, string> = { 1: 'success', 0: 'reverted', 2: 'error' };

export function RunPanel({ job, compile, transpile, image, defaultCall }: { job: JobView; compile: CompileView | null; transpile: TranspileView | null; image: ImageView; defaultCall: CallForm | null }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<CallForm | null>(defaultCall);
  const [run, setRun] = useState<RunSummary | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'debugger' | 'tables'>('debugger');
  const evm = job.lang === 'solidity';
  const go = async () => {
    if (!form) return;
    setBusy(true); setErr(null);
    try { setRun(await api.createRun(job.id, { call: form })); } catch (e) { setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)); } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <details open={!run} className="border-b border-border-soft p-4">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm text-text-soft">Call</summary>
        <div className="mt-3">
          {evm ? <EvmCallForm abi={compile?.lang === 'evm' ? compile.abi : []} value={form as EF | null} onChange={setForm} /> : <SbpfCallForm value={form as SF | null} onChange={setForm} />}
          <button onClick={go} disabled={busy || !form} className="mt-3 h-8 rounded-sm bg-accent px-4 text-on-accent disabled:opacity-50">{busy ? 'Running…' : 'Run on the zkVM'}</button>
          {err && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-xs text-negative">{err}</p>}
        </div>
      </details>
      {run && (
        <>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-6 gap-y-1 border-b border-border-soft px-4 py-2 text-xs">
            <span><span className="text-text-mute">cycles</span> <span className="mono">{groupDigits(run.cycles)}</span></span>
            {run.trap ? <span className="text-negative">trap: {run.trap}</span> : <span className={run.status === 1 ? 'text-positive' : 'text-warning'}>{STATUS[run.status] ?? `status ${run.status}`}</span>}
            <span><span className="text-text-mute">tier</span> <span className="mono">{run.tier ?? 'none fits'}</span></span>
            <span className="mono text-text-soft" title="the eight public output words">out {run.outputs.join(' ')}</span>
            <span className="text-text-mute">{groupDigits(run.input_words)} input words{run.public_words ? ` · ${groupDigits(run.public_words)} public` : ''}</span>
            {run.discovery.map((d) => <span key={d.run} className="text-text-mute">found storage slot <span className="mono">{d.slot.slice(0, 10)}…</span> on dry run {d.run + 1}</span>)}
          </div>
          {!run.trap && (
            <>
              <div className="flex gap-1 px-4 pt-2" role="tablist">{(['debugger', 'tables'] as const).map((t) => <button key={t} role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t} onClick={() => setTab(t)} className={`h-8 rounded-sm px-3 text-xs ${tab === t ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft'}`}>{t === 'debugger' ? 'Step through' : 'STARK tables'}</button>)}</div>
              <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">{tab === 'debugger' ? <Debugger run={run} image={image} transpile={transpile} compile={compile} /> : <TablesPanel run={run} />}</div>
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/Timeline.tsx` — a full-width bar (height 28 px) for `0 … cycles`: the syscall ticks drawn as 1 px marks coloured by kind (input/public reads in `--text-mute`, hash calls in `--accent`, writes and halt in `--positive`), a cursor at the current clk, click or drag to seek, a tooltip with the clk under the pointer, and a numeric clk input beside it.
```tsx
'use client';
import { useRef } from 'react';
import type { SyscallView } from '@/lib/types';

const TICK: Record<string, string> = { read_input: 'var(--text-mute)', read_public: 'var(--text-mute)', poseidon2: 'var(--accent)', poseidon2_len: 'var(--accent)', keccak: 'var(--accent)', sha256: 'var(--accent)', write_output: 'var(--positive)', halt: 'var(--positive)' };

export function Timeline({ total, clk, syscalls, onSeek }: { total: number; clk: number; syscalls: SyscallView[]; onSeek: (c: number) => void }) {
  const el = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const seek = (x: number) => { const r = el.current!.getBoundingClientRect(); onSeek(Math.max(0, Math.min(total - 1, Math.round(((x - r.left) / r.width) * (total - 1))))); };
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2">
      <div ref={el} role="slider" aria-label="cycle" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={total - 1} aria-valuenow={clk} tabIndex={0}
        onPointerDown={(e) => { e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); seek(e.clientX); }} onPointerMove={(e) => e.buttons && seek(e.clientX)}
        className="relative h-7 flex-1 cursor-pointer rounded-sm bg-surface-2">
        <svg className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full" preserveAspectRatio="none" viewBox={`0 0 ${Math.max(total, 1)} 28`}>
          {syscalls.map((s, i) => <rect key={i} x={s.clk} y={4} width={Math.max(1, total / 800)} height={20} fill={TICK[s.kind] ?? 'var(--text-mute)'} />)}
        </svg>
        <div className="absolute top-0 h-full w-0.5 bg-text-strong" style={{ left: `${(clk / Math.max(total - 1, 1)) * 100}%` }} />
      </div>
      <input aria-label="go to cycle" type="number" min={0} max={total - 1} value={clk} onChange={(e) => onSeek(Math.max(0, Math.min(total - 1, Number(e.target.value))))} className="mono h-8 w-28 rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-xs" />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/Registers.tsx` — 32 registers in a 4-column grid: `name`, value in hex, decimal on hover; changed-since-previous in `bg-hl` with the old value in the tooltip; the register the current instruction writes outlined.
```tsx
'use client';
import { REG_NAMES } from '@/lib/replay';
import { hex32 } from '@/lib/format';

export function Registers({ regs, changed, writes }: { regs: number[]; changed: Set<number>; writes: number | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 p-3 mono text-xs" aria-label="registers">
      {regs.map((v, i) => (
        <div key={i} title={`x${i} = ${v >>> 0}`} className={`flex justify-between rounded-sm px-1 ${changed.has(i) ? 'bg-hl' : ''} ${writes === i ? 'outline outline-1 outline-accent' : ''}`}>
          <span className="text-text-mute">{REG_NAMES[i]}</span><span>{hex32(v)}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/MemoryPanel.tsx` — the current cycle's access, and a 64-word window at a word address (byte addresses shown via `byteAddr`; the emulator addresses RAM in words):
```tsx
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { byteAddr, hex32, hexAddr } from '@/lib/format';
import type { StateView } from '@/lib/types';

export function MemoryPanel({ rid, state }: { rid: string; state: StateView }) {
  const c = state.cycle;
  const touched = c.mem_read || c.mem_write;
  const [base, setBase] = useState<number>(0);
  const [words, setWords] = useState<number[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => { if (touched) setBase(c.mem_addr & ~15); }, [touched, c.mem_addr]);
  useEffect(() => { let live = true; api.memory(rid, state.clk, base, 64).then((m) => live && setWords(m.words)); return () => { live = false; }; }, [rid, state.clk, base]);
  return (
    <section className="border-t border-border-soft p-3 text-xs" aria-label="memory">
      <p className="mono">{touched ? <>{c.mem_write ? 'store' : 'load'} {hexAddr(byteAddr(c.mem_addr))} {c.mem_write ? '←' : '→'} {hex32(c.mem_val)}</> : <span className="text-text-mute">no memory access this cycle</span>}</p>
      <label className="mt-2 block text-text-mute">window at byte address <input className="mono ml-1 h-7 w-32 rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-2" defaultValue={hexAddr(byteAddr(base))} key={base}
        onBlur={(e) => { const v = parseInt(e.target.value.replace(/^0x/, ''), 16); if (Number.isFinite(v)) setBase((v >>> 2) & ~15); }} /></label>
      <div className="mono mt-2 grid grid-cols-[auto_repeat(4,1fr)] gap-x-3">
        {Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, r) => (
          <div key={r} className="contents">
            <span className="text-text-mute">{hexAddr(byteAddr(base + r * 4))}</span>
            {words.slice(r * 4, r * 4 + 4).map((w, k) => <span key={k} className={touched && base + r * 4 + k === c.mem_addr ? 'bg-hl' : ''}>{hex32(w)}</span>)}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/SyscallLog.tsx` — the syscall list with the current one highlighted (click to seek), and the eight output words as they stand at the current cycle:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { SyscallView } from '@/lib/types';

export function SyscallLog({ syscalls, clk, onSeek }: { syscalls: SyscallView[]; clk: number; onSeek: (c: number) => void }) {
  const outputs = useMemo(() => {
    const o: (number | null)[] = Array(8).fill(null);
    for (const s of syscalls) { if (s.clk > clk) break; const m = /^out\[(\d)\] = (\d+)$/.exec(s.detail); if (s.kind === 'write_output' && m) o[Number(m[1])] = Number(m[2]); }
    return o;
  }, [syscalls, clk]);
  const current = syscalls.findLastIndex((s) => s.clk <= clk);
  return (
    <section className="border-t border-border-soft p-3 text-xs" aria-label="syscalls">
      <p className="mono text-text-soft">out {outputs.map((w) => (w == null ? '·' : w)).join(' ')}</p>
      <ol className="mono mt-2 max-h-48 overflow-auto">
        {syscalls.map((s, i) => (
          <li key={i}><button onClick={() => onSeek(s.clk)} className={`w-full text-left ${i === current ? 'bg-hl' : ''}`}><span className="text-text-mute">{s.clk}</span> {s.kind} <span className="text-text-mute">{s.detail}</span></button></li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/components/Listing.tsx` — the three levels for the current cycle, each labelled, so the source-op → C → RV32 chain is visible at once:
```tsx
'use client';
import { cLineAt } from './ImagePanel';
import { hexAddr } from '@/lib/format';
import type { CompileView, ImageView, StateView, TranspileView } from '@/lib/types';

function srcText(compile: CompileView | null, pc: number | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (compile == null || pc == null) return null;
  if (compile.lang === 'evm') { const o = compile.opcodes.find((x) => x.pc === pc); return o ? `0x${pc.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}  ${o.mnemonic}${o.push_hex ? ` 0x${o.push_hex}` : ''}` : null; }
  for (const f of compile.functions) for (const b of f.blocks) { const i = b.insns.find((x) => x.pc === pc); if (i) return `${pc}  ${i.text}`; }
  return null;
}

export function Listing({ state, image, transpile, compile }: { state: StateView; image: ImageView; transpile: TranspileView | null; compile: CompileView | null }) {
  const idx = Math.floor((state.cycle.pc - image.base_pc) / 4);
  const rv = image.disasm.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 8), idx + 9);
  const cLines = transpile?.c_source.split('\n') ?? [];
  const line = state.c_line ?? cLineAt(image.source_map?.pc_to_c, state.cycle.pc);
  const src = srcText(compile, state.src_pc);
  const label = compile?.lang === 'sbpf' ? 'sBPF instruction' : 'EVM opcode';
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-3 text-xs">
      <section><h3 className="text-text-mute">{label}</h3><p className="mono text-sm text-text-strong">{src ?? <span className="text-text-mute">no source map for this build</span>}</p></section>
      <section><h3 className="text-text-mute">generated C{line ? ` · line ${line}` : ''}</h3>
        {line ? <pre className="mono">{cLines.slice(Math.max(0, line - 4), line + 3).map((l, k) => { const n = Math.max(0, line - 4) + k + 1; return <div key={n} className={n === line ? 'bg-hl' : 'text-text-soft'}><span className="inline-block w-10 text-right text-text-mute">{n}</span>  {l}</div>; })}</pre>
          : <p className="text-text-mute">no source map for this build</p>}</section>
      <section><h3 className="text-text-mute">RV32IM · cycle {state.clk}</h3>
        <pre className="mono">{rv.map((d) => <div key={d.pc} className={d.pc === state.cycle.pc ? 'bg-hl text-text-strong' : 'text-text-soft'}><span className="inline-block w-20 text-text-mute">{hexAddr(d.pc)}</span>{d.text}{d.ecall && <span className="text-accent"> ; {d.ecall}</span>}</div>)}</pre></section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/components/Debugger.tsx`:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { changedRegs, nextMatching, PageCache } from '@/lib/replay';
import { Timeline } from './Timeline';
import { Registers } from './Registers';
import { Listing } from './Listing';
import { MemoryPanel } from './MemoryPanel';
import { SyscallLog } from './SyscallLog';
import type { CompileView, ImageView, RunSummary, StateView, SyscallView, TranspileView } from '@/lib/types';

export function Debugger({ run, image, transpile, compile }: { run: RunSummary; image: ImageView; transpile: TranspileView | null; compile: CompileView | null }) {
  const total = run.cycles;
  const cache = useMemo(() => new PageCache((f, c) => api.cycles(run.rid, f, c).then((p) => p.cycles)), [run.rid]);
  const [clk, setClk] = useState(() => { const m = /clk=(\d+)/.exec(typeof location !== 'undefined' ? location.hash : ''); return m ? Math.min(Number(m[1]), total - 1) : 0; });
  const [state, setState] = useState<StateView | null>(null);
  const prev = useRef<number[] | null>(null);
  const [changed, setChanged] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [syscalls, setSyscalls] = useState<SyscallView[]>([]);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => { api.syscalls(run.rid).then(setSyscalls); }, [run.rid]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    api.state(run.rid, clk).then((s) => { if (!live) return; setChanged(changedRegs(prev.current, s.regs)); prev.current = s.regs; setState(s); });
    history.replaceState(null, '', `#clk=${clk}`);
    return () => { live = false; };
  }, [run.rid, clk]);

  const seek = useCallback((c: number) => { setNote(null); setClk(Math.max(0, Math.min(total - 1, c))); }, [total]);
  const nextSyscall = useCallback(async (dir: 1 | -1) => { const c = await nextMatching(cache, clk, dir, (x) => x.syscall, total); c == null ? setNote('no further syscall') : seek(c); }, [cache, clk, total, seek]);
  const nextOp = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!state?.src_pc && state?.src_pc !== 0) { setNote('no source map for this build'); return; }
    for (let c = clk + 1, n = 0; c < total && n < 64; c++, n++) {
      const s = await api.state(run.rid, c);
      if (s.src_pc != null && s.src_pc !== state.src_pc) { seek(c); return; }
    }
    setNote('the next source op is more than 64 cycles away; use the timeline');
  }, [state, clk, total, run.rid, seek]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName === 'INPUT') return;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') seek(clk + (e.shiftKey ? 100 : 1));
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') seek(clk - (e.shiftKey ? 100 : 1));
      else if (e.key === 's') void nextSyscall(e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
      else if (e.key === 'o') void nextOp();
      else return;
      e.preventDefault();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [clk, seek, nextSyscall, nextOp]);

  const btn = 'h-8 rounded-sm px-2 text-xs text-text-soft hover:bg-surface';
  return (
    <div className="grid h-full grid-rows-[auto_auto_1fr]">
      <Timeline total={total} clk={clk} syscalls={syscalls} onSeek={seek} />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 px-4 pb-2" role="toolbar" aria-label="step controls">
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(0)} title="start">⏮</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(clk - 100)} title="−100 (Shift+←)">−100</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(clk - 1)} title="−1 (←)">◀</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(clk + 1)} title="+1 (→)">▶</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(clk + 100)} title="+100 (Shift+→)">+100</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => nextSyscall(1)} title="next syscall (s)">next syscall</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={nextOp} title="next source op (o)">next source op</button>
        <button className={btn} onClick={() => seek(total - 1)} title="end">⏭</button>
        {note && <span className="text-xs text-text-mute">{note}</span>}
      </div>
      {state && (
        <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.3fr)_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-0 border-t border-border-soft">
          <div className="min-h-0 overflow-auto border-r border-border-soft"><Listing state={state} image={image} transpile={transpile} compile={compile} /></div>
          <div className="min-h-0 overflow-auto">
            <Registers regs={state.regs} changed={changed} writes={state.cycle.writes_rd ? state.cycle.rd : null} />
            <MemoryPanel rid={run.rid} state={state} />
            <SyscallLog syscalls={syscalls} clk={clk} onSeek={seek} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Wire `RunPanel` into `StagePanel`'s `run` case:

### Task 24: STARK tables panel

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/tables.ts`, `web/components/TablesPanel.tsx` (replace the stub), `web/test/tables.test.ts`

Load the `dataviz` skill before writing the chart: it sets the palette, the bar spec and the light/dark rules this panel follows.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `tableBars(run: RunSummary, tier: number): { key: string; label: string; used: number; capacity: number; fill: number; over: boolean; explain: string }[]` for `cpu`, `memory`, `alu`, `poseidon2`, `keccak`, `sha256` (keccak/sha256 omitted when unused); `capacity` from `run.tiers` for that tier (`memory` uses `memory_max`); `fill = used / capacity`.
  - `cpuTotal(run) = run.rows.cpu + run.rows.digest_rows` — the CPU-table budget includes the digest rows, as `Tier::for_workload` counts them.
  - `<TablesPanel run={run} />`: a tier selector (10–20; tiers the run does not fit are disabled with "does not fit" as their text), and one horizontal bar per table: label, `used / capacity` in tabular figures, the bar (accent fill, `--negative` when over), and a one-line explanation:
    - cpu: "one row per executed cycle, plus the rows that hash the program, inputs and public inputs"
    - memory: "one row per register or RAM access; sorted by address to prove every read returns the last write"
    - alu: "one row per arithmetic event the CPU delegates (multiplies, divides, shifts, comparisons)"
    - poseidon2: "permutations for the digests and for every POSEIDON2 syscall block"
    - keccak / sha256: "one permutation (compression) per KECCAK (SHA256) syscall"
  - A sentence above the bars: "This run needs tier T: the smallest whose CPU table holds N cycles and whose Poseidon2 table holds P permutations. Proving cost grows with the tier." (or "No tier fits: …" when `run.tier` is null).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`web/test/tables.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { cpuTotal, tableBars } from '@/lib/tables';
import type { RunSummary } from '@/lib/types';

const tier = (t: number, fits: boolean) => ({ tier: t, cpu: 2 ** t, alu: 2 ** (t + 1), memory_min: 2 ** (t + 2), memory_max: 2 ** 24, poseidon2_permutations: 2 ** (t + 2) / 32, keccak_permutations: 2 ** (t + 5) / 32, sha256_compressions: 2 ** Math.min(t + 6, 20) / 64, fits });
const run = { rid: 'r', cycles: 66235, halted: true, outputs: [1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], status: 1, tier: 18,
  rows: { cpu: 66235, memory: 200000, alu: 30000, poseidon2_permutations: 4000, keccak_permutations: 0, sha256_compressions: 0, digest_rows: 3200 },
  tiers: [10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20].map((t) => tier(t, t >= 18)), syscall_counts: [], hot_pcs: [], input_words: 921, public_words: 0, discovery: [] } as RunSummary;

describe('tableBars', () => {
  it('fills against the chosen tier and drops unused tables', () => {
    const b = tableBars(run, 18);
    expect(b.map((x) => x.key)).toEqual(['cpu', 'memory', 'alu', 'poseidon2']);
    const cpu = b[0]!;
    expect(cpu.used).toBe(cpuTotal(run));
    expect(cpu.capacity).toBe(2 ** 18);
    expect(cpu.fill).toBeCloseTo((66235 + 3200) / 2 ** 18);
    expect(cpu.over).toBe(false);
  });
  it('flags overflow at a smaller tier', () => {
    const cpu = tableBars(run, 16)[0]!;
    expect(cpu.over).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

Run: `cd web && npx vitest run test/tables.test.ts 2>&1 | tail -3`

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `lib/tables.ts` and the panel**

`web/lib/tables.ts`:
```ts
import type { RunSummary } from './types';

export const cpuTotal = (r: RunSummary) => r.rows.cpu + r.rows.digest_rows;

const EXPLAIN: Record<string, string> = {
  cpu: 'one row per executed cycle, plus the rows that hash the program, inputs and public inputs',
  memory: 'one row per register or RAM access; sorted by address to prove every read returns the last write',
  alu: 'one row per arithmetic event the CPU delegates (multiplies, divides, shifts, comparisons)',
  poseidon2: 'permutations for the digests and for every POSEIDON2 syscall block',
  keccak: 'one permutation per KECCAK syscall',
  sha256: 'one compression per SHA256 syscall',
};

export function tableBars(r: RunSummary, tier: number) {
  const t = r.tiers.find((x) => x.tier === tier);
  if (!t) return [];
  const rows: [string, string, number, number][] = [
    ['cpu', 'CPU', cpuTotal(r), t.cpu],
    ['memory', 'Memory', r.rows.memory, t.memory_max],
    ['alu', 'ALU', r.rows.alu, t.alu],
    ['poseidon2', 'Poseidon2', r.rows.poseidon2_permutations, t.poseidon2_permutations],
    ['keccak', 'Keccak', r.rows.keccak_permutations, t.keccak_permutations],
    ['sha256', 'SHA-256', r.rows.sha256_compressions, t.sha256_compressions],
  ];
  return rows.filter(([k, , used]) => used > 0 || ['cpu', 'memory', 'alu', 'poseidon2'].includes(k))
    .map(([key, label, used, capacity]) => ({ key, label, used, capacity, fill: used / capacity, over: used > capacity, explain: EXPLAIN[key]! }));
}
```

`web/components/TablesPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { cpuTotal, tableBars } from '@/lib/tables';
import { groupDigits } from '@/lib/format';
import type { RunSummary } from '@/lib/types';

export function TablesPanel({ run }: { run: RunSummary }) {
  const [tier, setTier] = useState(run.tier ?? 20);
  const bars = tableBars(run, tier);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-text-soft">{run.tier != null
        ? <>This run needs <strong className="text-text-strong">tier {run.tier}</strong>: the smallest whose CPU table holds {groupDigits(cpuTotal(run))} rows and whose Poseidon2 table holds {groupDigits(run.rows.poseidon2_permutations)} permutations. Proving cost grows with the tier.</>
        : <>No tier fits this run: it needs {groupDigits(cpuTotal(run))} CPU rows and {groupDigits(run.rows.poseidon2_permutations)} Poseidon2 permutations.</>}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-1" role="radiogroup" aria-label="tier">
        {run.tiers.map((t) => (
          <button key={t.tier} role="radio" aria-checked={tier === t.tier} onClick={() => setTier(t.tier)}
            className={`h-8 rounded-sm px-3 text-xs ${tier === t.tier ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text-strong' : t.fits ? 'text-text-soft' : 'text-text-mute line-through'}`}
            title={t.fits ? `tier ${t.tier}` : `tier ${t.tier}: does not fit`}>{t.tier}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {bars.map((b) => (
          <li key={b.key}>
            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between text-xs"><span className="text-text-strong">{b.label}</span>
              <span className={`mono tabular-nums ${b.over ? 'text-negative' : 'text-text-soft'}`}>{groupDigits(b.used)} / {groupDigits(b.capacity)}{b.over ? ' — over' : ''}</span></div>
            <div className="mt-1 h-2 rounded-full bg-surface-2" role="meter" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={b.capacity} aria-valuenow={b.used} aria-label={b.label}>
              <div className={`h-2 rounded-full ${b.over ? 'bg-negative' : 'bg-accent'}`} style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, b.fill * 100)}%` }} />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-mute">{b.explain}</p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {run.hot_pcs.length > 0 && <details className="text-xs"><summary className="cursor-pointer text-text-soft">Hottest instructions</summary>
        <ol className="mono mt-2">{run.hot_pcs.slice(0, 10).map(([pc, n]) => <li key={pc}>0x{pc.toString(16)} <span className="text-text-mute">× {groupDigits(n)}</span></li>)}</ol></details>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Memory's capacity is the table's maximum height (`2^24`); its minimum (`memory_min`) is the padded floor and is not a limit, so it is not drawn.

### Task 25: End-to-end tests (recorded API, and live)

**Files:**
- Create: `api/crates/terminal-api/tests/record.rs`, `web/e2e/{mock-api.mjs,smoke.spec.ts,live.spec.ts,fixtures/}`, `web/playwright.config.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every API route; every panel.
- Produces: `npm run e2e` (hermetic: a Node mock serving recorded JSON) and `TERMINAL_E2E_LIVE=1 npm run e2e -- live.spec.ts` (against a running API with the sandbox image).

- [ ] **Step 1: Record the fixtures from the real API (fake sandbox)**

`api/crates/terminal-api/tests/record.rs`:
```rust
//! Writes the JSON the hermetic Playwright suite replays. Runs only with TERMINAL_RECORD=<dir>.
mod common;
use common::{fake::*, *};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

fn put(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str, v: &Value) { std::fs::write(dir.join(name), serde_json::to_vec(v).unwrap()).unwrap(); }

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn record_e2e_fixtures() {
    let Ok(out) = std::env::var("TERMINAL_RECORD") else { eprintln!("skipping: TERMINAL_RECORD unset"); return };
    let out = std::path::PathBuf::from(out);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&out).unwrap();
    let (app, _s, _d) = test_app(fake_runner());
    let get = |p: String| { let app = app.clone(); async move { call(&app, json_req("GET", &p, None)).await.2 } };
    put(&out, "samples.json", &get("/api/v1/samples".into()).await);
    let sample = get("/api/v1/samples/erc20".into()).await;
    put(&out, "sample-erc20.json", &sample);
    let (_, _, v) = call(&app, json_req("POST", "/api/v1/jobs", Some(json!({"lang": "solidity", "files": [{"name": "ERC20.sol", "content": sample["files"][0]["content"]}], "options": {"contract": "ERC20", "chain_id": 13, "stage": 2}})))).await;
    let id = v["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
    put(&out, "job.json", &wait_job(&app, &id).await);
    for s in ["compile", "transpile", "image"] { put(&out, &format!("{s}.json"), &get(format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/{s}")).await); }
    let form = serde_json::to_value(terminal_core::evm_call::erc20_transfer_form()).unwrap();
    let mut call_ = form.clone(); call_["lang"] = json!("evm");
    let (_, _, sum) = call(&app, json_req("POST", &format!("/api/v1/jobs/{id}/runs"), Some(json!({"call": call_})))).await;
    put(&out, "run.json", &sum);
    let rid = sum["rid"].as_str().unwrap();
    put(&out, "cycles-0.json", &get(format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/cycles?from=0&count=4096")).await);
    let sys = get(format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/syscalls")).await;
    put(&out, "syscalls.json", &sys);
    let mut at: Vec<u64> = (0..32).collect();
    at.extend(sys.as_array().unwrap().iter().take(64).map(|s| s["clk"].as_u64().unwrap()));
    let mut states = serde_json::Map::new();
    let mut mem = serde_json::Map::new();
    for a in at {
        let st = get(format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/state?at={a}")).await;
        let base = (st["cycle"]["mem_addr"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0)) & !15;
        mem.insert(a.to_string(), get(format!("/api/v1/runs/{rid}/memory?at={a}&addr={base}&words=64")).await);
        states.insert(a.to_string(), st);
    }
    put(&out, "states.json", &Value::Object(states));
    put(&out, "memory.json", &Value::Object(mem));
}
```

The job uses chain id 13 so the fake build's image (built at 13) agrees with `contract.c`.
