- [ ] **Step 2: The mock API**

`web/e2e/mock-api.mjs`:
```js
// Serves the recorded API responses for the hermetic Playwright suite (port 8799).
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const F = (n) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL(`./fixtures/${n}`, import.meta.url)));
const job = F('job.json'), run = F('run.json'), states = F('states.json'), memory = F('memory.json');
const send = (res, code, body) => { res.writeHead(code, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); res.end(JSON.stringify(body)); };
createServer((req, res) => {
  const u = new URL(req.url, 'http://x'); const p = u.pathname.replace(/^\/api\/v1/, '');
  if (p === '/samples') return send(res, 200, F('samples.json'));
  if (p === '/samples/erc20') return send(res, 200, F('sample-erc20.json'));
  if (req.method === 'POST' && p === '/jobs') return send(res, 201, { id: job.id });
  if (p === `/jobs/${job.id}`) return send(res, 200, job);
  if (p === `/jobs/${job.id}/events`) { res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' }); res.end(`event: job\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(job)}\n\nevent: done\ndata: {}\n\n`); return; }
  for (const s of ['compile', 'transpile', 'image']) if (p === `/jobs/${job.id}/${s}`) return send(res, 200, F(`${s}.json`));
  if (req.method === 'POST' && p === `/jobs/${job.id}/runs`) return send(res, 201, run);
  if (p === `/runs/${run.rid}`) return send(res, 200, run);
  if (p === `/runs/${run.rid}/cycles`) return send(res, 200, F('cycles-0.json'));
  if (p === `/runs/${run.rid}/syscalls`) return send(res, 200, F('syscalls.json'));
  if (p === `/runs/${run.rid}/state`) { const s = states[u.searchParams.get('at')]; return s ? send(res, 200, s) : send(res, 404, { error: 'not_found', message: 'not recorded' }); }
  if (p === `/runs/${run.rid}/memory`) { const m = memory[u.searchParams.get('at')]; return m ? send(res, 200, m) : send(res, 404, { error: 'not_found', message: 'not recorded' }); }
  send(res, 404, { error: 'not_found', message: p });
}).listen(8799, '127.0.0.1');
```

    {
      // The standalone server the droplet runs; rewrites are fixed at build time, so the API URL is a build input.
      command: 'npm run build && cp -r .next/static .next/standalone/.next/ && node .next/standalone/server.js',
      port: 3002,
      env: { TERMINAL_API_URL: live ? (process.env.TERMINAL_API_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8080') : 'http://127.0.0.1:8799', PORT: '3002', HOSTNAME: '127.0.0.1' },
      timeout: 300_000,
      reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    },
  ],
});

### Task 26: Deployment for randterminal.org

**Files:**
- Create: `deploy/{deploy.sh,nginx.conf,terminal-api.service,terminal-web.service,cloud-init.yaml,api.env.example}`, `deploy/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the built api binary, web standalone build, `sandbox/build.sh`.
- Produces: `deploy/deploy.sh <droplet-ip>` that rsyncs the tree, builds the sandbox image (only when the circuits revision changed), the api and the web app on the box, swaps them in, restarts both services and checks health.

Provisioning the droplet, pointing DNS at it and issuing the certificate are outward-facing: **stop and ask the user** for the droplet (or permission to create one) and for the DNS change before running Step 5. Everything before Step 5 is files in the repo.

- [ ] **Step 1: cloud-init and the units**

`deploy/cloud-init.yaml`:
```yaml
#cloud-config
# First boot of the randterminal.org droplet: web, a `terminal` user that owns the app and may run
# containers, Docker for the sandbox, Node 22 and Rust 1.98.1 to build on the box.
package_update: true
packages: [nginx, ufw, rsync, build-essential, clang, pkg-config, libssl-dev, git, docker.io, certbot, python3-certbot-nginx, fail2ban, unattended-upgrades]
users:
  - default
  - name: terminal
    shell: /bin/bash
    groups: [docker]
write_files:
  - path: /etc/sudoers.d/terminal
    permissions: '0440'
    content: |
      terminal ALL=(root) NOPASSWD: /usr/bin/systemctl restart terminal-api terminal-web
runcmd:
  - ufw default deny incoming
  - ufw allow 22/tcp
  - ufw allow 80/tcp
  - ufw allow 443/tcp
  - ufw --force enable
  - mkdir -p /opt/terminal/src /var/lib/terminal /etc/terminal /home/terminal/.ssh
  - cp /root/.ssh/authorized_keys /home/terminal/.ssh/authorized_keys
  - chown -R terminal:terminal /home/terminal/.ssh /opt/terminal /var/lib/terminal
  - chmod 700 /home/terminal/.ssh && chmod 600 /home/terminal/.ssh/authorized_keys
  - visudo -cf /etc/sudoers.d/terminal || rm -f /etc/sudoers.d/terminal
  - sed -i 's/^#\?PasswordAuthentication .*/PasswordAuthentication no/; s/^#\?PermitRootLogin .*/PermitRootLogin prohibit-password/' /etc/ssh/sshd_config
  - systemctl reload ssh
  - curl -fsSL https://deb.nodesource.com/setup_22.x | bash -
  - apt-get install -y nodejs
  - su - terminal -c "curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y --default-toolchain 1.98.1 --profile minimal"
```

Membership of the `docker` group is root-equivalent on the host. The api never runs user code itself, and every container it starts is locked down (Task 13), but a compromise of the api process is a compromise of the box: keep the droplet single-purpose and hold no other secrets on it. Rootless Docker is the follow-up if that is not acceptable.

`deploy/terminal-api.service`:
```ini
[Unit]
Description=Rand Terminal API
After=network-online.target docker.service
Requires=docker.service

[Service]
User=terminal
EnvironmentFile=/etc/terminal/api.env
ExecStart=/opt/terminal/bin/terminal-api
Restart=always
RestartSec=5
Environment=RUST_LOG=info
NoNewPrivileges=true
ProtectSystem=strict
ProtectHome=true
PrivateTmp=true
ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/terminal

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

`deploy/terminal-web.service`:
```ini
[Unit]
Description=Rand Terminal web (Next.js)
After=network-online.target terminal-api.service

[Service]
User=terminal
WorkingDirectory=/opt/terminal/web
ExecStart=/usr/bin/node server.js
Restart=always
RestartSec=5
Environment=NODE_ENV=production
Environment=PORT=3002
Environment=HOSTNAME=127.0.0.1
NoNewPrivileges=true
ProtectSystem=strict
ProtectHome=true
PrivateTmp=true

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

TERMINAL_SAMPLES=/opt/terminal/samples
TERMINAL_SANDBOX_IMAGE=terminal-sandbox:9bf3844
TERMINAL_MAX_PROGRAM_WORDS=65535
TERMINAL_MAX_CONCURRENT_BUILDS=2
TERMINAL_MAX_CONCURRENT_RUNS=4
TERMINAL_DATA_CAP_GB=20

- [ ] **Step 2: nginx**

`deploy/nginx.conf`:
```nginx
limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=tapi:10m rate=20r/s;

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name randterminal.org www.randterminal.org;
    return 301 https://randterminal.org$request_uri;
}
server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name www.randterminal.org;
    ssl_certificate     /etc/letsencrypt/live/randterminal.org/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/randterminal.org/privkey.pem;
    return 301 https://randterminal.org$request_uri;
}
server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name randterminal.org;
    ssl_certificate     /etc/letsencrypt/live/randterminal.org/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/randterminal.org/privkey.pem;
    ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
    add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000" always;
    client_max_body_size 4m;

    # Server-sent events: no buffering, long reads.
    location ~ ^/api/v1/jobs/[0-9a-f]+/events$ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Connection "";
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_cache off;
        proxy_read_timeout 900s;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr;
    }
    location /api/ {
        limit_req zone=tapi burst=40 nodelay;
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
        proxy_read_timeout 120s;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto https;
    }
    location / {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3002;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto https;
    }
}
```

If Cloudflare proxies the domain, add `set_real_ip_from` for Cloudflare's ranges and `real_ip_header CF-Connecting-IP;` as randbridge.org's config does.

- [ ] **Step 3: deploy.sh**

`deploy/deploy.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build and (re)start randterminal.org on the droplet. Usage: deploy/deploy.sh <droplet-ip>
set -euo pipefail
IP="${1:?usage: deploy/deploy.sh <droplet-ip>}"
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
SSH="ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new terminal@$IP"
SRC=/opt/terminal/src
REV=$(git -C "$HERE/circuits" rev-parse --short=7 HEAD)
rsync -az --delete --exclude target --exclude node_modules --exclude .next --exclude data --exclude 'web/e2e/fixtures' \
  "$HERE/" "terminal@$IP:$SRC/"

$SSH REV="$REV" bash -se <<'REMOTE'
set -euo pipefail
source "$HOME/.cargo/env"
cd /opt/terminal/src
docker image inspect "terminal-sandbox:$REV" >/dev/null 2>&1 || sandbox/build.sh
(cd api && cargo build --release -p terminal-api)
mkdir -p /opt/terminal/bin && install -m 0755 api/target/release/terminal-api /opt/terminal/bin/terminal-api.new
rsync -a --delete samples/ /opt/terminal/samples/
cd web && npx --yes npm@11 ci && npm run build
rm -rf /opt/terminal/web.new && mkdir -p /opt/terminal/web.new
cp -r .next/standalone/. /opt/terminal/web.new/ && cp -r .next/static /opt/terminal/web.new/.next/static
[ -d public ] && cp -r public /opt/terminal/web.new/public || true
mv /opt/terminal/bin/terminal-api.new /opt/terminal/bin/terminal-api
rm -rf /opt/terminal/web.old && { [ -d /opt/terminal/web ] && mv /opt/terminal/web /opt/terminal/web.old || true; }
mv /opt/terminal/web.new /opt/terminal/web
grep -q "TERMINAL_SANDBOX_IMAGE=terminal-sandbox:$REV" /etc/terminal/api.env || echo "WARNING: /etc/terminal/api.env names a different sandbox image than terminal-sandbox:$REV"
sudo systemctl restart terminal-api terminal-web
sleep 3
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/v1/health && echo
curl -fsS -o /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:3002/ && echo "web: up"
REMOTE
```

`deploy/README.md`: one page: provision with `cloud-init.yaml`; copy `api.env.example` to `/etc/terminal/api.env` (600, owner terminal); install the two units and `nginx.conf` (`/etc/nginx/sites-enabled/randterminal.org`); point `randterminal.org` and `www` A records at the droplet; `certbot --nginx -d randterminal.org -d www.randterminal.org`; then `deploy/deploy.sh <ip>`. The first deploy builds the sandbox image (30–40 minutes on a 4 vCPU droplet). Size: 4 vCPU / 8 GB / 160 GB minimum (two concurrent 4 GB sandboxes; SPL Token's emulation peaks around 1 GB; the data cap is 20 GB).

- [ ] **Step 4: Check the files**

Run: `bash -n deploy/deploy.sh && nginx -t -c /dev/null 2>/dev/null; docker run --rm -v "$PWD/deploy/nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf:ro" nginx:stable nginx -t 2>&1 | tail -2`
Expected: `bash -n` silent; nginx reports only the missing certificate files (they exist only on the box), no syntax errors.

- [ ] **Step 5: Deploy (after the user says go)**

Ask the user for: the droplet (existing IP, or permission to create one in their DigitalOcean account), and the DNS change for randterminal.org (check first whether `$CLOUDFLARE_API_DNS_TOKEN_BITWYRE` can see a `randterminal.org` zone; if not, the token is zone-scoped and the user must add the zone to it). With both: provision, configure, `certbot`, `deploy/deploy.sh <ip>`, then `curl -fsS https://randterminal.org/api/v1/health` and run the live e2e suite with `baseURL` pointed at the site.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add -A && git commit -m "deploy: droplet provisioning, nginx with SSE, systemd units, deploy script"
```

### Task 27: CI and coverage

**Files:**
- Create: `.github/workflows/{ci.yml,coverage.yml}`
- Modify: `README.md` (badges)

The circuits submodule is a private repository (`dendisuhubdy/zkp-circuits`): CI needs a read token for it as the secret `CIRCUITS_TOKEN` (a fine-grained PAT with read access to that one repository). Ask the user to create it; until it exists the workflows fail at checkout, and say so in the commit message.

- [ ] **Step 1: ci.yml**

```yaml
# Build and test the api (Rust) and the web app (Next.js); the hermetic e2e suite; the sandbox
# image on demand. Every `uses:` is pinned by commit sha with its tag in the trailing comment.
name: ci

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      sandbox:
        description: build the sandbox image and run the gated tests
        type: boolean
        default: false

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  api:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    defaults: { run: { working-directory: api } }
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@11d5960a326750d5838078e36cf38b85af677262 # v4.4.0
        with: { submodules: true, token: "${{ secrets.CIRCUITS_TOKEN }}" }
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@89b12181fb390509a0842a86cc55eeb8eb928c1d # stable (branch head, 2026-10-05)
      - name: components for the pinned toolchain
        run: rustup component add rustfmt clippy
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@6323deb102c322ba6fcbdcafc7e3dddab59af2b6 # v2.9.2
        with: { workspaces: api }
      - run: cargo fmt --all -- --check
      - run: cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings
      - run: cargo test --all

  web:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    defaults: { run: { working-directory: web } }
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@11d5960a326750d5838078e36cf38b85af677262 # v4.4.0
      - uses: actions/setup-node@49933ea5288caeca8642d1e84afbd3f7d6820020 # v4.4.0
        with: { node-version: '22', cache: npm, cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json }
      - run: npx --yes npm@11 ci
      - run: npm run typecheck
      - run: npm test
      - run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - run: npm run e2e

  sandbox:
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.sandbox
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 120
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@11d5960a326750d5838078e36cf38b85af677262 # v4.4.0
        with: { submodules: true, token: "${{ secrets.CIRCUITS_TOKEN }}" }
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@89b12181fb390509a0842a86cc55eeb8eb928c1d # stable (branch head, 2026-10-05)
      - name: build the image (its own hc checks run inside)
        run: echo "TAG=$(sandbox/build.sh | tail -1)" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
      - name: gated sandbox tests
        working-directory: api
        run: TERMINAL_SANDBOX=$TAG cargo test -p terminal-sandbox --test image -- --test-threads=1
```

Look up the current pinned sha of `actions/setup-node` v4 in another randprotocol workflow (`grep -rn setup-node ../*/.github/workflows`) and use that line verbatim; the sha above must be checked, not trusted.

- [ ] **Step 2: coverage.yml**

Copy `randbridge.org/.github/workflows/coverage.yml` and change: no sibling checkouts; `submodules: true` with `token: ${{ secrets.CIRCUITS_TOKEN }}`; `working-directory: api`; `workspaces: api`; `files: api/lcov.info`; the coverage command `cargo llvm-cov --workspace --lcov --output-path lcov.info`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Badges**

Add to `README.md` under the title:
```markdown
[![ci](https://github.com/randprotocol/randterminal.org/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/randprotocol/randterminal.org/actions/workflows/ci.yml)
[![coverage](https://codecov.io/gh/randprotocol/randterminal.org/graph/badge.svg)](https://codecov.io/gh/randprotocol/randterminal.org)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Lint the workflows and commit**

Run: `python3 -c "import yaml,sys;[yaml.safe_load(open(f)) for f in sys.argv[1:]]" .github/workflows/*.yml && echo ok`
Expected: `ok`.

```bash
git add -A && git commit -m "ci: api and web tests, hermetic e2e, on-demand sandbox build, coverage"
```

### Task 28: Publish the repo and mount it in the monorepo

Pushing is outward-facing: **ask the user before Step 1**.

- [ ] **Step 1: Point origin at the renamed repository and push**

```bash
cd terminal
git remote set-url origin git@github.com:randprotocol/randterminal.org.git
git push -u origin main
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Mount it as a submodule in the monorepo**

```bash
cd /Users/dendisuhubdy/Github/randprotocol
git submodule add -b main git@github.com:randprotocol/randterminal.org.git terminal
git add .gitmodules terminal
git commit -m "terminal: add randterminal.org (Rand Terminal IDE) as a submodule"
```

`git submodule add` adopts the existing checkout because its origin matches. Push the monorepo commit only when the user asks.

- [ ] **Step 3: Record it**

Update the project memory note `terminal_ide_project.md` with the merged commit, the deploy state, and the open follow-ups (proving button, Anchor projects, multi-version solc, rootless Docker).


## Addendum: final review fixes (2026-10-08)

Behaviour that changed after the tasks above (details in the spec):

- `GET /runs/:rid/next_src?from=N&dir=1|-1` replaces the debugger's client-side 64-cycle scan;
  GETs under `/runs/` have their own bucket (`runs_get_rpm`, 6 000/min) and nginx zone.
- Bounds: newest 8 runs per job; 503 for new jobs and runs while the data dir is over its cap;
  3 unfinished jobs per client (429 `too_many_jobs`), 32 in all (503).
- The sandbox tag is `sandbox/tag.sh`'s `terminal-sandbox:<circuits rev>-<hash of sandbox/>`;
  `TERMINAL_SANDBOX_IMAGE` must be set to it in production.
- The entrypoint runs under `umask 000` and `chmod -R a+rwX`es its mount on exit; the api skips
  entries it does not own and the sweeper tolerates what it cannot delete.
- A checker-rejected image builds and shows `fits: false`; a docker CLI that cannot reach the
  daemon is `internal`, "the build sandbox is unavailable"; `/health` adds `sandbox`.
- Cycle records are 40 B with `mem_old`; `mem_val` is the word a store wrote.
- Extractor rejections (400/413/415/422) are `{error, message}` JSON.
